---
title: "第 00 期 · 开源与经济学"
lecture_number: 0
topics: "开源在数字社会中的概况 / 开源的历史发展 / 开源的经济效果"
slide_count: 52
draft: false
editable: true
slides:
  - num: 1

    bullets:

      - "开源经济学12讲座     第零期 「开源之道」·适兕"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page {
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        background: var(--prussian);
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        color: var(--cream);
        box-sizing: border-box;
      }
      
      /* Subtle radial vignette */
      .slide-page::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: -20%;
        left: -10%;
        width: 80%;
        height: 80%;
        background: radial-gradient(ellipse, rgba(212,168,83,0.06) 0%, transparent 70%);
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .slide-page::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        bottom: -20%;
        right: -10%;
        width: 70%;
        height: 70%;
        background: radial-gradient(ellipse, rgba(212,168,83,0.04) 0%, transparent 70%);
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .cover-frame {
        position: absolute;
        top: 60px;
        left: 60px;
        right: 60px;
        bottom: 60px;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.3);
      }
      
      .cover-frame-inner {
        position: absolute;
        top: 72px;
        left: 72px;
        right: 72px;
        bottom: 72px;
        border: 1px solid rgba(245,240,232,0.1);
      }
      
      .slide-number {
        position: absolute;
        top: 40px;
        right: 50px;
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        color: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.7;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .slide-number-left {
        position: absolute;
        top: 40px;
        left: 50px;
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        color: var(--cream);
        opacity: 0.5;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .main-content {
        text-align: center;
        z-index: 2;
        position: relative;
      }
      
      .title-cn {
        font-size: 96px;
        font-weight: 300;

        margin: 0;

      }
      
      .title-divider {
        width: 60px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin: 40px auto;
        position: relative;
      }
      
      .title-divider::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: -3px;
        left: 25px;
        width: 7px;
        height: 7px;
        background: var(--amber);
        transform: rotate(45deg);
      }
      
      .subtitle-cn {
        font-size: 28px;
        font-weight: 300;
        letter-spacing: 12px;

        margin: 0;

      }
      
      .bottom-meta {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 45px;
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        text-align: center;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .meta-line {
        font-size: 15px;
        letter-spacing: 6px;
        color: var(--cream);
        opacity: 0.55;
        margin: 6px 0;
        font-weight: 300;
      }
      
      .meta-course {
        font-size: 17px;
        letter-spacing: 4px;
        color: var(--cream);
        opacity: 0.75;
      }
      
      .corner-deco {
        position: absolute;
        width: 40px;
        height: 40px;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .corner-deco.tl {
        top: 28px;
        left: 28px;
        border-top: 1px solid var(--amber);
        border-left: 1px solid var(--amber);
      }
      
      .corner-deco.tr {
        top: 28px;
        right: 28px;

        border-right: 1px solid var(--amber);
      }
      
      .corner-deco.bl {
        bottom: 28px;

        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--amber);
        border-left: 1px solid var(--amber);
      }
      
      .corner-deco.br {
        bottom: 28px;

        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--amber);
        border-right: 1px solid var(--amber);
      }
      
      /* Geometric accent bars */
      .geo-bar-left {

    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --prussian-deep: #0F2545;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
      
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        background: var(--prussian-deep);
        color: var(--cream);
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        padding: 56px 72px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
      }
      
      /* Top header bar evoking flight info board */
      .board-header {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: baseline;
        padding-bottom: 18px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid var(--copper);
        margin-bottom: 8px;
      }
      
      .board-header .title-left {
        font-size: 28px;
        font-weight: 400;
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        color: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .board-header .title-right {
        font-size: 12px;
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      .board-subtitle {
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 1.5px;
        padding-bottom: 22px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.4);
        margin-bottom: 20px;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
      }
      
      /* Column headers like flight board */
      .col-headers {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 80px 1fr 200px 100px;
        padding: 8px 0;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.3);
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      /* Agenda rows */
      .agenda-list {
        list-style: none;
        padding: 0;
        margin: 0;
      }
      
      .agenda-row {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 80px 1fr 200px 100px;
        align-items: center;
        padding: 14px 0;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.12);
        transition: background 0.3s;
      }
      
      .agenda-row:hover {
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.05);
      }
      
      .agenda-row .row-num {
        font-size: 18px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 400;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
      }
      
      .agenda-row .row-topic {
        font-size: 19px;
        color: var(--cream);
        line-height: 1.4;
      }
      
      .agenda-row .row-topic .topic-en {
        display: block;
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        margin-top: 3px;
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      .agenda-row .row-tag {
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 1.5px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      .agenda-row .row-status {
        font-size: 12px;
        letter-spacing: 1.5px;
        text-align: right;
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      .status-boarded {
        color: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .status-confirmed {
        color: var(--cream);
        opacity: 0.7;
      }
      
      /* Footer line */
      .board-footer {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 36px;
        left: 72px;
        right: 72px;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
        padding-top: 14px;
        border-top: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.3);
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      .footer-metaphor {
        font-style: italic;
        text-transform: none;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        font-size: 12px;
      }
      
      /* Decorative top-left mark */
      .corner-mark {
        position: absolute;
        top: 24px;
        left: 72px;
        width: 32px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .corner-mark::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: -1px;
        left: 0;
        width: 1px;
        height: 20px;
        background: var(--amber);
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="corner-mark"></div>
      
      <div class="board-header">
        <span class="title-left">课 程 议 程</span>
        <span class="title-right">Flight Board · Session 02 / 52</span>
      </div>
      
      <div class="board-subtitle">
        <span>Open Source in the Digital Society</span>
        <span>— 等待中的出行与确认 —</span>
      </div>
      
      <div class="col-headers">
        <span>№</span>
        <span>Destination / 主题</span>
        <span>Track</span>
        <span style="text-align:right;">Status</span>
      </div>
      
      <ul class="agenda-list">
        <li class="agenda-row">
          <span class="row-num">01</span>
          <span class="row-topic">议程<span class="topic-en">The Agenda — Overview of the Journey</span></span>
          <span class="row-tag">Orientation</span>
          <span class="row-status status-boarded">ON BOARD</span>

        <li class="agenda-row">
          <span class="row-num">02</span>
          <span class="row-topic">为什么要做这件事？<span class="topic-en">Why This Matters — The Driving Question</span></span>
          <span class="row-tag">Motivation</span>
          <span class="row-status status-confirmed">CONFIRMED</span>
        </li>
        <li class="agenda-row">
          <span class="row-num">03</span>
          <span class="row-topic">开源在数字社会中的概况<span class="topic-en">Open Source in the Digital Society — A Panorama</span></span>
          <span class="row-tag">Landscape</span>
          <span class="row-status status-confirmed">CONFIRMED</span>
        </li>
        <li class="agenda-row">
          <span class="row-num">04</span>
          <span class="row-topic">开源的历史（嵌入视角）<span class="topic-en">A History of Open Source — Embedded Perspective</span></span>
          <span class="row-tag">History</span>
          <span class="row-status status-confirmed">CONFIRMED</span>
        </li>
        <li class="agenda-row">
          <span class="row-num">05</span>
          <span class="row-topic">产业视角下的开源<span class="topic-en">Open Source from the Industry Lens</span></span>
          <span class="row-tag">Industry</span>
          <span class="row-status status-confirmed">CONFIRMED</span>
        </li>
        <li class="agenda-row">
          <span class="row-num">06</span>
          <span class="row-topic">12讲座的思考路径与目标<span class="topic-en">A Roadmap for 12 Lectures — Path &amp; Goals</span></span>
          <span class="row-tag">Roadmap</span>
          <span class="row-status status-confirmed">CONFIRMED</span>
        </li>
      </ul>
      
      <div class="board-footer">
        <span class="footer-metaphor">"如旅客在机场等待，我们在此确认航向"</span>
        <span>06 Stops · 1 Semester</span>
      </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 3
    visual: "课堂上高举的手——知识传递中的参与和勇气"
    bullets:
      - "开源是什么？为什么需要一套经济学的语言来理解它？"
      - "12讲将回答：开源如何在制度、组织与文化的层层嵌套中，"
      - "  形成一种替代性的协作生产秩序？"
      - "统一分析框架：Williamson 的 L1/L2/L3/L4 四层制度层级"
      - "  ——L1 社会嵌入层、L2 制度环境层、L3 治理机制层、L4 资源配置层"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        color: var(--cream);
        background: var(--prussian);
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        padding: 60px 80px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      
      /* Raised hands motif — vertical bars of varying heights as subtle background */
      .hands-motif {
        position: absolute;
        left: 0;
        bottom: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 180px;
        display: flex;
        align-items: flex-end;
        justify-content: space-between;
        padding: 0 80px;
        opacity: 0.08;
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      .hands-motif .hand {
        width: 2px;
        background: var(--amber);
        border-radius: 1px 1px 0 0;
      }
      
      .slide-title {
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 0.25em;
        color: var(--amber);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 18px;
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      
      .slide-heading {
        font-size: 30px;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--cream);
        line-height: 1.35;
        margin-bottom: 36px;
        max-width: 1000px;
      }
      
      .slide-heading em {
        font-style: italic;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      /* Divider */
      .divider {
        width: 60px;
        height: 2px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin-bottom: 28px;
      }
      
      /* Framework section */
      .framework {
        display: flex;
        gap: 18px;
        margin-bottom: 30px;
      }
      
      .layer {
        flex: 1;
        position: relative;
        padding: 18px 16px 18px 22px;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.25);
        background: rgba(27, 59, 107, 0.5);
        border-radius: 2px;
        transition: all 0.3s;
      }
      
      .layer::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        left: 0;
        top: 0;
        bottom: 0;
        width: 3px;
        background: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .layer .layer-num {
        font-size: 22px;
        font-weight: 700;
        color: var(--amber);
        letter-spacing: 0.05em;
        margin-bottom: 6px;
        display: block;
      }
      
      .layer .layer-name {
        font-size: 14px;
        color: var(--cream);
        font-weight: 600;
        margin-bottom: 4px;
        line-height: 1.4;
      }
      
      .layer .layer-en {
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 0.08em;
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      /* Framework label */
      .framework-label {
        font-size: 12px;
        letter-spacing: 0.18em;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 10px;
      }
      
      /* Descenders — visual connection between layers (like arms reaching up) */
      .descenders {
        display: flex;
        gap: 18px;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
      }
      .descenders .d {
        flex: 1;
        height: 12px;
        position: relative;
      }
      .descenders .d::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        left: 50%;
        top: 0;
        width: 1px;
        height: 12px;
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
      }
      
      /* Bottom note */
      .bottom-note {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 14px;
        margin-top: 8px;
      }
      
      .bottom-note .dot {
        width: 6px;
        height: 6px;
        background: var(--amber);
        border-radius: 50%;
        flex-shrink: 0;
      }
      
      .bottom-note .text {
        font-size: 14px;
        color: var(--copper);
        font-style: italic;
        letter-spacing: 0.02em;
      }
      
      /* Page number */
      .page-num {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 24px;
        right: 80px;
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 0.15em;
      }
      
      /* Corner mark */
      .corner-mark {
        position: absolute;
        top: 30px;
        right: 80px;
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 0.2em;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="corner-mark">III / LII</div>
      
      <div class="hands-motif">
        <div class="hand" style="height:60px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:95px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:40px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:120px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:75px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:150px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:55px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:110px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:30px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:140px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:70px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:160px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:45px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:100px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:80px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:130px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:50px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:90px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:35px;"></div>
        <div class="hand" style="height:115px;"></div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="slide-title">Introduction · 引论</div>
      
      <div class="slide-heading">
        开源是什么？<em>为什么需要一套经济学的语言</em>来理解它？<br>
        <span style="font-size:20px;font-weight:400;color:var(--copper);">12 讲将回答：开源如何在制度、组织与文化的层层嵌套中，形成一种替代性的协作生产秩序？</span>
      </div>
      
      <div class="divider"></div>
      
      <div class="framework-label">Unified Analytical Framework · 统一分析框架 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Williamson L1–L4</div>
      
      <div class="descenders">
        <div class="d"></div><div class="d"></div><div class="d"></div><div class="d"></div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="framework">
        <div class="layer">
          <span class="layer-num">L1</span>
          <div class="layer-name">社会嵌入层</div>
          <div class="layer-en">Social Embedding</div>
        </div>
        <div class="layer">
          <span class="layer-num">L2</span>
          <div class="layer-name">制度环境层</div>
          <div class="layer-en">Institutional Environment</div>
        </div>
        <div class="layer">
          <span class="layer-num">L3</span>
          <div class="layer-name">治理机制层</div>
          <div class="layer-en">Governance Mechanism</div>
        </div>
        <div class="layer">
          <span class="layer-num">L4</span>
          <div class="layer-name">资源配置层</div>
          <div class="layer-en">Resource Allocation</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="bottom-note">
        <span class="dot"></span>
        <span class="text">四层层级由社会文化至个体行动，自上而下嵌套，自下而上传导</span>
      </div>
      
      <div class="page-num">3 / 52</div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 4

    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{

        --cream:#F5F0E8;
        --copper:#8B7355;
        --amber:#D4A853;
        --muted:#555555;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        padding: 48px 72px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      .slide-inner .page-tag{
        position: absolute;
        top: 28px;

        font-style: italic;
      }

        font-size: 26px;

      }
      .slide-inner .subtitle{
        font-size: 14px;
        color: var(--copper);

      }

        width: 60px;

      }
      .slide-inner .main{
        display: flex;
        gap: 48px;

      }
      .slide-inner .stack{
        width: 380px;
        flex-shrink: 0;
        position: relative;
        padding: 8px 0;
      }
      .slide-inner .layer{

        padding: 14px 20px;
        text-align: center;
        position: relative;
        transition: none;
      }
      .slide-inner .layer .lv-num{
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--amber);
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        font-style: italic;
        display: block;
        margin-bottom: 4px;
      }
      .slide-inner .layer .lv-name{
        font-size: 16px;
        color: var(--cream);
        font-weight: 600;
        display: block;
        margin-bottom: 3px;
      }
      .slide-inner .layer .lv-time{
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 1px;
      }
      .slide-inner .layer-l4{
        width: 180px;
        border-color: var(--amber);
        background: rgba(212,168,83,0.08);
      }
      .slide-inner .layer-l3{
        width: 240px;
        border-color: var(--copper);
        background: rgba(139,115,85,0.10);
      }
      .slide-inner .layer-l2{
        width: 300px;
        border-color: var(--copper);
        background: rgba(139,115,85,0.14);
      }
      .slide-inner .layer-l1{
        width: 360px;
        border-color: var(--amber);
        background: rgba(212,168,83,0.12);
      }
      .slide-inner .depth-label{
        text-align: center;
        font-size: 10px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        margin-top: 4px;
        font-style: italic;
      }
      .slide-inner .divider-v{
        width: 1px;
        align-self: stretch;
        background: linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent, var(--copper) 30%, var(--copper) 70%, transparent);
        flex-shrink: 0;
      }
      .slide-inner .descriptions{
        flex: 1;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 14px;
        padding-top: 6px;
      }
      .slide-inner .desc-item{
        display: flex;
        gap: 16px;
        align-items: flex-start;
        padding: 12px 0;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(139,115,85,0.25);
      }
      .slide-inner .desc-item:last-child{
        border-bottom: none;
      }
      .slide-inner .desc-item .tag{
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 600;
        letter-spacing: 2px;

      }
      .slide-inner .desc-item .content{
        flex: 1;
      }
      .slide-inner .desc-item .content .title-line{
        font-size: 14px;
        color: var(--cream);

        margin-bottom: 4px;
      }
      .slide-inner .desc-item .content .detail{

      .slide-inner .desc-item .content .lectures{

      .slide-inner .conclusion{

        color: var(--cream);

        line-height: 1.5;
        padding-left: 14px;
        border-left: 2px solid var(--amber);
      }
      .slide-inner .conclusion .c-item strong{
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: normal;
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="page-tag">Lecture Map · 04/52</div>
      
      <h1>制度层级结构</h1>
      <div class="subtitle">Institutional Hierarchy — Rules, Environment &amp; Embedded Norms</div>
      <div class="accent-line"></div>
      
      <div class="main">
      
        <div class="stack">
          <div class="layer layer-l4">
            <span class="lv-num">L4</span>
            <span class="lv-name">日常实践</span>
            <span class="lv-time">日 / 周</span>
          </div>
          <div class="layer layer-l3">
            <span class="lv-num">L3</span>
            <span class="lv-name">治理机制</span>
            <span class="lv-time">1 – 10 年</span>
          </div>
          <div class="layer layer-l2">
            <span class="lv-num">L2</span>
            <span class="lv-name">制度环境</span>
            <span class="lv-time">10 – 100 年</span>
          </div>
          <div class="layer layer-l1">
            <span class="lv-num">L1</span>
            <span class="lv-name">社会嵌入</span>
            <span class="lv-time">100 – 1000 年</span>
          </div>
          <div class="depth-label">深层 ↕ 表层</div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="divider-v"></div>
      
        <div class="descriptions">
      
          <div class="desc-item">
            <span class="tag">L3</span>
            <div class="content">
              <div class="title-line">治理机制层 — 契约与组织形态</div>
              <div class="detail">市场 / 混合 / 层级：治理结构的选择与演化</div>
              <div class="lectures">第 4 讲（劳动力市场）· 第 5 讲（交易成本）</div>
            </div>
          </div>
      
          <div class="desc-item">
            <span class="tag">L2</span>
            <div class="content">
              <div class="title-line">制度环境层 — 法律与产权框架</div>
              <div class="detail">法律体系 / 产权界定 / 官僚体制的长期塑造</div>
              <div class="lectures">第 2 讲（知识财产法）· 第 9 讲（排他权）</div>
            </div>
          </div>
      
          <div class="desc-item">
            <span class="tag">L1</span>
            <div class="content">
              <div class="title-line">社会嵌入层 — 文化与信念根基</div>
              <div class="detail">文化传统 / 集体信念 / 社会习俗的深层嵌入</div>
              <div class="lectures">第 7 讲（文化）</div>
            </div>
          </div>
      
          <div class="desc-item">
            <span class="tag">—</span>
            <div class="content">
              <div class="title-line">框架说明</div>
              <div class="detail">每一讲都在这四个层级中的某一层定位其核心问题</div>
            </div>
          </div>
      
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="conclusion">
        <div class="c-item">
          <strong>制度不是中性的</strong>——它塑造了开源的形态与边界。
        </div>
      </div>
      
      </div>
      
  - num: 5
    visual: "暗蓝背景上的白色节点网络——制度层级的数字化连接"
    bullets:
      - "L3 治理机制层（市场/混合/层级，1-10年）：第4讲（劳动力市场）·第5讲（交易成本）·第6讲（组织与治理）"
      - "L4 资源配置层（价格/数量/激励，连续）：第10讲（排他权）·第11讲（劳动报酬）"
      - "L2 制度环境层（许可证、基金会、行为准则）：第2、10讲——IP法与许可演化"
      - "L2 制度环境层（法律/产权/官僚体制，10-100年）：第8讲（政治经济学）·第9讲（排他权）"
      - "L1 社会嵌入层（文化/信念/习俗，100-1000年）：第7讲——文化"
      - "L3 治理机制层：第3讲——商业模式（许可费替代机制）"
      - "L2 制度环境层：第9讲——信息商品的产权界定"
      - "L0 认识论基础（贯穿全部）：第0、1讲——开源作为研究对象"
      - "跨层分析：第11讲——劳动报酬与分配正义"
      - "制度不是分层，而是嵌套——每一讲都同时触及多个层级"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        color: var(--cream);
        background: var(--prussian);
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        padding: 48px 64px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      
      /* Network background decoration */
      .slide-page::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0;
        background-image:
          radial-gradient(circle at 15% 25%, rgba(245,240,232,0.03) 0, transparent 40%),
          radial-gradient(circle at 85% 75%, rgba(212,168,83,0.04) 0, transparent 40%),
          radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(245,240,232,0.02) 0, transparent 60%);
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      /* Grid dot pattern */
      .slide-page::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0;
        background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(245,240,232,0.06) 1px, transparent 1px);
        background-size: 28px 28px;
        background-position: 14px 14px;
        pointer-events: none;
        opacity: 0.5;
      }
      
      .slide-title {
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        color: var(--amber);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .slide-heading {
        font-size: 26px;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--cream);
        margin-bottom: 6px;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .slide-heading em {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: normal;
      }
      
      .slide-sub {
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--copper);
        margin-bottom: 28px;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .layer-network {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 110px 1fr;
        row-gap: 0;
        column-gap: 18px;
      }
      
      .layer-row {
        display: contents;
      }
      
      .layer-label {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 8px;
        padding: 10px 0;
      }
      
      .layer-badge {
        width: 36px;
        height: 36px;
        border: 1.5px solid var(--amber);
        border-radius: 50%;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        font-size: 12px;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--amber);
        background: rgba(212,168,83,0.08);
        flex-shrink: 0;
        position: relative;
      }
      
      .layer-badge::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 50%;
        left: 50%;
        transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
        width: 44px;
        height: 44px;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.2);
        border-radius: 50%;
      }
      
      .layer-info {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 1px;
      }
      
      .layer-name {
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 1.5px;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      .layer-timescale {
        font-size: 9.5px;
        color: var(--muted);
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      .layer-content {
        border-left: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.25);
        padding: 8px 0 8px 18px;
        position: relative;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 4px;
      }
      
      .layer-content::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        left: -4px;
        top: 14px;
        width: 7px;
        height: 7px;
        background: var(--amber);
        border-radius: 50%;
      }
      
      .layer-desc {
        font-size: 12.5px;
        color: var(--cream);
        line-height: 1.4;
      }
      
      .layer-desc strong {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      
      .lecture-tags {
        display: flex;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 5px;
      }
      
      .lecture-tag {
        font-size: 10px;
        padding: 2px 7px;
        border: 1px solid rgba(245,240,232,0.15);
        border-radius: 3px;
        color: var(--cream);
        background: rgba(245,240,232,0.04);
        letter-spacing: 0.3px;
      }
      
      .layer-footnote {
        font-size: 9.5px;
        color: var(--copper);
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      .insight-bar {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        margin-top: 22px;
        padding: 14px 24px;
        border: 1px solid var(--amber);
        background: rgba(212,168,83,0.06);
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        gap: 14px;
      }
      
      .insight-bar .icon {
        width: 8px;
        height: 8px;
        background: var(--amber);
        border-radius: 50%;
        flex-shrink: 0;
      }
      
      .insight-bar .text {
        font-size: 14px;
        color: var(--cream);
        font-style: italic;
        text-align: center;
      }
      
      .insight-bar .text strong {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: normal;
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="slide-title">Course Map</div>
      <div class="slide-heading">制度层级 <em>×</em> 课程讲次</div>
      <div class="slide-sub">Institutional Layers & Lecture Mapping — 从 L0 认识论到 L4 资源配置</div>
      
      <div class="layer-network">
      
        <div class="layer-row">
          <div class="layer-label">
            <div class="layer-badge">L4</div>
            <div class="layer-info">
              <span class="layer-name">资源配置</span>

            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="layer-content">
            <div class="layer-desc"><strong>价格 / 数量 / 激励</strong></div>
            <div class="lecture-tags">
              <span class="lecture-tag">第10讲 · 排他权</span>
              <span class="lecture-tag">第11讲 · 劳动报酬</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="layer-row">
          <div class="layer-label">
            <div class="layer-badge">L3</div>
            <div class="layer-info">
              <span class="layer-name">治理机制</span>
              <span class="layer-timescale">1–10 年</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="layer-content">
            <div class="layer-desc"><strong>市场 / 混合 / 层级</strong></div>
            <div class="lecture-tags">
              <span class="lecture-tag">第3讲 · 商业模式（许可费替代）</span>
              <span class="lecture-tag">第4讲 · 劳动力市场</span>
              <span class="lecture-tag">第5讲 · 交易成本</span>
              <span class="lecture-tag">第6讲 · 组织与治理</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="layer-row">
          <div class="layer-label">
            <div class="layer-badge">L2</div>
            <div class="layer-info">
              <span class="layer-name">制度环境</span>
              <span class="layer-timescale">10–100 年</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="layer-content">
            <div class="layer-desc"><strong>法律 / 产权 / 许可证 / 基金会 / 行为准则</strong></div>
            <div class="lecture-tags">
              <span class="lecture-tag">第2、10讲 · IP法与许可演化</span>
              <span class="lecture-tag">第8讲 · 政治经济学</span>
              <span class="lecture-tag">第9讲 · 排他权 / 产权界定</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="layer-row">
          <div class="layer-label">
            <div class="layer-badge">L1</div>
            <div class="layer-info">
              <span class="layer-name">社会嵌入</span>
              <span class="layer-timescale">100–1000 年</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="layer-content">
            <div class="layer-desc"><strong>文化 / 信念 / 习俗</strong></div>
            <div class="lecture-tags">
              <span class="lecture-tag">第7讲 · 文化</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="layer-row">
          <div class="layer-label">
            <div class="layer-badge">L0</div>
            <div class="layer-info">
              <span class="layer-name">认识论基础</span>
              <span class="layer-timescale">贯穿全部</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="layer-content">
            <div class="layer-desc"><strong>开源作为研究对象</strong></div>
            <div class="lecture-tags">
              <span class="lecture-tag">第0、1讲 · 方法论导入</span>
            </div>
            <div class="layer-footnote">跨层分析：第11讲 — 劳动报酬与分配正义</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      
      </div>
      
      <div class="insight-bar">
        <span class="icon"></span>
        <span class="text">制度不是分层，而是<strong>嵌套</strong>——每一讲都同时触及多个层级</span>
        <span class="icon"></span>
      </div>
      
      </div>
      
  - num: 6
    visual: "圆形桌边围坐的人——讲座规则下的平等交流"
    bullets:
      - "关于讲座的规则说明"
      - "随时交流或提问"
      - "指出错误有奖励"
      - "个人的视角是有限的，协作更佳"
      - "项目开始，就没有结束"
      - "遵循「开源之道」"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        background:var(--prussian);
        color:var(--cream);
        width:1280px;height:720px;
        padding:60px 80px;
        box-sizing:border-box;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:row;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:space-between;
        gap:60px;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
      }
      
      /* 圆形桌视觉隐喻 */
      .table-metaphor{
        position:relative;
        width:380px;height:380px;
        flex-shrink:0;
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:center;
      }
      
      .table-metaphor .table-circle{
        position:absolute;
        width:280px;height:280px;
        border-radius:50%;
        border:1px solid var(--copper);
        box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(212,168,83,0.3);
      }
      
      .table-metaphor .table-inner{
        position:absolute;
        width:200px;height:200px;
        border-radius:50%;
        border:1px dashed rgba(212,168,83,0.25);
      }
      
      .table-metaphor .table-label{
        position:relative;
        font-size:13px;
        letter-spacing:3px;
        color:var(--copper);
        text-transform:uppercase;
        text-align:center;
        line-height:1.6;
        z-index:2;
      }
      
      .table-metaphor .table-label strong{
        display:block;
        font-size:18px;
        color:var(--amber);
        font-weight:400;
        letter-spacing:2px;
        margin-top:4px;
      }
      
      /* 围坐的"人"——小圆点 */
      .person{
        position:absolute;
        width:14px;height:14px;
        border-radius:50%;
        background:var(--amber);
        box-shadow:0 0 0 3px var(--prussian),0 0 0 4px var(--copper);
      }
      .p1{top:20px;left:50%;margin-left:-7px;}
      .p2{top:50px;right:60px;}
      .p3{bottom:50px;right:60px;}
      .p4{bottom:20px;left:50%;margin-left:-7px;}
      .p5{bottom:50px;left:60px;}
      .p6{top:50px;left:60px;}
      
      /* 右侧内容区 */
      .rules-section{
        flex:1;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        gap:18px;
      }
      
      .slide-num{
        font-size:13px;
        letter-spacing:4px;
        color:var(--copper);
        margin-bottom:4px;
      }
      
      .rules-title{
        font-size:36px;
        font-weight:400;
        color:var(--cream);
        letter-spacing:1px;
        margin:0 0 6px 0;
      }
      
      .rules-title::after{
        content:'';
        display:block;
        width:80px;

        background:var(--amber);

      }
      
      .rules-list{
        list-style:none;
        margin:0;padding:0;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        gap:10px;
      }
      
      .rules-list li{
        display:flex;
        align-items:baseline;
        gap:16px;
        font-size:17px;
        color:var(--cream);
        line-height:1.5;
      }
      
      .rules-list .num{
        font-size:14px;
        color:var(--amber);
        letter-spacing:1px;
        min-width:28px;
        font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;
      }
      
      .rules-list .divider{
        width:1px;
        height:14px;
        background:var(--copper);
        flex-shrink:0;
        transform:translateY(2px);
      }
      
      .rules-list .text{
        flex:1;
      }
      
      .rules-list .text em{
        font-style:italic;
        color:var(--amber);
      }
      
      .footer-note{
        margin-top:8px;
        font-size:12px;
        letter-spacing:2px;
        color:var(--muted);
        text-align:right;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="table-metaphor">
        <div class="table-circle"></div>
        <div class="table-inner"></div>

        <span class="person p2"></span>
        <span class="person p3"></span>
        <span class="person p4"></span>
        <span class="person p5"></span>
        <span class="person p6"></span>
        <div class="table-label">
          ROUND TABLE
          <strong>平等 · 协作</strong>
        </div>

      <div class="rules-section">

        <ul class="rules-list">

          <li>
            <span class="num">03</span>

            <span class="text">指出错误 <em>有奖励</em></span>
          </li>
          <li>
            <span class="num">04</span>

  - num: 7
    visual: "黑暗空间中一束光裂开裂缝——「为什么做这个讲座？」的探索"
    bullets:
      - "为什么要做这个讲座？"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        --prussian:#1B3B6B;
        --cream:#F5F0E8;
        --copper:#8B7355;
        --amber:#D4A853;
        --muted:#555555;
        background: var(--prussian);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        color: var(--cream);
      }
      
      /* Deep darkness vignette */
      .darkness{
        position: absolute;
        inset: 0;
        background: radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 60% at 50% 50%, rgba(27,59,107,0.4) 0%, rgba(5,15,35,0.95) 70%, #050B1A 100%);
        z-index: 1;
      }
      
      /* Vertical crack — the fissure in the dark */
      .crack{
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 50%;
        transform: translateX(-50%);
        width: 2px;
        height: 100%;
        z-index: 3;
        background: linear-gradient(
          to bottom,
          transparent 0%,
          transparent 18%,
          var(--amber) 35%,
          rgba(212,168,83,0.8) 50%,
          rgba(212,168,83,0.6) 65%,
          transparent 85%,
          transparent 100%
        );
        box-shadow:
          0 0 8px rgba(212,168,83,0.6),
          0 0 20px rgba(212,168,83,0.4),
          0 0 40px rgba(212,168,83,0.2);
      }
      
      /* Beam of light spreading from the crack */
      .beam{
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 50%;
        transform: translateX(-50%);
        width: 600px;
        height: 100%;
        z-index: 2;
        background: radial-gradient(
          ellipse 80px 350px at center,
          rgba(212,168,83,0.25) 0%,
          rgba(212,168,83,0.1) 30%,
          rgba(212,168,83,0.03) 55%,
          transparent 75%
        );
        mix-blend-mode: screen;
      }
      
      /* Upper glow */
      .glow-top{
        position: absolute;
        top: 5%;
        left: 50%;
        transform: translateX(-50%);
        width: 4px;
        height: 8px;
        background: var(--amber);
        border-radius: 2px;
        box-shadow:
          0 0 15px var(--amber),
          0 0 40px rgba(212,168,83,0.7),
          0 0 80px rgba(212,168,83,0.4),
          0 0 140px rgba(212,168,83,0.2);
        z-index: 4;
      }
      
      /* Side crack branches */
      .crack-branch-left{
        position: absolute;
        top: 42%;
        left: 49.5%;
        width: 1px;
        height: 60px;
        z-index: 3;
        background: linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(212,168,83,0.6), transparent);
        transform: rotate(-12deg);
        transform-origin: top center;
        box-shadow: 0 0 4px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      .crack-branch-right{
        position: absolute;
        top: 58%;
        left: 50.5%;
        width: 1px;
        height: 50px;
        z-index: 3;
        background: linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(212,168,83,0.5), transparent);
        transform: rotate(10deg);
        transform-origin: top center;
        box-shadow: 0 0 4px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      /* Floating dust particles */
      .particle{
        position: absolute;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
        z-index: 4;
        animation: drift 6s ease-in-out infinite;
      }
      .p1{ top: 22%; left: 48%; width: 3px; height: 3px; opacity: 0.6; animation-delay: 0s; }
      .p2{ top: 35%; left: 52%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.4; animation-delay: 1.5s; }
      .p3{ top: 68%; left: 49%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.5; animation-delay: 3s; }
      .p4{ top: 75%; left: 51.5%; width: 3px; height: 3px; opacity: 0.35; animation-delay: 4s; }
      .p5{ top: 45%; left: 47%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.45; animation-delay: 2s; }
      
      @keyframes drift {
        0%,100% { transform: translate(0,0); opacity: 0.3; }
        50% { transform: translate(3px,-8px); opacity: 0.7; }
      }
      
      /* Content area */
      .content{
        position: relative;
        z-index: 5;
        text-align: center;
        padding: 0 80px;
        max-width: 900px;
      }
      
      .eyebrow{
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 0.4em;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--amber);
        margin-bottom: 36px;
        opacity: 0.85;
      }
      
      .eyebrow-line{
        display: inline-block;
        width: 50px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--amber);
        vertical-align: middle;
        margin: 0 16px;
        opacity: 0.7;
      }
      
      .question{
        font-size: 56px;
        font-weight: 300;
        line-height: 1.35;
        color: var(--cream);
        letter-spacing: 0.02em;
        text-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(212,168,83,0.15);
      }
      
      .question .accent{
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: italic;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .subtitle{
        margin-top: 40px;
        font-size: 18px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 0.15em;
        font-weight: 300;
      }
      
      .footer-line{
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 36px;
        left: 50%;
        transform: translateX(-50%);
        z-index: 5;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 16px;
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 0.3em;
        color: var(--copper);
        opacity: 0.6;
      }
      .footer-line .dot{
        width: 4px;
        height: 4px;
        background: var(--amber);
        border-radius: 50%;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="darkness"></div>
      <div class="beam"></div>
      <div class="glow-top"></div>
      <div class="crack"></div>
      <div class="crack-branch-left"></div>
      <div class="crack-branch-right"></div>
      <div class="particle p1"></div>
      <div class="particle p2"></div>
      <div class="particle p3"></div>
      <div class="particle p4"></div>
      <div class="particle p5"></div>
      
      <div class="content">
        <div class="eyebrow"><span class="eyebrow-line"></span>Interrogation<span class="eyebrow-line"></span></div>
        <h1 class="question">为什么要做<br>这个<span class="accent">讲座</span>？</h1>
        <p class="subtitle">Why this lecture exists</p>
      </div>
      
      <div class="footer-line">
        <span class="dot"></span>
        <span>CHAPTER · 07 / 52</span>
      </div>
      
      </div>
      
  - num: 8
    visual: "铁锈斑驳的旧扳手排列——开源经济学是解决问题的工具"
    bullets:
      - "凡事问个为什么"
      - "经常被问：开源怎么赚钱？"
      - "开源是如此的普及"
      - "社会价值与经济价值"
      - "“天下没有免费的午餐”错了吗？"
      - "开源还有前途吗？"
    designed: |
      <style>
        .slide-page {
          --prussian: #1B3B6B;
          --cream: #F5F0E8;
          --copper: #8B7355;
          --amber: #D4A853;
          --muted: #555555;
          background: var(--prussian);
          color: var(--cream);
          font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
          width: 1280px;
          height: 720px;
          padding: 60px 80px;
          box-sizing: border-box;
          position: relative;
          overflow: hidden;
        }
      
        /* Wrench decorations - top row */
        .wrench-row {
          position: absolute;
          top: 28px;
          left: 80px;
          right: 80px;
          display: flex;
          justify-content: space-between;
          align-items: center;
          pointer-events: none;
        }
      
        .wrench {
          position: relative;
          width: 44px;
          height: 44px;
          opacity: 0.35;
        }
      
        .wrench .head {
          position: absolute;
          top: 0;
          left: 10px;
          width: 24px;
          height: 24px;
          border: 3px solid var(--amber);
          border-radius: 50%;
          box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 6px var(--prussian);
        }
      
        .wrench .handle {
          position: absolute;
          top: 22px;
          left: 20px;
          width: 4px;
          height: 22px;
          background: var(--amber);
          border-radius: 2px;
          transform: rotate(15deg);
          transform-origin: top center;
        }
      
        .wrench.w2 .head { box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 5px var(--prussian); }
        .wrench.w3 .head { box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 7px var(--prussian); }
        .wrench.w4 .head { box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 4px var(--prussian); }
        .wrench.w5 .head { box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 6px var(--prussian); }
        .wrench.w6 .head { box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 5px var(--prussian); }
      
        .wrench.w1 .handle { transform: rotate(-10deg); }
        .wrench.w2 .handle { transform: rotate(20deg); }
        .wrench.w3 .handle { transform: rotate(-15deg); }
        .wrench.w4 .handle { transform: rotate(10deg); }
        .wrench.w5 .handle { transform: rotate(-5deg); }
        .wrench.w6 .handle { transform: rotate(18deg); }
      
        .slide-header {
          margin-top: 70px;
          display: flex;
          align-items: baseline;
          justify-content: space-between;
          border-bottom: 1px solid var(--copper);
          padding-bottom: 20px;
        }
      
        .slide-header h2 {
          font-size: 28px;
          font-weight: 400;
          color: var(--amber);
          letter-spacing: 2px;
          margin: 0;
        }
      
        .slide-header .page-num {
          font-size: 14px;
          color: var(--copper);
          letter-spacing: 3px;
        }
      
        .content {
          margin-top: 45px;
          display: grid;
          grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
          gap: 30px 60px;
        }
      
        .tool-item {
          display: flex;
          align-items: flex-start;
          gap: 20px;
          padding: 14px 0;
          border-bottom: 1px dashed rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.3);
        }
      
        .tool-num {
          font-size: 22px;
          color: var(--amber);
          font-weight: 600;
          min-width: 38px;
          line-height: 1.3;
          border-left: 3px solid var(--amber);
          padding-left: 10px;
        }
      
        .tool-text {
          font-size: 20px;
          color: var(--cream);
          line-height: 1.5;
          flex: 1;
        }
      
        .tool-text .sub {
          display: block;
          font-size: 14px;
          color: var(--copper);
          margin-top: 4px;
          letter-spacing: 1px;
        }
      
        .tool-text em {
          color: var(--amber);
          font-style: italic;
        }
      
        .footer-bar {
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 30px;
          left: 80px;
          right: 80px;
          display: flex;
          justify-content: space-between;
          align-items: center;
          font-size: 12px;
          color: var(--copper);
          letter-spacing: 2px;
        }
      
        .footer-bar .line {
          flex: 1;
          height: 1px;
          background: var(--copper);
          margin: 0 20px;
          opacity: 0.4;
        }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="wrench-row">
        <div class="wrench w1"><div class="head"></div><div class="handle"></div></div>
        <div class="wrench w2"><div class="head"></div><div class="handle"></div></div>
        <div class="wrench w3"><div class="head"></div><div class="handle"></div></div>
        <div class="wrench w4"><div class="head"></div><div class="handle"></div></div>
        <div class="wrench w5"><div class="head"></div><div class="handle"></div></div>
        <div class="wrench w6"><div class="head"></div><div class="handle"></div></div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="slide-header">
        <h2>开源经济学工具箱</h2>
        <span class="page-num">08 / 52</span>
      </div>
      
      <div class="content">
        <div class="tool-item">
          <span class="tool-num">01</span>
          <span class="tool-text">凡事问个为什么<span class="sub">WHY — 第一性原理</span></span>
        </div>
        <div class="tool-item">
          <span class="tool-num">02</span>
          <span class="tool-text">经常被问：开源怎么赚钱？<span class="sub">MONETIZATION — 商业模式</span></span>
        </div>
        <div class="tool-item">
          <span class="tool-num">03</span>
          <span class="tool-text">开源是如此的普及<span class="sub">UBIQUITY — 无处不在</span></span>
        </div>
        <div class="tool-item">
          <span class="tool-num">04</span>
          <span class="tool-text">社会价值与<em>经济价值</em><span class="sub">DUAL VALUE — 双重价值</span></span>
        </div>
        <div class="tool-item">
          <span class="tool-num">05</span>
          <span class="tool-text">"天下没有免费的午餐"错了吗？<span class="sub">FREE LUNCH — 悖论之问</span></span>
        </div>
        <div class="tool-item">
          <span class="tool-num">06</span>
          <span class="tool-text">开源还有前途吗？<span class="sub">FUTURE — 前路何在</span></span>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="footer-bar">
        <span>OPEN SOURCE ECONOMICS</span>
        <span class="line"></span>
        <span>SIX QUESTIONS — SIX TOOLS</span>
      </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 9
    visual: "众人手叠手围成一圈——团队共识与集体力量"
    bullets:
      - "经济学是研究资源配置的，研究经济学是为了使有限的资源被用于最合理、最有效的方面，以便增加社会总的财富，使社会日益富裕。研究经济学，是为了寻找一种令人满意的机制，以便合理利用资源，有效配置资源，既能增加社会财富，又能使财富或收入的分配趋于公平。"
      - "———— 厉以宁 《超越市场与超越政府》"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        padding: 80px 120px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      
      /* 同心圆装饰——隐喻众人围圈 */
      .ring-deco {
        position: absolute;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px solid var(--copper);
        opacity: 0.15;
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      .ring-1 {
        width: 700px;
        height: 700px;
        right: -180px;
        top: -200px;
        border-color: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.08;
      }
      .ring-2 {
        width: 500px;
        height: 500px;
        right: -100px;
        top: -120px;
        border-color: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.10;
      }
      .ring-3 {
        width: 320px;
        height: 320px;
        right: -20px;
        top: -40px;
        border-color: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.12;
      }
      
      /* 左侧竖线 */
      .left-bar {
        position: absolute;
        left: 60px;
        top: 100px;
        bottom: 100px;
        width: 2px;
        background: var(--copper);
        opacity: 0.5;
      }
      .left-bar::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        left: -1px;
        top: 0;
        width: 4px;
        height: 60px;
        background: var(--amber);
      }
      
      /* 引号 */
      .quote-mark {
        font-size: 120px;
        line-height: 1;
        color: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.4;
        margin-bottom: 20px;
        font-style: italic;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .quote-body {
        font-size: 24px;
        line-height: 1.9;
        color: var(--cream);
        max-width: 900px;
        text-align: justify;
        font-weight: 300;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        letter-spacing: 0.02em;
      }
      
      .quote-body .highlight {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: italic;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .attribution {
        margin-top: 40px;
        text-align: right;
        color: var(--copper);
        font-size: 18px;
        font-style: italic;
        letter-spacing: 0.03em;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .attribution::before {
        content: '──────';
        display: block;
        color: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.5;
        margin-bottom: 10px;
        letter-spacing: 0.1em;
      }
      
      /* 页码 */
      .page-num {
        position: absolute;
        right: 60px;
        bottom: 40px;
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--copper);
        opacity: 0.6;
        letter-spacing: 0.15em;
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      /* 底部细线 */
      .bottom-line {
        position: absolute;
        left: 120px;
        right: 120px;
        bottom: 70px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--copper);
        opacity: 0.2;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="ring-deco ring-1"></div>
      <div class="ring-deco ring-2"></div>
      <div class="ring-deco ring-3"></div>
      <div class="left-bar"></div>
      
      <div class="quote-mark">"</div>
      
      <p class="quote-body">
      经济学是研究资源配置的，研究经济学是为了使有限的资源被用于<span class="highlight">最合理、最有效</span>的方面，以便增加社会总的财富，使社会日益富裕。研究经济学，是为了寻找一种令人满意的机制，以便合理利用资源，有效配置资源，既能增加社会财富，又能使<span class="highlight">财富或收入的分配趋于公平</span>。
      </p>
      
      <div class="attribution">
      <cite>—— 厉以宁 《超越市场与超越政府》</cite>
      </div>
      
      <div class="bottom-line"></div>
      <div class="page-num">09 / 52</div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 10
    visual: "黑色背景中红色球体与白色同心圆——开源作为价值源头的辐射"
    bullets:

      - "文化与价值观"
      - "生活态度"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        padding: 56px 72px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
      }
      
      .slide-header {
        display: flex;
        align-items: baseline;
        justify-content: space-between;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
      }
      
      .slide-title {
        font-size: 36px;
        font-weight: 600;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        color: var(--cream);
      }
      
      .slide-num {
        font-size: 14px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
      }
      
      .slide-rule {
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--copper);
        opacity: 0.5;
        margin-bottom: 32px;
        width: 100%;
      }
      
      .slide-body {
        display: flex;
        flex: 1;
        gap: 48px;
        align-items: center;
      }
      
      /* Left: radiating concentric circles */
      .radial-container {
        position: relative;
        width: 380px;
        height: 380px;
        flex-shrink: 0;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
      }
      
      .core {
        width: 56px;
        height: 56px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: radial-gradient(circle at 35% 35%, var(--amber), #a67d35);
        box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        position: absolute;
        z-index: 5;
      }
      
      .ring {
        position: absolute;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px solid var(--cream);
        opacity: 0.18;
      }
      
      .ring-1 { width: 110px; height: 110px; }
      .ring-2 { width: 160px; height: 160px; opacity: 0.15; }
      .ring-3 { width: 210px; height: 210px; opacity: 0.12; }
      .ring-4 { width: 260px; height: 260px; opacity: 0.10; }
      .ring-5 { width: 310px; height: 310px; opacity: 0.08; }
      .ring-6 { width: 360px; height: 360px; opacity: 0.06; }
      
      .ring-fill {
        position: absolute;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px dashed var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.22;
      }
      
      .ring-fill-1 { width: 135px; height: 135px; }
      .ring-fill-2 { width: 190px; height: 190px; opacity: 0.16; }
      .ring-fill-3 { width: 245px; height: 245px; opacity: 0.12; }
      .ring-fill-4 { width: 300px; height: 300px; opacity: 0.08; }
      
      /* Right: facet list */
      .facets {
        flex: 1;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 14px;
      }
      
      .facet-intro {
        font-size: 15px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 10px;
      }
      
      .facet-item {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 18px;
        padding: 10px 0;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(139,115,85,0.2);
      }
      
      .facet-item:last-child {
        border-bottom: none;
      }
      
      .facet-num {
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', Georgia, serif;
        width: 32px;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        flex-shrink: 0;
      }
      
      .facet-text {
        font-size: 20px;
        color: var(--cream);
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      }
      
      .facet-text .accent {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      
      /* Connecting ray lines */
      .ray {
        position: absolute;
        height: 1px;
        background: linear-gradient(to right, var(--amber), transparent);
        opacity: 0.3;
        transform-origin: left center;
        z-index: 1;
      }
      
      .ray-1 { width: 120px; top: 190px; left: 240px; transform: rotate(-30deg); }
      .ray-2 { width: 130px; top: 190px; left: 240px; transform: rotate(-15deg); }
      .ray-3 { width: 140px; top: 190px; left: 240px; transform: rotate(0deg); }
      .ray-4 { width: 130px; top: 190px; left: 240px; transform: rotate(15deg); }
      .ray-5 { width: 120px; top: 190px; left: 240px; transform: rotate(30deg); }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="slide-header">
        <div class="slide-title">开源的多维面貌</div>
        <div class="slide-num">10 / 52</div>
      </div>
      <div class="slide-rule"></div>
      
      <div class="slide-body">
        <div class="radial-container">
          <div class="ring ring-6"></div>
          <div class="ring-fill ring-fill-4"></div>
          <div class="ring ring-5"></div>

          <div class="ring ring-4"></div>
          <div class="ring-fill ring-fill-2"></div>
          <div class="ring ring-3"></div>
          <div class="ring-fill ring-fill-1"></div>
          <div class="ring ring-2"></div>
          <div class="ring ring-1"></div>
          <div class="core"></div>
          <div class="ray ray-1"></div>
          <div class="ray ray-2"></div>
          <div class="ray ray-3"></div>
          <div class="ray ray-4"></div>
          <div class="ray ray-5"></div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="facets">
          <div class="facet-intro">What is Open Source — 七个维度</div>
      
          <div class="facet-item">
            <span class="facet-num">01</span>
            <span class="facet-text">一种<span class="accent">生产模式</span></span>
          </div>
          <div class="facet-item">
            <span class="facet-num">02</span>

          <div class="facet-item">
            <span class="facet-num">03</span>
            <span class="facet-text">一种<span class="accent">社会现象</span></span>
          </div>
          <div class="facet-item">
            <span class="facet-num">04</span>
            <span class="facet-text">集体行动与<span class="accent">个体激励</span></span>
          </div>
          <div class="facet-item">
            <span class="facet-num">05</span>
