  - num: 11
    visual: "蓝色微距下的蒲公英绒球——开源价值随风飘散而自由传播"
    bullets:
      - "开源经济：开源的价值"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
      
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        width: 100%;
        height: 100vh;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        padding: 0 12%;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      
      /* 背景：径向渐变模拟蓝色微距景深 */
      .slide-inner::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0;
        background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 72% 48%, rgba(27,59,107,0.4) 0%, rgba(15,32,60,1) 70%);
        z-index: 0;
      }
      
      .slide-inner > * {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      /* 蒲公英绒球 - 中心球体 */
      .puff {
        position: absolute;
        right: 10%;
        top: 50%;
        transform: translateY(-50%);
        width: 320px;
        height: 320px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.15);
        z-index: 1;
      }
      
      /* 绒球中心核 */
      .puff-core {
        position: absolute;
        right: 10%;
        top: 50%;
        transform: translate(50%,-50%);
        width: 160px;
        height: 160px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.25);
        z-index: 1;
      }
      
      .puff-core::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 50%; left: 50%;
        transform: translate(-50%,-50%);
        width: 8px;
        height: 8px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.6;
        box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      /* 种子点阵列 - 模拟蒲公英冠毛 */
      .seeds {
        position: absolute;
        right: 10%;
        top: 50%;
        transform: translate(50%,-50%);
        width: 320px;
        height: 320px;
        z-index: 1;
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .seed-dot {
        position: absolute;
        width: 4px;
        height: 4px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: rgba(245,240,232,0.5);
        top: 50%; left: 50%;
        transform-origin: 50% 50%;
      }
      
      /* 飘散的种子 - 随风飞散 */
      .drift-seed {
        position: absolute;
        width: 3px;
        height: 3px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.7;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .drift-seed.d1 { top: 22%; left: 60%; opacity: 0.8; }
      .drift-seed.d2 { top: 35%; left: 45%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.5; }
      .drift-seed.d3 { top: 15%; left: 38%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.4; }
      .drift-seed.d4 { top: 68%; left: 52%; opacity: 0.6; }
      .drift-seed.d5 { top: 55%; left: 30%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.5; }
      .drift-seed.d6 { top: 80%; left: 42%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.3; }
      .drift-seed.d7 { top: 42%; left: 18%; opacity: 0.6; }
      .drift-seed.d8 { top: 75%; left: 25%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.4; }
      .drift-seed.d9 { top: 30%; left: 8%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.3; }
      .drift-seed.d10 { top: 88%; left: 15%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.3; }
      .drift-seed.d11 { top: 10%; left: 70%; width: 2px; height: 2px; opacity: 0.4; }
      .drift-seed.d12 { top: 62%; left: 62%; opacity: 0.5; }
      
      /* 风的轨迹线 */
      .wind-line {
        position: absolute;
        height: 1px;
        background: linear-gradient(to left, rgba(212,168,83,0.3), transparent);
        z-index: 1;
      }
      
      .wind-line.w1 { top: 38%; left: 20%; width: 200px; }
      .wind-line.w2 { top: 52%; left: 12%; width: 150px; }
      .wind-line.w3 { top: 28%; left: 8%; width: 100px; }
      .wind-line.w4 { top: 72%; left: 18%; width: 180px; }
      
      /* 文字内容 */
      .section-label {
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 4px;
        color: var(--amber);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 24px;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .slide-title {
        font-size: 48px;
        font-weight: 400;
        color: var(--cream);
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        line-height: 1.3;
        margin-bottom: 20px;
        max-width: 520px;
      }
      
      .slide-title em {
        font-style: italic;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .title-underline {
        width: 80px;
        height: 2px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin-bottom: 28px;
        opacity: 0.8;
      }
      
      .slide-desc {
        font-size: 17px;
        color: rgba(245,240,232,0.7);
        line-height: 1.8;
        max-width: 460px;
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      /* 页码 */
      .page-num {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 36px;
        right: 12%;
        font-size: 12px;
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        color: rgba(212,168,83,0.5);
        z-index: 3;
      }
      
      /* 左下角标识 */
      .corner-mark {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 36px;
        left: 12%;
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        color: rgba(245,240,232,0.35);
        z-index: 3;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="puff"></div>
      <div class="puff-core"></div>
      
      <div class="seeds">
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(85px, 0px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-85px, 0px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(0px, 85px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(0px, -85px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(60px, 60px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-60px, 60px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(60px, -60px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-60px, -60px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(100px, 30px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-100px, 30px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(100px, -30px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-100px, -30px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(30px, 100px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-30px, 100px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(30px, -100px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-30px, -100px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(55px, 80px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-55px, 80px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(80px, 55px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-80px, 55px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(55px, -80px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-55px, -80px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(80px, -55px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-80px, -55px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(80px, 40px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-80px, -40px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(40px, -80px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-40px, 80px);"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(110px, 15px); opacity: 0.3;"></span>
        <span class="seed-dot" style="transform: translate(-50%,-50%) translate(-110px, -15px); opacity: 0.3;"></span>
      </div>
      
      <span class="drift-seed d1"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d2"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d3"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d4"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d5"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d6"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d7"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d8"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d9"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d10"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d11"></span>
      <span class="drift-seed d12"></span>
      
      <span class="wind-line w1"></span>
      <span class="wind-line w2"></span>
      <span class="wind-line w3"></span>
      <span class="wind-line w4"></span>
      
      <div class="section-label">OPEN SOURCE ECONOMY</div>
      <h1 class="slide-title">开源的<em>价值</em></h1>
      <div class="title-underline"></div>
      <p class="slide-desc">一粒种子随风而行，<br>自由传播，随处生根。</p>
      
      <div class="corner-mark">SOURCE · OPEN</div>
      <div class="page-num">11 / 52</div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 12
    visual: "野餐桌上的双筒望远镜——观察开源的理想与现实"
    bullets:
      - "理想中的开源世界"
      - "最优的模块胜出，保持整体最佳"
      - "人们的搭便车本能消失了"
      - "【术语定义】搭便车（Free Rider Problem）：享受公共品收益却不承担生产成本。理论出处：奥尔森《集体行动的逻辑》（1965）"
      - "劳动成果和价值能够完美公平的货币化"
      - "复用到极致，所有的资源都用于创新"
      - "人际和谐，平等交流，一片富足"
      - "赢得社会尊重～"
    designed: |
      <style>
          .slide-inner{
            font-family: 'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
            background: var(--prussian);
            color: var(--cream);
            padding: 56px 72px;
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
            height: 100vh;
            box-sizing: border-box;
          }
      
          /* 远景地平线 — 野餐场的意象 */
          .slide-inner::before{
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0;
            height: 180px;
            background: linear-gradient(to top, rgba(27,43,67,0.95) 0%, rgba(27,43,67,0.4) 60%, rgba(27,43,67,0) 100%);
            pointer-events: none;
          }
      
          .slide-inner::after{
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            left: 72px; right: 72px;
            bottom: 140px;
            height: 1px;
            background: var(--copper);
            opacity: 0.35;
          }
      
          .slide-header{
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 24px;
            margin-bottom: 28px;
          }
      
          .slide-header .slide-num{
            font-size: 13px;
            letter-spacing: 0.2em;
            color: var(--amber);
            border: 1px solid var(--copper);
            padding: 6px 14px;
            white-space: nowrap;
          }
      
          .slide-header .slide-title{
            font-size: 22px;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--cream);
            letter-spacing: 0.02em;
          }
      
          /* 双筒望远镜 — 核心视觉隐喻 */
          .binoculars{
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            gap: 20px;
            margin: 0 auto 24px auto;
            position: relative;
          }
      
          .lens{
            width: 78px;
            height: 78px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            border: 3px solid var(--amber);
            background: radial-gradient(circle at 35% 30%, rgba(212,168,83,0.25) 0%, rgba(27,43,67,0.9) 70%);
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 6px rgba(27,43,67,0.6), inset 0 0 12px rgba(212,168,83,0.15);
            position: relative;
          }
      
          .lens::after{
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 12px; left: 12px;
            width: 14px; height: 10px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: rgba(245,240,232,0.12);
          }
      
          .bridge{
            width: 20px;
            height: 6px;
            background: var(--copper);
            border-radius: 2px;
          }
      
          .eyepiece-label{
            font-size: 10px;
            letter-spacing: 0.25em;
            color: var(--copper);
            text-align: center;
            margin-top: 4px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
          }
      
          .binocular-caption{
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 12px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 0.15em;
            font-style: italic;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
          }
      
          .main-title{
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 30px;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: var(--amber);
            margin-bottom: 8px;
            letter-spacing: 0.04em;
          }
      
          .main-subtitle{
            text-align: center;
            font-size: 14px;
            color: var(--copper);
            font-style: italic;
            margin-bottom: 28px;
          }
      
          .content-area{
            max-width: 780px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
          }
      
          .ideal-list{
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
            gap: 10px 32px;
          }
      
          .ideal-list li{
            display: flex;
            align-items: flex-start;
            gap: 12px;
            font-size: 14px;
            line-height: 1.55;
            color: var(--cream);
            padding: 6px 0;
          }
      
          .ideal-list li .num{
            flex-shrink: 0;
            width: 22px;
            height: 22px;
            border: 1px solid var(--amber);
            border-radius: 50%;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            font-size: 10px;
            color: var(--amber);
            font-weight: 600;
            margin-top: 1px;
          }
      
          .ideal-list li .text{
            flex: 1;
          }
      
          .term-box{
            grid-column: 1 / -1;
            margin-top: 14px;
            border: 1px solid var(--copper);
            border-left: 3px solid var(--amber);
            padding: 14px 18px;
            background: rgba(139,115,85,0.08);
            font-size: 13px;
            line-height: 1.65;
            color: var(--cream);
          }
      
          .term-box .term-label{
            display: inline-block;
            font-size: 10px;
            letter-spacing: 0.2em;
            color: var(--amber);
            border: 1px solid var(--amber);
            padding: 2px 8px;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
          }
      
          .term-box .term-name{
            font-weight: 700;
            color: var(--amber);
            font-size: 14px;
          }
      
          .term-box .term-source{
            display: block;
            margin-top: 6px;
            font-size: 11px;
            color: var(--copper);
            font-style: italic;
          }
      
          .footer-bar{
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 36px;
            left: 72px;
            right: 72px;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            font-size: 11px;
            letter-spacing: 0.15em;
            color: var(--copper);
          }
      
          .footer-bar .dot{
            width: 4px; height: 4px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: var(--amber);
            display: inline-block;
            margin: 0 6px;
            vertical-align: middle;
          }
        </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
        
      
        <div class="slide-header">
          <span class="slide-num">12 / 52</span>
          <span class="slide-title">开源理想主义</span>
        </div>
      
        <div class="binoculars">
          <div>
            <div class="lens"></div>
            <div class="eyepiece-label">IDEAL</div>
          </div>
          <div class="bridge"></div>
          <div>
            <div class="lens"></div>
            <div class="eyepiece-label">REALITY</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="binocular-caption">— 透过双筒望远镜观察 —</div>
      
        <div class="content-area">
          <div class="main-title">理想中的开源世界</div>
          <div class="main-subtitle">A Utopia of Open Source</div>
      
          <ul class="ideal-list">
            <li>
              <span class="num">01</span>
              <span class="text">最优的模块胜出，保持整体最佳</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="num">02</span>
              <span class="text">人们的搭便车本能消失了</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="num">03</span>
              <span class="text">劳动成果和价值能够完美公平的货币化</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="num">04</span>
              <span class="text">复用到极致，所有资源都用于创新</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="num">05</span>
              <span class="text">人际和谐，平等交流，一片富足</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="num">06</span>
              <span class="text">赢得社会尊重～</span>
            </li>
      
            <li class="term-box">
              <div>
                <span class="term-label">术语定义</span>
                <span class="term-name">搭便车（Free Rider Problem）</span>
                ：享受公共品收益却不承担生产成本。
                <span class="term-source">理论出处：奥尔森《集体行动的逻辑》（Mancur Olson, The Logic of Collective Action, 1965）</span>
              </div>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      
        <div class="footer-bar">
          <span>OPEN SOURCE</span>
          <span><span class="dot"></span>IDEAL · REALITY<span class="dot"></span></span>
          <span>CH. 12</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 13
    visual: "蓝色木桌上放着一本打开的旧书——制度约束的隐喻"
    bullets:
      - "但是，没有完美世界"
      - "世界上有很多力量"
      - "正如人本身的内心活动"
      - "必然受到各类制约"
      - "制度：法律、道德、文化、习俗的共同作用"
      - "开源和其它类是共存的"
    designed: |
      <style>
          .slide-page {
            width: 1280px;
            height: 720px;
            background: var(--prussian);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: center;
            align-items: center;
            font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
            color: var(--cream);
            padding: 60px 80px;
            box-sizing: border-box;
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
          }
      
          .slide-page::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            right: 0;
            height: 1px;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, var(--copper) 40%, var(--amber) 50%, var(--copper) 60%, transparent);
          }
      
          .slide-header {
            text-align: center;
            margin-bottom: 36px;
          }
      
          .slide-header .eyebrow {
            font-size: 13px;
            letter-spacing: 4px;
            color: var(--amber);
            text-transform: uppercase;
            margin-bottom: 14px;
          }
      
          .slide-header h2 {
            font-size: 32px;
            font-weight: 600;
            color: var(--cream);
            letter-spacing: 2px;
          }
      
          .slide-header h2::after {
            content: '';
            display: block;
            width: 60px;
            height: 2px;
            background: var(--amber);
            margin: 14px auto 0;
          }
      
          .book-container {
            width: 1040px;
            height: 360px;
            display: flex;
            position: relative;
            align-items: stretch;
            justify-content: center;
          }
      
          /* Book spine */
          .book-spine {
            width: 4px;
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--copper), var(--amber), var(--copper));
            box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
            flex-shrink: 0;
            border-radius: 2px;
          }
      
          /* Book pages */
          .book-page {
            flex: 1;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #F5F0E8 0%, #EDE5D3 100%);
            padding: 28px 34px;
            color: var(--prussian);
            font-size: 17px;
            line-height: 1.7;
            position: relative;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: center;
          }
      
          .book-page.left {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #F5F0E8 0%, #E8E0CE 100%);
            border-radius: 4px 0 0 4px;
            box-shadow: inset -4px 0 12px rgba(27, 59, 107, 0.06);
          }
      
          .book-page.right {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #EDE5D3 0%, #F5F0E8 100%);
            border-radius: 0 4px 4px 0;
            box-shadow: inset 4px 0 12px rgba(27, 59, 107, 0.06);
          }
      
          .page-corner-tl, .page-corner-tr, .page-corner-bl, .page-corner-br {
            position: absolute;
            width: 16px;
            height: 16px;
            border-color: var(--amber);
          }
      
          .page-corner-tl { top: 10px; left: 10px; border-top: 1.5px solid var(--amber); border-left: 1.5px solid var(--amber); }
          .page-corner-tr { top: 10px; right: 10px; border-top: 1.5px solid var(--amber); border-right: 1.5px solid var(--amber); }
          .page-corner-bl { bottom: 10px; left: 10px; border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--amber); border-left: 1.5px solid var(--amber); }
          .page-corner-br { bottom: 10px; right: 10px; border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--amber); border-right: 1.5px solid var(--amber); }
      
          .page-label {
            font-size: 11px;
            letter-spacing: 3px;
            color: var(--copper);
            text-transform: uppercase;
            margin-bottom: 14px;
            text-align: center;
          }
      
          .page-label .chapter {
            display: block;
            font-size: 14px;
            color: var(--prussian);
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            font-style: italic;
            margin-top: 4px;
          }
      
          .page-list {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
          }
      
          .page-list li {
            display: flex;
            align-items: flex-start;
            margin-bottom: 14px;
            padding-left: 0;
          }
      
          .page-list li:last-child {
            margin-bottom: 0;
          }
      
          .page-list li .num {
            font-size: 14px;
            color: var(--amber);
            font-weight: 600;
            min-width: 28px;
            flex-shrink: 0;
            border-right: 1.5px solid var(--copper);
            padding-right: 10px;
            margin-right: 12px;
            font-family: Georgia, serif;
          }
      
          .page-list li .text {
            font-size: 15.5px;
            color: var(--prussian);
            line-height: 1.55;
            flex: 1;
          }
      
          .page-list li .text strong {
            color: var(--prussian);
            font-weight: 700;
          }
      
          .page-list li .text em {
            color: var(--copper);
            font-style: italic;
          }
      
          /* Table surface shadow beneath book */
          .book-shadow {
            width: 1100px;
            height: 8px;
            margin-top: 10px;
            background: radial-gradient(ellipse, rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.4) 0%, transparent 70%);
          }
      
          .slide-footer {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 28px;
            left: 0;
            right: 0;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            padding: 0 60px;
            font-size: 11px;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            color: var(--muted);
            color: var(--copper);
          }
        </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
        
      
        <div class="slide-header">
          <div class="eyebrow">No. 13 &nbsp;·&nbsp; Institutional Constraints</div>
          <h2>没有完美世界的制约</h2>
        </div>
      
        <div class="book-container">
          <div class="book-page left">
            <div class="page-corner-tl"></div>
            <div class="page-corner-bl"></div>
            <div class="page-label">左页<span class="chapter">Chapter of Reality</span></div>
            <ul class="page-list">
              <li>
                <span class="num">§ 1</span>
                <span class="text">但是，没有<strong>完美世界</strong></span>
              </li>
              <li>
                <span class="num">§ 2</span>
                <span class="text">世界上有很多力量</span>
              </li>
              <li>
                <span class="num">§ 3</span>
                <span class="text">正如人本身的<em>内心活动</em></span>
              </li>
              <li>
                <span class="num">§ 4</span>
                <span class="text">必然受到<em>各类制约</em></span>
              </li>
            </ul>
          </div>
      
          <div class="book-spine"></div>
      
          <div class="book-page right">
            <div class="page-corner-tr"></div>
            <div class="page-corner-br"></div>
            <div class="page-label">右页<span class="chapter">Chapter of Coexistence</span></div>
            <ul class="page-list">
              <li>
                <span class="num">§ 5</span>
                <span class="text"><strong>制度</strong>：法律、道德、文化、习俗的共同作用</span>
              </li>
              <li>
                <span class="num">§ 6</span>
                <span class="text">开源和其它类是<em>共存</em>的</span>
              </li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="book-shadow"></div>
      
        <div class="slide-footer">
          <span>OPEN SOURCE ETHICS</span>
          <span>13 / 52</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 14
    visual: "蓝色二进制代码流——拒绝数学迷信的理性反思"
    bullets:
      - "为什么不相信模型经济学？"
      - "经济学是一门社会人文学科"
      - "所有的理论都假设了条件：理性人、道德人、完美的人"
      - "建模很多时候是自洽逻辑的倒推"
      - "数学的迷信，确实很唬人"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        padding: 60px 90px;
      }
      
      /* Binary code stream background effect */
      .slide-inner::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        bottom: 0;
        background:
          repeating-linear-gradient(
            0deg,
            transparent 0px,
            transparent 24px,
            rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.015) 24px,
            rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.015) 25px
          );
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .binary-stream {
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        right: -40px;
        bottom: 0;
        width: 320px;
        font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
        font-size: 11px;
        line-height: 18px;
        color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.08);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        writing-mode: vertical-rl;
        text-orientation: mixed;
        word-break: break-all;
        overflow: hidden;
        user-select: none;
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .slide-inner::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        right: 0;
        width: 320px;
        bottom: 0;
        background: linear-gradient(
          to left,
          transparent 0%,
          rgba(27, 59, 107, 0.5) 50%,
          var(--prussian) 100%
        );
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .slide-header {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 20px;
        margin-bottom: 38px;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .header-accent {
        width: 4px;
        height: 52px;
        background: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .header-text {
        font-size: 14px;
        letter-spacing: 4px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .slide-title {
        font-size: 38px;
        font-weight: 400;
        line-height: 1.3;
        margin-bottom: 12px;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        color: var(--cream);
      }
      
      .slide-title em {
        font-style: italic;
        color: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .title-underline {
        width: 80px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--copper);
        margin-bottom: 32px;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .bullets {
        list-style: none;
        padding: 0;
        margin: 0;
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 14px;
      }
      
      .bullets li {
        display: flex;
        align-items: baseline;
        gap: 20px;
        font-size: 17px;
        line-height: 1.5;
        padding-left: 0;
      }
      
      .bullets li .num {
        font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--amber);
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        min-width: 38px;
        flex-shrink: 0;
        opacity: 0.8;
      }
      
      .bullets li .text {
        color: var(--cream);
        font-size: 17px;
      }
      
      .bullets li .text strong {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      
      .bullets li .text .strike {
        text-decoration: line-through;
        text-decoration-color: rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.6);
        color: rgba(245, 240, 232, 0.5);
      }
      
      .footer {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 30px;
        left: 90px;
        right: 90px;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        color: var(--copper);
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .footer-line {
        flex: 1;
        height: 1px;
        background: rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.3);
        margin: 0 20px;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="binary-stream">01101001 01010000 01110100 01101000 01101001 01101101 01110011 01101001 01100101 01110011 01101111 01100011 01101001 01100001 01101100 01101001 01110101 01110011 01010100 01101000 01100101 01101110 00100000 01000010 01101001 01101110 01100001 01110010 01110101 00100000 01100001 01101110 01100100 00100000 01001110 01110101 01101101 01100001 01110010 01110100 01101001 01100011 01110011 01010100 01101000 01100101 01101110 00100000 01010100 01101000 01100101 01101110 00100000 01000010 01101001 01101110 01100001 01110010 01110101 00100000 01100001 01101110 01100100 00100000 01001110 01110101 01101101 01100001 01110010 01110100 01101001 01100011 01110011</div>
      
      <div class="slide-header">
        <div class="header-accent"></div>
        <div class="header-text">Skepticism · Critical Reflection</div>
      </div>
      
      <h1 class="slide-title">为什么不相信<em>模型</em>经济学？</h1>
      <div class="title-underline"></div>
      
      <ul class="bullets">
        <li>
          <span class="num">01</span>
          <span class="text">经济学是一门<strong>社会人文学科</strong>，而非自然科学</span>
        </li>
        <li>
          <span class="num">02</span>
          <span class="text">所有的理论都假设了条件：<span class="strike">理性人</span>、<span class="strike">道德人</span>、<span class="strike">完美的人</span></span>
        </li>
        <li>
          <span class="num">03</span>
          <span class="text">建模很多时候是<strong>自洽逻辑的倒推</strong>，而非现实的描摹</span>
        </li>
        <li>
          <span class="num">04</span>
          <span class="text">数学的迷信，<strong>确实很唬人</strong></span>
        </li>
      </ul>
      
      <div class="footer">
        <span>CHAPTER · Skepticism</span>
        <span class="footer-line"></span>
        <span>14 / 52</span>
      </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 15
    visual: "金融数据图表与硬币并置——开源无法提供答案，只提供探讨"
    bullets:
      - "无法提供答案"
      - "所有的都是探讨"
      - "以及前人的研究和挖掘"
      - "继续探索：结合本土文化和环境"
      - "能够为我们的日常工作提供一个思考路径"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        width:1280px;height:720px;
        background:var(--prussian);
        color:var(--cream);
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:1.05fr 1fr;
        padding:72px 80px 64px 80px;
        box-sizing:border-box;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
      }
      
      /* Subtle grid texture like a financial dashboard */
      .slide-inner::before{
        content:"";
        position:absolute;top:0;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;
        background-image:
          linear-gradient(rgba(245,240,232,0.03) 1px, transparent 1px),
          linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(245,240,232,0.03) 1px, transparent 1px);
        background-size:48px 48px;
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      
      /* LEFT COLUMN — Financial chart metaphor */
      .chart-col{
        position:relative;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        justify-content:flex-end;
        padding-bottom:24px;
      }
      
      .chart-frame{
        position:relative;
        height:340px;
        border-left:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.35);
        border-bottom:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.35);
        display:flex;
        align-items:flex-end;
        justify-content:space-around;
        padding:0 18px 0 18px;
        margin-bottom:14px;
      }
      
      .bar{
        width:22px;
        background:linear-gradient(180deg, var(--amber) 0%, rgba(212,168,83,0.25) 100%);
        border-radius:2px 2px 0 0;
        opacity:0.7;
      }
      .bar.b1{height:62%}
      .bar.b2{height:44%}
      .bar.b3{height:80%}
      .bar.b4{height:55%}
      .bar.b5{height:92%}
      .bar.b6{height:38%}
      .bar.b7{height:70%}
      .bar.b8{height:48%}
      
      /* Faint area line overlay */
      .chart-line{
        position:absolute;
        left:18px;right:18px;top:20px;bottom:0;
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      .chart-line .seg{
        position:absolute;
        height:1px;
        background:rgba(212,168,83,0.18);
      }
      .seg.s1{left:0;width:30%;top:42%}
      .seg.s2{left:28%;width:22%;top:32%}
      .seg.s3{left:48%;width:28%;top:24%}
      .seg.s4{left:74%;width:26%;top:38%}
      
      /* Coin cluster above chart */
      .coin-zone{
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:flex-start;
        gap:14px;
        margin-bottom:28px;
        padding-left:8px;
      }
      .coin{
        border-radius:50%;
        background:radial-gradient(circle at 35% 30%, var(--amber) 0%, #8B7355 70%, #5C4A32 100%);
        box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.35), inset 0 0 0 3px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        position:relative;
      }
      .coin.c1{width:46px;height:46px}
      .coin.c2{width:38px;height:38px;margin-left:-14px}
      .coin.c3{width:52px;height:52px;margin-left:-12px}
      .coin.c4{width:34px;height:34px;margin-left:-10px}
      .coin::after{
        content:"￥";
        position:absolute;
        top:50%;left:50%;
        transform:translate(-50%,-50%);
        color:rgba(27,59,107,0.6);
        font-size:18px;
        font-weight:700;
      }
      .coin.c3::after{font-size:22px}
      
      /* RIGHT COLUMN — Text */
      .text-col{
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        justify-content:center;
        padding-left:16px;
        border-left:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.2);
      }
      
      .tag-row{
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        gap:12px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      }
      .tag-num{
        font-size:13px;
        color:var(--amber);
        letter-spacing:0.25em;
        font-weight:600;
      }
      .tag-line{
        flex:1;
        height:1px;
        background:rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      h1.slide-title{
        font-size:38px;
        font-weight:600;
        line-height:1.2;
        margin:0 0 12px 0;
        color:var(--cream);
        letter-spacing:0.01em;
      }
      h1.slide-title em{
        font-style:italic;
        color:var(--amber);
        font-weight:400;
      }
      
      .subtitle{
        font-size:15px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:0.15em;
        margin-bottom:30px;
        font-style:italic;
      }
      
      .bullet-list{
        list-style:none;
        margin:0;
        padding:0;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        gap:14px;
      }
      .bullet-list li{
        display:flex;
        align-items:baseline;
        gap:14px;
        font-size:17px;
        line-height:1.5;
        color:var(--cream);
      }
      .bullet-list li .idx{
        flex-shrink:0;
        width:28px;
        font-size:12px;
        color:var(--amber);
        font-weight:700;
        letter-spacing:0.1em;
        padding-top:2px;
      }
      .bullet-list li .txt{
        flex:1;
      }
      .bullet-list li .txt strong{
        color:var(--amber);
        font-weight:600;
      }
      .bullet-list li .txt em{
        font-style:italic;
        color:var(--copper);
      }
      
      /* Footer */
      .footer{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:28px;
        left:80px;
        right:80px;
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        font-size:11px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:0.2em;
      }
      .footer .dot{
        display:inline-block;
        width:5px;height:5px;
        background:var(--amber);
        border-radius:50%;
        margin:0 8px;
        vertical-align:middle;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <h1 class="slide-title">无法提供<em>答案</em>，<br>只提供<em>探讨</em></h1>
      <div class="subtitle">OPEN SOURCE · NO ANSWERS, ONLY INQUIRY</div>
      
      <ul class="bullet-list">
        <li><span class="idx">01</span><span class="txt"><strong>无法提供答案</strong>，只能呈现数据与观察</span></li>
        <li><span class="idx">02</span><span class="txt">所有的都是<strong>探讨</strong>，而非定论</span></li>
        <li><span class="idx">03</span><span class="txt">以及<em>前人的研究和挖掘</em>，作为我们立足的土壤</span></li>
        <li><span class="idx">04</span><span class="txt">继续探索：结合<em>本土文化和环境</em>，而非照搬</span></li>
        <li><span class="idx">05</span><span class="txt">能够为我们的<strong>日常工作</strong>提供一个<em>思考路径</em></span></li>
      </ul>
      </div>
      
  - num: 16
    visual: "三次分配金字塔——自由市场、政府配置、慈善道德的层次"
    bullets:
      - "开源属于第几次分配？"
      - "第一次分配：自由市场"
      - "第二次分配：政府配置"
      - "第三次分配：慈善与道德"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        --prussian:#1B3B6B;
        --cream:#F5F0E8;
        --copper:#8B7355;
        --amber:#D4A853;
        --muted:#555555;
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        background:var(--prussian);
        color:var(--cream);
        width:100%;
        height:100%;
        padding:56px 80px;
        box-sizing:border-box;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
      }
      .s16-wrap{
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:1fr 1.1fr;
        gap:60px;
        height:100%;
        align-items:center;
      }
      .s16-left{
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        justify-content:center;
        gap:28px;
      }
      .s16-label{
        font-size:13px;
        letter-spacing:4px;
        color:var(--amber);
        text-transform:uppercase;
      }
      .s16-question{
        font-size:42px;
        line-height:1.3;
        color:var(--cream);
        font-weight:400;
        border-left:3px solid var(--amber);
        padding-left:24px;
      }
      .s16-question em{
        font-style:italic;
        color:var(--amber);
      }
      .s16-sub{
        font-size:15px;
        color:var(--copper);
        line-height:1.7;
        padding-left:27px;
        max-width:360px;
      }
      
      /* Pyramid */
      .s16-right{
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:center;
        gap:0;
      }
      .s16-tier{
        position:relative;
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:flex-end;
        padding:20px 40px;
        margin-bottom:6px;
        transition:none;
      }
      .s16-tier-1{
        width:60%;
        height:78px;
        background:linear-gradient(135deg,#3D6B3A,#2D5330);
        border:1px solid #4A7E45;
        clip-path:polygon(8% 0,92% 0,100% 100%,0 100%);
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:center;
        gap:14px;
      }
      .s16-tier-2{
        width:80%;
        height:82px;
        background:linear-gradient(135deg,#5A6B4A,#3D4F35);
        border:1px solid #6B7E55;
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:space-between;
        padding:0 48px 0 72px;
        gap:14px;
      }
      .s16-tier-3{
        width:100%;
        height:90px;
        background:linear-gradient(135deg,#D4A853,#8B7355);
        border:1px solid #E0BC6A;
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:space-between;
        padding:0 48px 0 72px;
        color:var(--prussian);
      }
      .s16-tier-num{
        font-size:14px;
        letter-spacing:3px;
        opacity:0.85;
        white-space:nowrap;
      }
      .s16-tier-text{
        font-size:17px;
        font-weight:600;
        letter-spacing:1px;
        white-space:nowrap;
      }
      .s16-tier-3 .s16-tier-text{
        color:var(--prussian);
        font-size:18px;
      }
      
      .s16-tier-label{
        font-size:12px;
        letter-spacing:2px;
        color:var(--copper);
        margin-top:6px;
        text-align:right;
        width:78%;
      }
      .s16-tier-3-label{
        width:98%;
      }
      .s16-tier-1-label{
        width:58%;
      }
      
      .s16-pyramid-frame{
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        align-items:center;
      }
      
      .s16-arrow{
        font-size:11px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:3px;
        margin-top:14px;
        text-align:center;
      }
      
      /* Page number */
      .s16-pagenum{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:28px;
        right:48px;
        font-size:12px;
        letter-spacing:3px;
        color:var(--copper);
      }
      .s16-pagenum .cur{
        color:var(--amber);
        font-size:14px;
      }
      
      /* Decorative line */
      .s16-deco{
        position:absolute;
        top:0;
        left:80px;
        right:80px;
        height:1px;
        background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,var(--amber) 30%,var(--amber) 70%,transparent);
        opacity:0.4;
      }
      .s16-deco-bot{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:60px;
        left:80px;
        right:80px;
        height:1px;
        background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,var(--copper) 30%,var(--copper) 70%,transparent);
        opacity:0.3;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="s16-deco"></div>
      
      <div class="s16-wrap">
      
        <div class="s16-left">
          <div class="s16-label">第 04 讲 · 分配理论</div>
          <div class="s16-question">开源属于<em>第几次分配</em>？</div>
          <div class="s16-sub">经济学中的三次分配框架，试图回答价值如何从生产端流向消费端。开源软件的无偿分享，究竟落在哪一层？</div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="s16-right">
          <div class="s16-pyramid-frame">
      
            <div class="s16-tier s16-tier-1">
              <span class="s16-tier-num">I</span>
              <span class="s16-tier-text">自由市场</span>
            </div>
            <div class="s16-tier-label s16-tier-1-label">第一次分配</div>
      
            <div class="s16-tier s16-tier-2">
              <span class="s16-tier-num">II</span>
              <span class="s16-tier-text">政府配置</span>
            </div>
            <div class="s16-tier-label">第二次分配</div>
      
            <div class="s16-tier s16-tier-3">
              <span class="s16-tier-num">III</span>
              <span class="s16-tier-text">慈善与道德</span>
            </div>
            <div class="s16-tier-label s16-tier-3-label">第三次分配</div>
      
          </div>
          <div class="s16-arrow">↑ 道德约束力递减 &nbsp;/&nbsp; 强制性递增 ↓</div>
        </div>
      
      </div>
      
      <div class="s16-deco-bot"></div>
      
      <div class="s16-pagenum"><span class="cur">16</span> &nbsp;/&nbsp; 52</div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 17
    visual: "圆桌前围坐的学者——共识的达成需要理性对话"

    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page designed { display:block; }
      .slide-inner{
        width:100%;
        height:100vh;
        background:var(--prussian);
        color:var(--cream);
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
        padding:60px 80px;
        box-sizing:border-box;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .page-num{
        position:absolute;
        top:36px;
        right:60px;
        font-size:13px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:2px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header{
        display:flex;
        align-items:baseline;
        justify-content:space-between;
        border-bottom:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.25);
        padding-bottom:20px;
        margin-bottom:30px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header h1{
        font-size:32px;
        font-weight:400;
        color:var(--amber);
        letter-spacing:3px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header .subtitle{
        font-size:14px;
        color:var(--copper);
        font-style:italic;
        letter-spacing:1px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .main{
        flex:1;
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:1fr 1.4fr;
        gap:60px;
        align-items:center;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .table-visual{
        position:relative;
        width:360px;
        height:360px;
        justify-self:center;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .ring{
        position:absolute;
        border-radius:50%;
        border:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.3);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .ring.outer{
        width:360px;height:360px;top:0;left:0;
        border-style:dashed;
        border-color:rgba(139,115,85,0.4);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .ring.mid{
        width:260px;height:260px;top:50px;left:50px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .ring.inner{
        width:140px;height:140px;top:110px;left:110px;
        border:2px solid var(--amber);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .center-glyph{
        position:absolute;
        top:140px;left:140px;
        width:80px;height:80px;
        border-radius:50%;
        background:radial-gradient(circle at 35% 35%, rgba(212,168,83,0.25), transparent 70%);
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:center;
        font-size:28px;
        color:var(--amber);
        font-style:italic;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .seats{
        position:absolute;
        width:14px;height:14px;
        border-radius:50%;
        background:var(--amber);
        box-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(212,168,83,0.5);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .seats.s1{top:0;left:50%;margin-left:-7px;}
      .slide-inner .seats.s2{top:30%;right:-7px;}
      .slide-inner .seats.s3{bottom:0;left:50%;margin-left:-7px;}
      .slide-inner .seats.s4{top:30%;left:-7px;}
      .slide-inner .seats.s5{top:50%;right:25%;margin-top:-7px;background:var(--copper);}
      
      .slide-inner .points{
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        gap:18px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .point{
        display:flex;
        align-items:baseline;
        gap:20px;
        padding:14px 20px;
        border-left:2px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        background:rgba(245,240,232,0.03);
        transition:none;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .point .num{
        font-size:13px;
        color:var(--amber);
        letter-spacing:2px;
        font-weight:600;
        min-width:32px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .point .text{
        font-size:18px;
        color:var(--cream);
        line-height:1.5;
        flex:1;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .point .text em{
        color:var(--amber);
        font-style:italic;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .footer{
        margin-top:20px;
        display:flex;

        justify-content:space-between;
        border-top:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.15);
        padding-top:18px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .footer .motto{
        font-style:italic;
        font-size:14px;

        letter-spacing:1px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .footer .tag{

        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:3px;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="page-num">17 / 52</div>
      
      <div class="header">

        <div class="table-visual">

          <div class="ring mid"></div>
          <div class="ring inner"></div>
          <div class="center-glyph">∑</div>

          <div class="seats s2"></div>
          <div class="seats s3"></div>
          <div class="seats s4"></div>
          <div class="seats s5"></div>

        <div class="points">
          <div class="point">
            <span class="num">I</span>
            <span class="text">共识是<em>协作集体行动</em>的基础</span>
          </div>
          <div class="point">
            <span class="num">II</span>
            <span class="text">开源有很多被<em>误解、曲解</em>的地方</span>
          </div>
          <div class="point">
            <span class="num">III</span>
            <span class="text">开源是复杂的，看到的只是<em>表象</em></span>
          </div>
          <div class="point">

          <div class="point">

          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="footer">
        <span class="motto">"真理愈辩愈明" — 围坐方桌，各自陈述，终归共识</span>
        <span class="tag">PART V · CONSENSUS</span>
      </div>
      
      </div>
      
  - num: 18
    visual: "黑色背景下白色羽毛飘动——新制度经济学的轻盈切入"
    bullets:
      - "新制度经济学"
      - "开源软件的经济和社会价值"
      - "制度是一切的基础：集体行动、财产分配、商业交易"
      - "从历史中寻找规律"
      - "尽量少假设，往不假设靠近"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
      
        background: #0A0E14;
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        padding: 70px 100px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
      }
      
      /* Feather-like shapes drifting in background */
      .feather {
        position: absolute;
        border-radius: 50% 50% 50% 0;
        opacity: 0.06;
        filter: blur(0.3px);
      }
      
      .feather.f1 {
        top: 40px;
        left: 60px;
        width: 80px;
        height: 160px;
        background: var(--cream);
        transform: rotate(-25deg);
      }
      
      .feather.f2 {
        top: 80px;
        right: 120px;
        width: 55px;
        height: 110px;
        background: var(--cream);
        transform: rotate(15deg);
        opacity: 0.045;
      }
      
      .feather.f3 {
        bottom: 60px;
        left: 200px;
        width: 40px;
        height: 90px;
        background: var(--cream);
        transform: rotate(30deg);
        opacity: 0.05;
      }
      
      .feather.f4 {
        bottom: 30px;
        right: 80px;
        width: 70px;
        height: 140px;
        background: var(--cream);
        transform: rotate(-10deg);
        opacity: 0.04;
      }
      
      .feather.f5 {
        top: 300px;
        left: -10px;
        width: 35px;
        height: 70px;
        background: var(--amber);
        transform: rotate(45deg);
        opacity: 0.05;
      }
      
      .feather.f6 {
        top: 200px;
        right: 40px;
        width: 45px;
        height: 95px;
        background: var(--cream);
        transform: rotate(-40deg);
        opacity: 0.035;
      }
      
      /* Soft glow center */
      .center-glow {
        position: absolute;
        top: 50%;
        left: 50%;
        transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
        width: 600px;
        height: 600px;
        background: radial-gradient(ellipse at center, rgba(212,168,83,0.04) 0%, transparent 70%);
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .content {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        max-width: 900px;
        margin-left: auto;
      }
      
      .eyebrow {
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 4px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        color: var(--amber);
        margin-bottom: 30px;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .main-title {
        font-size: 44px;
        font-weight: 400;
        line-height: 1.25;
        color: var(--cream);
        margin: 0 0 28px 0;
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      }
      
      .subtitle {
        font-size: 18px;
        color: var(--copper);
        font-style: italic;
        margin: 0 0 50px 0;
        letter-spacing: 0.3px;
        line-height: 1.5;
      }
      
      .divider {
        width: 80px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin: 0 0 40px 0;
        opacity: 0.7;
      }
      
      .bullet-list {
        list-style: none;
        padding: 0;
        margin: 0;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li {
        font-size: 17px;
        color: var(--cream);
        line-height: 1.6;
        padding: 12px 0 12px 30px;
        position: relative;
        letter-spacing: 0.2px;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        left: 0;
        top: 22px;
        width: 14px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.8;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li:first-child {
        padding-top: 4px;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li:first-child::before {
        top: 14px;
      }
      
      .slide-number {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 30px;
        right: 50px;
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        opacity: 0.6;
        z-index: 3;
      }
      
      .footer-accent {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 30px;
        left: 50px;
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        opacity: 0.4;
        z-index: 3;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">

      <div class="content">
        <div class="eyebrow">Part II &middot; 新制度经济学</div>
        <h1 class="main-title">从历史中寻找规律</h1>
        <p class="subtitle">开源软件的经济和社会价值 &mdash; 一种轻盈的切入</p>

  - num: 19
    visual: "俯瞰欧洲的夜间卫星影像——开源文明点亮人类聚居地"
    bullets:
      - "开源在现代数字社会中的概况"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --prussian-deep: #0F2440;
        --prussian-mid: #244E7F;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --amber-glow: #E8C76A;
        --muted: #555555;
      
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        color: var(--cream);
        background: var(--prussian-deep);
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        padding: 0;
      }
      
      /* Starfield / satellite atmosphere */
      .star-layer {
        position: absolute;
        inset: 0;
        opacity: 0.35;
        background-image:
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 8% 12%, rgba(245,240,232,0.5) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 22% 28%, rgba(245,240,232,0.3) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 35% 8%, rgba(245,240,232,0.4) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 48% 22%, rgba(245,240,232,0.3) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 60% 15%, rgba(245,240,232,0.5) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 72% 30%, rgba(245,240,232,0.3) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 85% 18%, rgba(245,240,232,0.4) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 92% 35%, rgba(245,240,232,0.3) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 15% 45%, rgba(245,240,232,0.3) 0, transparent 100%),
          radial-gradient(1px 1px at 40% 52%, rgba(245,240,232,0.4) 0, transparent 100%);
      }
      
      /* Landmass silhouette suggestion via gradients */
      .landmass {
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        opacity: 0.12;
        background:
          radial-gradient(ellipse 380px 260px at 62% 58%, var(--prussian-mid) 0%, transparent 70%),
          radial-gradient(ellipse 200px 140px at 50% 42%, var(--prussian-mid) 0%, transparent 70%),
          radial-gradient(ellipse 120px 80px at 44% 55%, var(--prussian-mid) 0%, transparent 70%),
          radial-gradient(ellipse 90px 60px at 58% 48%, var(--prussian-mid) 0%, transparent 70%);
      }
      
      /* Settlement grid — main dot pattern representing European light clusters */
      .settlement-grid {
        position: absolute;
        top: 60px;
        right: 60px;
        width: 620px;
        height: 540px;
      }
      
      .dot {
        position: absolute;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
        box-shadow: 0 0 6px var(--amber-glow), 0 0 14px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        animation: pulse 3s ease-in-out infinite;
      }
      
      @keyframes pulse {
        0%, 100% { opacity: 0.7; }
        50% { opacity: 1; }
      }
      
      /* Cluster: Northern Europe */
      .c1 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 40px; left: 180px; animation-delay: 0s; }
      .c2 { width: 3px; height: 3px; top: 55px; left: 210px; animation-delay: 0.3s; }
      .c3 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 70px; left: 240px; animation-delay: 0.6s; }
      .c4 { width: 3px; height: 3px; top: 85px; left: 195px; animation-delay: 0.9s; }
      .c5 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 60px; left: 270px; animation-delay: 1.2s; }
      
      /* Cluster: Benelux / Germany */
      .c6 { width: 6px; height: 6px; top: 140px; left: 165px; animation-delay: 0.2s; }
      .c7 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 155px; left: 195px; animation-delay: 0.5s; }
      .c8 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 130px; left: 220px; animation-delay: 0.8s; }
      .c9 { width: 7px; height: 7px; top: 165px; left: 240px; animation-delay: 1.1s; box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--amber-glow), 0 0 22px rgba(212,168,83,0.6); }
      .c10 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 148px; left: 135px; animation-delay: 1.4s; }
      .c11 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 175px; left: 200px; animation-delay: 1.7s; }
      .c12 { width: 3px; height: 3px; top: 120px; left: 180px; animation-delay: 2.0s; }
      
      /* Cluster: France */
      .c13 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 200px; left: 110px; animation-delay: 0.4s; }
      .c14 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 220px; left: 140px; animation-delay: 0.7s; }
      .c15 { width: 6px; height: 6px; top: 235px; left: 165px; animation-delay: 1.0s; box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--amber-glow), 0 0 18px rgba(212,168,83,0.5); }
      .c16 { width: 3px; height: 3px; top: 210px; left: 85px; animation-delay: 1.3s; }
      .c17 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 245px; left: 130px; animation-delay: 1.6s; }
      .c18 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 215px; left: 160px; animation-delay: 0.9s; }
      
      /* Cluster: UK / Ireland */
      .c19 { width: 6px; height: 6px; top: 130px; left: 80px; animation-delay: 0.5s; box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--amber-glow), 0 0 22px rgba(212,168,83,0.6); }
      .c20 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 150px; left: 95px; animation-delay: 0.8s; }
      .c21 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 145px; left: 65px; animation-delay: 1.1s; }
      .c22 { width: 3px; height: 3px; top: 165px; left: 80px; animation-delay: 1.4s; }
      
      /* Cluster: Italy / Mediterranean */
      .c23 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 290px; left: 175px; animation-delay: 0.3s; }
      .c24 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 310px; left: 195px; animation-delay: 0.6s; }
      .c25 { width: 6px; height: 6px; top: 330px; left: 180px; animation-delay: 0.9s; box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--amber-glow), 0 0 18px rgba(212,168,83,0.5); }
      .c26 { width: 3px; height: 3px; top: 350px; left: 200px; animation-delay: 1.2s; }
      .c27 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 280px; left: 155px; animation-delay: 1.5s; }
      
      /* Cluster: Eastern Europe */
      .c28 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 130px; left: 290px; animation-delay: 0.4s; }
      .c29 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 155px; left: 320px; animation-delay: 0.7s; }
      .c30 { width: 6px; height: 6px; top: 175px; left: 280px; animation-delay: 1.0s; box-shadow: 0 0 8px var(--amber-glow), 0 0 18px rgba(212,168,83,0.5); }
      .c31 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 195px; left: 310px; animation-delay: 1.3s; }
      .c32 { width: 3px; height: 3px; top: 145px; left: 345px; animation-delay: 1.6s; }
      .c33 { width: 5px; height: 5px; top: 210px; left: 270px; animation-delay: 0.5s; }
      .c34 { width: 4px; height: 4px; top: 180px; left: 350px; animation-delay: 1.8s; }
      
      /* Scattered smaller dots */
      .s1 { width: 2px; height: 2px; top: 100px; left: 30px; opacity: 0.5; animation-delay: 0.2s; }
      .s2 { width: 2px; height: 2px; top: 180px; left: 50px; opacity: 0.4; animation-delay: 0.8s; }
      .s3 { width: 2px; height: 2px; top: 260px; left: 240px; opacity: 0.5; animation-delay: 1.4s; }
      .s4 { width: 2px; height: 2px; top: 370px; left: 155px; opacity: 0.4; animation-delay: 2.0s; }
      .s5 { width: 2px; height: 2px; top: 105px; left: 350px; opacity: 0.5; animation-delay: 1.0s; }
      .s6 { width: 2px; height: 2px; top: 240px; left: 360px; opacity: 0.4; animation-delay: 1.7s; }
      
      /* Connection lines suggestion via thin gradients */
      .connection-veil {
        position: absolute;
        top: 60px;
        right: 60px;
        width: 620px;
        height: 540px;
        opacity: 0.08;
        background:
          linear-gradient(25deg, transparent 48%, var(--amber) 48%, var(--amber) 52%, transparent 52%),
          linear-gradient(150deg, transparent 45%, var(--amber) 45%, var(--amber) 47%, transparent 47%),
          linear-gradient(-10deg, transparent 40%, var(--amber) 40%, var(--amber) 42%, transparent 42%);
      }
      
      /* Content area */
      .content {
        position: absolute;
        top: 80px;
        left: 70px;
        width: 480px;
        z-index: 10;
      }
      
      .section-num {
        font-size: 14px;
        letter-spacing: 6px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 400;
        margin-bottom: 18px;
      }
      
      .section-num::before {
        content: '';
        display: inline-block;
        width: 36px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--amber);
        vertical-align: middle;
        margin-right: 14px;
      }
      
      .content h1 {
        font-size: 42px;
        font-weight: 400;
        line-height: 1.25;
        color: var(--cream);
        margin: 0 0 24px 0;
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      }
      
      .content .accent-line {
        width: 80px;
        height: 2px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin-bottom: 22px;
      }
      
      .content p.lead {
        font-size: 16px;
        line-height: 1.8;
        color: var(--cream);
        opacity: 0.85;
        margin: 0 0 28px 0;
        max-width: 420px;
      }
      
      .meta-row {
        display: flex;
        gap: 32px;
        align-items: center;
        margin-top: 20px;
      }
      
      .meta-item {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 4px;
      }
      
      .meta-item .num {
        font-size: 22px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .meta-item .label {
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      /* Bottom strip */
      .bottom-strip {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 40px;
        left: 70px;
        right: 70px;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
        border-top: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.2);
        padding-top: 18px;
      }
      
      .bottom-strip .left {
        font-size: 12px;
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      .bottom-strip .page-num {
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
      }
      
      .bottom-strip .page-num strong {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="star-layer"></div>
      <div class="landmass"></div>
      
      <div class="settlement-grid">
        <span class="dot c1"></span><span class="dot c2"></span><span class="dot c3"></span><span class="dot c4"></span><span class="dot c5"></span>
        <span class="dot c6"></span><span class="dot c7"></span><span class="dot c8"></span><span class="dot c9"></span><span class="dot c10"></span><span class="dot c11"></span><span class="dot c12"></span>
        <span class="dot c13"></span><span class="dot c14"></span><span class="dot c15"></span><span class="dot c16"></span><span class="dot c17"></span><span class="dot c18"></span>
        <span class="dot c19"></span><span class="dot c20"></span><span class="dot c21"></span><span class="dot c22"></span>
        <span class="dot c23"></span><span class="dot c24"></span><span class="dot c25"></span><span class="dot c26"></span><span class="dot c27"></span>
        <span class="dot c28"></span><span class="dot c29"></span><span class="dot c30"></span><span class="dot c31"></span><span class="dot c32"></span><span class="dot c33"></span><span class="dot c34"></span>
        <span class="dot s1"></span><span class="dot s2"></span><span class="dot s3"></span><span class="dot s4"></span><span class="dot s5"></span><span class="dot s6"></span>
      </div>
      <div class="connection-veil"></div>
      
      <div class="content">
        <div class="section-num">SECTION · IV</div>
        <h1>开源在现代数字社会中的概况</h1>
        <div class="accent-line"></div>
        <p class="lead">从代码仓库到城市基础设施，开源已如夜间灯光般散布于人类活动的每一个节点——它既是协作的文明，也是数字时代的公共知识体系。</p>
        <div class="meta-row">

            <span class="num">04</span>

          </div>
          <div class="meta-item">
            <span class="num">19</span>
            <span class="label">Now</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="bottom-strip">
        <span class="left">The Landscape of Open Source</span>
        <span class="page-num"><strong>19</strong> &nbsp;/&nbsp; 52</span>

  - num: 20
    visual: "蓝色点阵世界地图与橙色数据流——开源无处不在的全球连接"
    bullets:
      - "无处不在"
      - "来源：LF Research Measuring the Economic Value of Open Source： A Survey and a Preliminary Analysis"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
      }
      
      /* Dot matrix world map background */
      .map-grid {
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        opacity: 0.35;
        background-image: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(245,240,232,0.4) 1.5px, transparent 2px);
        background-size: 22px 22px;
      }
      
      /* Highlighted continent dots */
      .continent {
        position: absolute;
        display: flex;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 3px;
      }
      
      .continent .dot {
        width: 3px;
        height: 3px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: rgba(245,240,232,0.7);
      }
      
      /* North America */
      .c-na { top: 180px; left: 140px; width: 200px; }
      /* South America */
      .c-sa { top: 390px; left: 230px; width: 80px; }
      /* Europe */
      .c-eu { top: 170px; left: 560px; width: 120px; }
      /* Africa */
      .c-af { top: 320px; left: 570px; width: 130px; }
      /* Asia */
      .c-as { top: 170px; left: 700px; width: 320px; }
      /* Australia */
      .c-au { top: 450px; left: 970px; width: 120px; }
      
      /* Key node points */
      .node {
        position: absolute;
        width: 10px;
        height: 10px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
        box-shadow: 0 0 12px var(--amber), 0 0 24px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      .node::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: -5px;
        left: -5px;
        width: 20px;
        height: 20px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.3);
      }
      
      .n1 { top: 200px; left: 200px; }
      .n2 { top: 260px; left: 420px; }
      .n3 { top: 190px; left: 600px; }
      .n4 { top: 240px; left: 820px; }
      .n5 { top: 280px; left: 1000px; }
      .n6 { top: 420px; left: 500px; }
      .n7 { top: 470px; left: 1000px; }
      .n8 { top: 350px; left: 280px; }
      
      /* Data flow arcs using CSS borders */
      .flow {
        position: absolute;
        border: 1.5px solid var(--amber);
        border-radius: 50%;
        opacity: 0;
        animation: pulse 3s ease-in-out infinite;
      }
      
      @keyframes pulse {
        0%, 100% { opacity: 0; }
        50% { opacity: 0.5; }
      }
      
      .flow1 { top: 170px; left: 175px; width: 350px; height: 140px; border-color: rgba(212,168,83,0.4); animation-delay: 0s; }
      .flow2 { top: 160px; left: 575px; width: 370px; height: 160px; border-color: rgba(212,168,83,0.3); animation-delay: 1s; }
      .flow3 { top: 250px; left: 400px; width: 650px; height: 200px; border-color: rgba(212,168,83,0.25); animation-delay: 2s; }
      .flow4 { top: 340px; left: 260px; width: 480px; height: 220px; border-color: rgba(212,168,83,0.3); animation-delay: 0.5s; }
      
      /* Flowing dashed lines */
      .dash-line {
        position: absolute;
        height: 1px;
        background: repeating-linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(212,168,83,0.5) 0, rgba(212,168,83,0.5) 8px, transparent 8px, transparent 16px);
        transform-origin: left center;
        animation: flowDash 4s linear infinite;
      }
      
      @keyframes flowDash {
        0% { background-position: 0 0; }
        100% { background-position: 32px 0; }
      }
      
      .dl1 { top: 230px; left: 205px; width: 400px; transform: rotate(12deg); }
      .dl2 { top: 205px; left: 605px; width: 400px; transform: rotate(8deg); }
      .dl3 { top: 330px; left: 285px; width: 500px; transform: rotate(-10deg); }
      .dl4 { top: 430px; left: 505px; width: 500px; transform: rotate(-8deg); }
      
      /* Central content */
      .content {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 10;
        text-align: center;
        margin-top: 80px;
      }
      
      .eyebrow {
        font-size: 14px;
        letter-spacing: 8px;
        color: var(--amber);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 30px;
      }
      
      .main-title {
        font-size: 108px;
        font-weight: 400;
        color: var(--cream);
        letter-spacing: 24px;
        line-height: 1.2;
        margin: 0;
        padding-left: 24px;
      }
      
      .title-underline {
        width: 180px;
        height: 2px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin: 40px auto;
        position: relative;
      }
      
      .title-underline::before,
      .title-underline::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        top: -3px;
        width: 8px;
        height: 8px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .title-underline::before { left: -16px; }
      .title-underline::after { right: -16px; }
      
      .subtitle {
        font-size: 18px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 4px;
        margin-bottom: 60px;
      }
      
      /* Source */
      .source {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 40px;
        left: 60px;
        right: 60px;
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
      }
      
      .source .src-text {
        line-height: 1.6;
        max-width: 800px;
      }
      
      .source .src-text em {
        font-style: italic;
        color: rgba(245,240,232,0.5);
      }
      
      .page-num {
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 3px;
      }
      
      /* Left/right decorative lines */
      .side-line {
        position: absolute;
        top: 60px;
        bottom: 60px;
        width: 1px;
        background: rgba(212,168,83,0.2);
      }
      
      .side-line.left { left: 60px; }
      .side-line.right { right: 60px; }
      
      /* Corner accents */
      .corner {
        position: absolute;
        width: 20px;
        height: 20px;
        border-color: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .corner.tl { top: 45px; left: 45px; border-top: 1px solid var(--amber); border-left: 1px solid var(--amber); }
      .corner.tr { top: 45px; right: 45px; border-top: 1px solid var(--amber); border-right: 1px solid var(--amber); }
      .corner.bl { bottom: 45px; left: 45px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--amber); border-left: 1px solid var(--amber); }
      .corner.br { bottom: 45px; right: 45px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--amber); border-right: 1px solid var(--amber); }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="map-grid"></div>
      
      <div class="continent c-na">
      <div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div>
      <div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div>
      <div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div>
      <div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div>
      <div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div>
      <div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div><div class="dot"></div>
