    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        color:var(--cream);
        background:var(--prussian);
        width:100%;
        height:660px;
        padding:60px 80px;
        box-sizing:border-box;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
      }
      
      /* Sequin grid backdrop */
      .sequin-grid{
        position:absolute;
        top:0;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;
        background-image:
          radial-gradient(circle at 25% 25%, rgba(212,168,83,0.06) 2px, transparent 2.5px),
          radial-gradient(circle at 75% 25%, rgba(212,168,83,0.05) 2px, transparent 2.5px),
          radial-gradient(circle at 25% 75%, rgba(212,168,83,0.05) 2px, transparent 2.5px),
          radial-gradient(circle at 75% 75%, rgba(212,168,83,0.06) 2px, transparent 2.5px);
        background-size:60px 60px, 60px 60px, 60px 60px, 60px 60px;
        opacity:0.8;
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      
      /* Accent geometric grid lines */
      .grid-lines{
        position:absolute;
        top:0;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;
        border-right:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.08);
        border-bottom:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.08);
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      .grid-lines::before{
        content:'';
        position:absolute;
        top:33.33%;left:0;right:0;
        border-top:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.07);
      }
      .grid-lines::after{
        content:'';
        position:absolute;
        top:66.66%;left:0;right:0;
        border-top:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.07);
      }
      
      .content{
        position:relative;
        z-index:2;
        height:100%;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
      }
      
      .header{
        display:flex;
        align-items:flex-end;
        justify-content:space-between;
        margin-bottom:8px;
      }
      
      .title-block{
        flex:1;
      }
      
      .eyebrow{
        font-size:13px;
        letter-spacing:4px;
        text-transform:uppercase;
        color:var(--amber);
        margin-bottom:18px;
        font-weight:600;
      }
      
      h1{
        font-size:40px;
        font-weight:700;
        line-height:1.2;
        color:var(--cream);
        margin:0 0 12px 0;
        letter-spacing:0.5px;
      }
      
      h1 em{
        font-style:italic;
        color:var(--amber);
        font-weight:600;
      }
      
      .subtitle{
        font-size:16px;
        color:rgba(245,240,232,0.6);
        font-style:italic;
        font-weight:400;
      }
      
      .slide-number{
        font-size:13px;
        color:rgba(212,168,83,0.5);
        letter-spacing:2px;
        text-align:right;
        font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;
      }
      
      .divider{
        width:80px;
        height:2px;
        background:var(--amber);
        margin:22px 0 28px 0;
        position:relative;
      }
      .divider::after{
        content:'';
        position:absolute;
        left:80px;top:50%;
        width:100%;
        height:1px;
        background:rgba(212,168,83,0.15);
      }
      
      .bullet-list{
        list-style:none;
        margin:0;
        padding:0;
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;
        gap:14px 60px;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li{
        display:flex;
        align-items:flex-start;
        gap:16px;
        font-size:17px;
        line-height:1.5;
        color:rgba(245,240,232,0.85);
        padding:10px 0;
        border-bottom:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.12);
      }
      
      .bullet-list li .num{
        font-size:12px;
        font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;
        color:var(--amber);
        letter-spacing:1px;
        min-width:28px;
        padding-top:4px;
        font-weight:600;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li .text{
        flex:1;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li .text strong{
        color:var(--cream);
        font-weight:600;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li .text span.orgs{
        display:block;
        font-size:12px;
        color:rgba(212,168,83,0.7);
        letter-spacing:0.5px;
        margin-top:3px;
        font-weight:400;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li.future{
        grid-column:1 / -1;
        justify-content:flex-start;
        padding:16px 0 0 0;
        border-bottom:none;
        margin-top:6px;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li.future .text{
        font-style:italic;
        font-size:18px;
        color:var(--amber);
        font-weight:500;
      }
      
      .footer{
        margin-top:auto;
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:space-between;
        padding-top:20px;
        font-size:11px;
        color:rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        letter-spacing:2px;
        text-transform:uppercase;
      }
      
      .footer .seq{
        display:flex;
        gap:6px;
      }
      .footer .seq span{
        display:inline-block;
        width:8px;height:8px;
        border:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.3);
      }
      .footer .seq span:nth-child(odd){
        background:rgba(212,168,83,0.2);
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="sequin-grid"></div>
      <div class="grid-lines"></div>
      
      <div class="content">
        <div class="header">
          <div class="title-block">
            <div class="eyebrow">Open Source Institutions</div>
            <h1>开源机构与组织<br><em>— 自由软件基金会的设计与光芒</em></h1>
            <p class="subtitle">从 FSF 的诞生到开源生态的全球网络</p>

          <div class="slide-number">31 / 52</div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="divider"></div>
      
        <ul class="bullet-list">
          <li>
            <span class="num">01</span>
            <span class="text"><strong>开源机构与组织</strong></span>
          </li>
          <li>
            <span class="num">02</span>
            <span class="text"><strong>自由软件基金会的诞生与设计</strong></span>
          </li>
          <li>
            <span class="num">03</span>
            <span class="text"><strong>早期的开放组织、标准组织</strong><span class="orgs">IETF &amp; standards bodies</span></span>
          </li>
          <li>
            <span class="num">04</span>
            <span class="text"><strong>开源软件基金会的崛起</strong></span>

          <li>

          </li>
          <li class="future">
            <span class="num">06</span>
            <span class="text">开源的未来 ～</span>
          </li>
        </ul>
      
        <div class="footer">
          <span>Section Ⅴ · The Institutions of Open Source</span>
          <div class="seq"><span></span><span></span><span></span><span></span><span></span></div>
          <span>Free Software Foundation · Design &amp; Radiance</span>
        </div>
      </div>

  - num: 32
    visual: "三色绳索紧密交织的绳结——商业模式的多元融合"
    bullets:
      - "商业模式的探索"
      - "商业是制度的产物"
      - "开源许可放弃了排他权式的授权收取费用模式"

    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        --prussian-light: #2C5A9E;
        --copper-light: #A89070;
      
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        padding: 60px 80px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header {
        display: flex;
        align-items: baseline;
        justify-content: space-between;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
        padding-bottom: 16px;
        margin-bottom: 32px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header h1 {
        font-size: 32px;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--cream);
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
        margin: 0;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header .page-num {
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: italic;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .subtitle {
        font-size: 15px;
        color: var(--copper-light);
        font-style: italic;
        margin-bottom: 28px;
        letter-spacing: 0.3px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .main-area {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 280px 1fr;
        gap: 50px;
        align-items: start;
      }
      
      /* Braid Visual Metaphor */
      .slide-inner .braid-container {
        position: relative;
        height: 420px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: flex-start;
        padding-top: 10px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .braid {
        position: relative;
        width: 160px;
        height: 320px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .strand {
        position: absolute;
        width: 46px;
        height: 320px;
        border-radius: 23px;
        top: 0;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .strand-a {
        left: 0;
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--prussian-light) 0%, var(--prussian) 30%, var(--amber) 55%, var(--copper) 75%, var(--prussian-light) 100%);
        box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(44, 90, 158, 0.4), inset 0 0 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
        transform: skewX(-8deg);
        z-index: 1;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .strand-b {
        left: 57px;
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--amber) 0%, var(--copper) 25%, var(--prussian) 50%, var(--amber) 70%, var(--copper) 100%);
        box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3), inset 0 0 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
        transform: skewX(8deg);
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .strand-c {
        left: 114px;
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--copper) 0%, var(--prussian) 30%, var(--amber) 50%, var(--prussian-light) 70%, var(--copper) 100%);
        box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.4), inset 0 0 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
        transform: skewX(-4deg);
        z-index: 1;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .knot {
        position: absolute;
        width: 80px;
        height: 80px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 3px solid var(--amber);
        top: 110px;
        left: 40px;
        z-index: 3;
        background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--prussian) 30%, rgba(27,59,107,0.6) 70%, transparent 100%);
        box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.25);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .knot::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        width: 24px;
        height: 24px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
        top: 28px;
        left: 28px;
        box-shadow: 0 0 8px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.6);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .braid-label {
        text-align: center;
        margin-top: 20px;
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: italic;
        line-height: 1.5;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .braid-label .strong {
        display: block;
        font-style: normal;
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--copper-light);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        margin-bottom: 6px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      /* Content Area */
      .slide-inner .content {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .thesis {
        font-size: 19px;
        color: var(--cream);
        line-height: 1.7;
        padding: 14px 20px;
        border-left: 3px solid var(--amber);
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.06);
        margin-bottom: 22px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .thesis em {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .revenue-title {
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--copper-light);
        letter-spacing: 2.5px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 14px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .streams {
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 10px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .stream {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 50px 1fr;
        align-items: center;
        padding: 10px 16px;
        border-radius: 4px;
        background: rgba(245, 240, 232, 0.03);
        border: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .stream-num {
        font-size: 22px;
        font-weight: 600;
        text-align: center;
        border-right: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.3);
        padding-right: 12px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .stream-1 .stream-num { color: var(--amber); }
      .slide-inner .stream-2 .stream-num { color: var(--copper-light); }
      .slide-inner .stream-3 .stream-num { color: var(--prussian-light); }
      
      .slide-inner .stream-text {
        padding-left: 16px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .stream-text .label {
        font-size: 15px;
        color: var(--cream);
        font-weight: 500;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .stream-text .desc {
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--copper-light);
        font-style: italic;
        margin-top: 2px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .footer-line {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 24px;
        left: 80px;
        right: 80px;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 1.5px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .footer-line .dot {
        width: 4px;
        height: 4px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
        display: inline-block;
        margin: 0 8px;
        vertical-align: middle;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <h1 style="display:none"></h1>
      
      <div class="header">
        <h1>商业模式的探索</h1>
        <span class="page-num">032 / 052</span>
      </div>
      
      <p class="subtitle">三色绳索紧密交织的绳结 —— 商业模式的多元融合</p>
      
      <div class="main-area">
      
        <div class="braid-container">
          <div class="braid">
            <div class="strand strand-a"></div>
            <div class="strand strand-b"></div>
            <div class="strand strand-c"></div>
            <div class="knot"></div>
          </div>
          <div class="braid-label">
            <span class="strong">Braided Model</span>
            三条绳索，一个绳结<br>多元收入，一体共生
          </div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="content">
          <div class="thesis">
            商业是<em>制度</em>的产物。开源许可放弃了<em>排他权式的授权</em>收取费用模式，转而编织出多元化的价值索取结构。
          </div>
      
          <div class="revenue-title">三种收入来源的交织</div>
      
          <div class="streams">
            <div class="stream stream-1">
              <div class="stream-num">Ⅰ</div>
              <div class="stream-text">
                <span class="label">提供定制</span>
                <div class="desc">服务 / 支持 / 培训 / 咨询</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="stream stream-2">
              <div class="stream-num">Ⅱ</div>
              <div class="stream-text">
                <span class="label">安全 / 声誉 / 保证</span>
                <div class="desc">以信任与品牌溢价获取收益</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="stream stream-3">
              <div class="stream-num">Ⅲ</div>
              <div class="stream-text">
                <span class="label">订阅</span>
                <div class="desc">持续交付，长期绑定</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="footer-line">
        <span>BUSINESS MODEL</span>
        <span>OSS <span class="dot"></span> REVENUE <span class="dot"></span> ECOSYSTEM</span>
      </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 33
    visual: "紫色亮片如梦幻星空——「开源之史」讲谈项目的光芒与梦想"
    bullets:
      - "同时启动通识项目：「开源之史」讲谈"
      - "每双周一次讲谈"
      - "东杰、海玲项目联合发起人，查史料、辅讲"
      - "如和开源经济学冲突，则顺延"
      - "2024 年底看效果"
      - "诚邀加入，一起学习、探讨、讲谈关于开源相关的历史！"
    designed: |
      <style>
          .slide-page {
            width: 1280px;
            height: 720px;
            background: var(--prussian);
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
            font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
            color: var(--cream);
            padding: 60px 80px;
            box-sizing: border-box;
          }
      
          /* Starry sky effect using box-shadow sparkles */
          .star-layer {
            position: absolute;
            top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0;
            pointer-events: none;
            z-index: 0;
          }
          .star {
            position: absolute;
            width: 2px;
            height: 2px;
            background: var(--cream);
            border-radius: 50%;
            opacity: 0.85;
            box-shadow: 0 0 4px 1px rgba(245, 240, 232, 0.5);
          }
          .star.s2 {
            width: 3px; height: 3px;
            background: var(--amber);
            box-shadow: 0 0 6px 2px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.6);
          }
          .star.s3 {
            width: 1.5px; height: 1.5px;
            background: #c4a1e0;
            box-shadow: 0 0 5px 1.5px rgba(196, 161, 224, 0.5);
          }
          .star.s4 {
            width: 2.5px; height: 2.5px;
            background: #b08cd6;
            box-shadow: 0 0 6px 2px rgba(176, 140, 214, 0.5);
          }
      
          .content {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
            height: 100%;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
          }
      
          .header {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 20px;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
          }
          .header-line {
            flex: 1;
            height: 1px;
            background: linear-gradient(to right, transparent, var(--copper), transparent);
          }
          .page-num {
            font-size: 13px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 2px;
          }
      
          .title-zone {
            text-align: center;
            margin-bottom: 36px;
            padding: 20px 0;
          }
          .title-glow {
            width: 280px;
            height: 280px;
            position: absolute;
            top: 50%;
            left: 50%;
            transform: translate(-50%, -58%);
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(196, 161, 224, 0.12) 0%, rgba(176, 140, 214, 0.06) 40%, transparent 70%);
            z-index: 1;
            pointer-events: none;
          }
          .title-zone h1 {
            font-size: 42px;
            font-weight: 400;
            color: var(--cream);
            letter-spacing: 6px;
            margin: 0 0 12px 0;
            position: relative;
          }
          .title-zone h1 .accent {
            color: var(--amber);
          }
          .title-zone .subtitle {
            font-size: 16px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 4px;
            font-style: italic;
          }
          .title-zone .divider-dot {
            display: inline-block;
            width: 6px; height: 6px;
            background: var(--amber);
            border-radius: 50%;
            margin: 0 16px;
            vertical-align: middle;
            box-shadow: 0 0 8px 2px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.5);
          }
      
          .body-zone {
            flex: 1;
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
            gap: 50px;
            align-items: start;
            padding: 0 20px;
          }
      
          .left-col {
            border-left: 2px solid var(--copper);
            padding: 6px 0 6px 28px;
          }
          .left-col h3 {
            font-size: 14px;
            color: var(--amber);
            letter-spacing: 3px;
            margin: 0 0 18px 0;
            font-weight: 400;
          }
          .left-col ul {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
          }
          .left-col ul li {
            font-size: 15px;
            color: var(--cream);
            line-height: 1.9;
            padding-left: 18px;
            position: relative;
          }
          .left-col ul li::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            left: 0;
            top: 12px;
            width: 5px;
            height: 5px;
            background: var(--amber);
            border-radius: 50%;
            box-shadow: 0 0 4px 1px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.5);
          }
      
          .right-col {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 18px;
          }
          .info-card {
            border: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
            border-radius: 4px;
            padding: 18px 22px;
            background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.04);
          }
          .info-card .card-label {
            font-size: 12px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            margin-bottom: 6px;
          }
          .info-card .card-value {
            font-size: 16px;
            color: var(--cream);
            line-height: 1.6;
          }
          .info-card .card-value strong {
            color: var(--amber);
            font-weight: 400;
          }
      
          .cta-zone {
            text-align: center;
            margin-top: 24px;
            padding-top: 22px;
            border-top: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.3);
            position: relative;
          }
          .cta-zone .cta-text {
            font-size: 18px;
            color: var(--cream);
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            font-style: italic;
          }
          .cta-zone .cta-text .highlight {
            color: var(--amber);
            font-style: normal;
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--amber);
            padding-bottom: 2px;
          }
          .cta-zone .cta-sub {
            font-size: 13px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 3px;
            margin-top: 8px;
          }
        </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
        
      
        <div class="star-layer">
          <div class="star" style="top:5%; left:8%;"></div>
          <div class="star s2" style="top:8%; left:22%;"></div>
          <div class="star s3" style="top:12%; left:35%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:6%; left:48%;"></div>
          <div class="star s4" style="top:14%; left:58%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:9%; left:72%;"></div>

          <div class="star s3" style="top:4%; left:91%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:22%; left:14%;"></div>
          <div class="star s4" style="top:28%; left:3%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:32%; left:45%;"></div>
          <div class="star s2" style="top:26%; left:88%;"></div>
          <div class="star s3" style="top:38%; left:92%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:42%; left:6%;"></div>
          <div class="star s4" style="top:50%; left:2%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:55%; left:95%;"></div>
          <div class="star s2" style="top:62%; left:4%;"></div>
          <div class="star s3" style="top:68%; left:97%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:74%; left:8%;"></div>
          <div class="star s4" style="top:80%; left:2%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:84%; left:94%;"></div>
          <div class="star s2" style="top:88%; left:12%;"></div>
          <div class="star s3" style="top:92%; left:24%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:95%; left:38%;"></div>
          <div class="star s4" style="top:90%; left:52%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:93%; left:66%;"></div>
          <div class="star s2" style="top:88%; left:78%;"></div>
          <div class="star s3" style="top:95%; left:88%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:48%; left:1%;"></div>
          <div class="star s2" style="top:44%; left:98%;"></div>
          <div class="star s4" style="top:72%; left:96%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:6%; left:62%;"></div>
          <div class="star s3" style="top:18%; left:46%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:34%; left:74%;"></div>
          <div class="star s2" style="top:46%; left:8%;"></div>
          <div class="star" style="top:58%; left:95%;"></div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="content">
          <div class="header">
            <span class="page-num">33 / 52</span>
            <div class="header-line"></div>
            <span class="page-num">PROJECT · LAUNCH</span>
          </div>
      
          <div class="title-zone">
            <div class="title-glow"></div>
            <h1><span class="accent">开源之史</span>讲谈项目</h1>
            <div class="subtitle">History of Open Source <span class="divider-dot"></span> A Dream in Stars</div>
          </div>
      
          <div class="body-zone">
            <div class="left-col">
              <h3>PROJECT BRIEF</h3>
              <ul>
                <li>同时启动通识项目：「开源之史」讲谈</li>

  - num: 34
    visual: "鹰立于雪山之巅——产业视角下的开源：视野与力量"
    bullets:
      - "产业视角下的开源"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        --prussian:#1B3B6B;
        --cream:#F5F0E8;
        --copper:#8B7355;
        --amber:#D4A853;
        --muted:#555555;
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        width:1280px;
        height:720px;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
        background:linear-gradient(180deg, #0F2A4D 0%, #1B3B6B 55%, #2A4F7F 100%);
        color:var(--cream);
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        align-items:center;
        justify-content:center;
      }
      
      /* Mountain peak silhouette - top center triangle */
      .peak{
        position:absolute;
        top:60px;
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        width:0;
        height:0;
        border-left:180px solid transparent;
        border-right:180px solid transparent;
        border-bottom:280px solid rgba(245,240,232,0.04);
      }
      
      .peak-inner{
        position:absolute;
        top:110px;
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        width:0;
        height:0;
        border-left:120px solid transparent;
        border-right:120px solid transparent;
        border-bottom:220px solid rgba(245,240,232,0.06);
      }
      
      .peak-core{
        position:absolute;
        top:170px;
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        width:0;
        height:0;
        border-left:60px solid transparent;
        border-right:60px solid transparent;
        border-bottom:160px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.08);
      }
      
      /* Eagle / eye motif at the peak apex */
      .eagle{
        position:absolute;
        top:50px;
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        width:50px;
        height:50px;
      }
      
      .eagle-dot{
        position:absolute;
        top:8px;
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        width:10px;
        height:10px;
        border-radius:50%;
        background:var(--amber);
        box-shadow:0 0 20px rgba(212,168,83,0.5), 0 0 40px rgba(212,168,83,0.25);
      }
      
      .eagle-line{
        position:absolute;
        top:22px;
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        width:2px;
        height:14px;
        background:var(--amber);
        opacity:0.6;
      }
      
      .eagle-wing-l{
        position:absolute;
        top:26px;
        left:2px;
        width:20px;
        height:1px;
        background:var(--amber);
        opacity:0.5;
      }
      
      .eagle-wing-r{
        position:absolute;
        top:26px;
        right:2px;
        width:20px;
        height:1px;
        background:var(--amber);
        opacity:0.5;
      }
      
      /* Horizontal horizon line */
      .horizon{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:180px;
        left:0;
        right:0;
        height:1px;
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, rgba(212,168,83,0.3) 20%, rgba(212,168,83,0.5) 50%, rgba(212,168,83,0.3) 80%, transparent 100%);
      }
      
      .horizon-lower{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:150px;
        left:15%;
        right:15%;
        height:1px;
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, rgba(212,168,83,0.15) 50%, transparent 100%);
      }
      
      /* Vertical accent lines */
      .vline-left{
        position:absolute;
        left:90px;
        top:120px;
        width:1px;
        height:480px;
        background:linear-gradient(180deg, transparent 0%, rgba(212,168,83,0.2) 30%, rgba(212,168,83,0.2) 70%, transparent 100%);
      }
      
      .vline-right{
        position:absolute;
        right:90px;
        top:120px;
        width:1px;
        height:480px;
        background:linear-gradient(180deg, transparent 0%, rgba(212,168,83,0.2) 30%, rgba(212,168,83,0.2) 70%, transparent 100%);
      }
      
      /* Section number */
      .section-num{
        font-size:13px;
        letter-spacing:6px;
        color:var(--amber);
        text-transform:uppercase;
        margin-bottom:30px;
        opacity:0.9;
      }
      
      /* Main title */
      .main-title{
        font-size:56px;
        font-weight:600;
        color:var(--cream);
        letter-spacing:8px;
        text-align:center;
        line-height:1.4;
        text-indent:8px;
        position:relative;
        z-index:2;
      }
      
      /* Title underline */
      .title-underline{
        width:120px;
        height:2px;
        background:var(--amber);
        margin:35px auto 30px auto;
        box-shadow:0 0 12px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      /* Subtitle */
      .subtitle{
        font-size:16px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:4px;
        text-align:center;
        font-style:italic;
        text-indent:4px;
        z-index:2;
      }
      
      /* Bottom meta */
      .bottom-meta{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:50px;
        left:0;
        right:0;
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        padding:0 90px;
        font-size:12px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:3px;
        opacity:0.7;
      }
      
      /* Small diamond decorations on horizon */
      .diamond{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:177px;
        width:8px;
        height:8px;
        background:var(--amber);
        transform:rotate(45deg);
        box-shadow:0 0 10px rgba(212,168,83,0.5);
      }
      
      .diamond-l{left:22%;}
      .diamond-r{right:22%;}
      .diamond-c{left:50%;margin-left:-4px;opacity:0.6;width:6px;height:6px;}
      
      /* Corner marks */
      .corner-tl{
        position:absolute;
        top:40px;
        left:40px;
        width:24px;
        height:24px;
        border-top:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        border-left:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      .corner-tr{
        position:absolute;
        top:40px;
        right:40px;
        width:24px;
        height:24px;
        border-top:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        border-right:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      .corner-bl{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:40px;
        left:40px;
        width:24px;
        height:24px;
        border-bottom:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        border-left:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      .corner-br{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:40px;
        right:40px;
        width:24px;
        height:24px;
        border-bottom:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        border-right:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      
      /* Snowfall - subtle dots */
      .snow{
        position:absolute;
        width:2px;
        height:2px;
        background:rgba(245,240,232,0.3);
        border-radius:50%;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="corner-tl"></div>
      <div class="corner-tr"></div>
      <div class="corner-bl"></div>
      <div class="corner-br"></div>
      
      <div class="vline-left"></div>
      <div class="vline-right"></div>
      
      <div class="peak"></div>
      <div class="peak-inner"></div>
      <div class="peak-core"></div>
      
      <div class="eagle">
        <div class="eagle-dot"></div>
        <div class="eagle-wing-l"></div>
        <div class="eagle-wing-r"></div>
        <div class="eagle-line"></div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="horizon"></div>
      <div class="horizon-lower"></div>
      
      <div class="diamond diamond-l"></div>
      <div class="diamond diamond-r"></div>
      <div class="diamond diamond-c"></div>
      
      <div class="section-num">PART&nbsp;&nbsp;·&nbsp;&nbsp;SECTION</div>
      
      <h1 class="main-title">产业视角下的开源</h1>
      
      <div class="title-underline"></div>
      
      <p class="subtitle">Vision and Power from an Industrial Perspective</p>
      
      <div class="bottom-meta">
        <span>OPEN SOURCE</span>
        <span>34 / 52</span>
      </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 35
    visual: "三分天下：全封闭、半封闭、全开放的几何分割——生态三势力"
    bullets:
      - "生态之三分天下"
      - "全封闭（Apple）"
      - "硬件开放，软件封闭（Windows + Intel）"
      - "全开放：Linux  （强劲的生命力）"
    designed: |
      <style>
          .slide-inner {
            --prussian:#1B3B6B;
            --cream:#F5F0E8;
            --copper:#8B7355;
            --amber:#D4A853;
            --muted:#555555;
            font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
            background: var(--prussian);
            color: var(--cream);
            width:1280px;
            height:720px;
            position:relative;
            overflow:hidden;
            padding:60px 80px;
            box-sizing:border-box;
          }
          .s35-header {
            display:flex;
            align-items:baseline;
            justify-content:space-between;
            margin-bottom:12px;
          }
          .s35-title {
            font-size:34px;
            font-weight:600;
            letter-spacing:2px;
            color: var(--cream);
          }
          .s35-title em {
            color: var(--amber);
            font-style:normal;
          }
          .s35-page-num {
            font-size:14px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing:3px;
          }
          .s35-rule {
            width:100%;
            height:1px;
            background: linear-gradient(to right, transparent, var(--copper) 20%, var(--copper) 80%, transparent);
            margin-bottom:42px;
          }
      
          .s35-trichotomy {
            display:grid;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr;
            gap:30px;
            height:430px;
          }
          .s35-col {
            position:relative;
            padding:30px 26px 26px 26px;
            border-top:3px solid var(--amber);
            display:flex;
            flex-direction:column;
          }
          .s35-col::before {
            content:"";
            position:absolute;
            top:-3px;
            left:0;
            width:40px;
            height:3px;
            background: var(--amber);
            box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 transparent;
          }
          .s35-col-num {
            font-size:13px;
            letter-spacing:4px;
            color: var(--copper);
            margin-bottom:18px;
          }
          .s35-col-label {
            font-size:14px;
            letter-spacing:3px;
            color: var(--amber);
            margin-bottom:10px;
            text-transform:uppercase;
          }
          .s35-col-name {
            font-size:26px;
            font-weight:600;
            color: var(--cream);
            margin-bottom:8px;
            line-height:1.3;
          }
          .s35-col-sub {
            font-size:13px;
            color: var(--copper);
            margin-bottom:22px;
            letter-spacing:1px;
          }
      
          .s35-geo {
            height:80px;
            display:flex;
            align-items:center;
            justify-content:center;
            margin-bottom:22px;
          }
          /* 全封闭：实心圆 */
          .s35-geo-closed {
            width:64px;
            height:64px;
            border-radius:50%;
            background: var(--amber);
          }
          /* 半封闭：半圆 */
          .s35-geo-half {
            width:64px;
            height:32px;
            border-radius:64px 64px 0 0;
            background: var(--amber);
            position:relative;
          }
          .s35-geo-half::after {
            content:"";
            position:absolute;
            bottom:-2px;
            left:0;
            width:64px;
            height:1px;
            background: var(--amber);
          }
          /* 全开放：空心圆 + 发散 */
          .s35-geo-open {
            width:60px;
            height:60px;
            border-radius:50%;
            border:2px solid var(--amber);
            position:relative;
          }
          .s35-geo-open::before {
            content:"";
            position:absolute;
            top:50%;
            left:50%;
            transform:translate(-50%,-50%);
            width:24px;
            height:24px;
            border-radius:50%;
            border:1px solid var(--copper);
          }
          .s35-geo-open::after {
            content:"";
            position:absolute;
            top:50%;
            left:50%;
            transform:translate(-50%,-50%);
            width:88px;
            height:88px;
            border-radius:50%;
            border:1px dashed var(--copper);
            opacity:0.5;
          }
      
          .s35-desc {
            font-size:15px;
            line-height:1.75;
            color: var(--cream);
            opacity:0.92;
            margin-bottom:16px;
          }
          .s35-desc strong {
            color: var(--amber);
            font-weight:600;
          }
          .s35-tag {
            margin-top:auto;
            font-size:12px;
            letter-spacing:2px;
            color: var(--copper);
            border-top:1px solid rgba(139,115,85,0.4);
            padding-top:12px;
          }
          .s35-tag-vital {
            color: var(--amber);
          }
      
          .s35-footer {
            position:absolute;
            bottom:30px;
            left:80px;
            right:80px;
            display:flex;
            justify-content:space-between;
            align-items:center;
            font-size:12px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing:2px;
          }
          .s35-footer-line {
            flex:1;
            height:1px;
            background: rgba(139,115,85,0.3);
            margin:0 20px;
          }
        </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
        
        <div class="s35-header">
          <div class="s35-title">生态之<em>三分天下</em></div>
          <div class="s35-page-num">35 / 52</div>
        </div>
        <div class="s35-rule"></div>
      
        <div class="s35-trichotomy">
          <div class="s35-col">
            <div class="s35-col-num">I</div>
            <div class="s35-col-label">Fully Closed</div>
            <div class="s35-col-name">全封闭</div>
            <div class="s35-col-sub">— Apple —</div>
            <div class="s35-geo"><div class="s35-geo-closed"></div></div>
            <div class="s35-desc">硬件与软件<strong>一体化掌控</strong>。从芯片到操作系统，从应用商店到开发者政策，全部由单一实体定义边界。</div>
            <div class="s35-tag">WALLED GARDEN · 围墙花园</div>
          </div>
      
          <div class="s35-col">
            <div class="s35-col-num">II</div>
            <div class="s35-col-label">Semi-Closed</div>
            <div class="s35-col-name">半封闭</div>
            <div class="s35-col-sub">— Windows + Intel —</div>
            <div class="s35-geo"><div class="s35-geo-half"></div></div>
            <div class="s35-desc">硬件<strong>开放生态</strong>，软件<strong>封闭掌控</strong>。OEM 厂商百花齐放，操作系统由微软统一收编——一半开放，一半垄断。</div>
            <div class="s35-tag">HYBRID · 半透膜</div>
          </div>
      
          <div class="s35-col">
            <div class="s35-col-num">III</div>
            <div class="s35-col-label">Fully Open</div>
            <div class="s35-col-name">全开放</div>
            <div class="s35-col-sub">— Linux —</div>
            <div class="s35-geo"><div class="s35-geo-open"></div></div>
            <div class="s35-desc">无主之地，众人共建。内核开源、发行多元、社区自治。<strong>无边界者，生命力最强</strong>。</div>
            <div class="s35-tag s35-tag-vital">OPEN FRONTIER · 生命力强劲</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="s35-footer">
          <span>ECOSYSTEM TRIPTYCH</span>
          <span class="s35-footer-line"></span>
          <span>三势力 · 三范式 · 三命运</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 36
    visual: "蓝色像素地球与漂浮二进制数字——开源数字经济的全球图景"
    bullets:
      - "供应链"
      - "Internet 的架构设计：点对点分布式"
      - "散落在各处的开源项目及其共同体"
      - "关键节点与分发权"
      - "控制力与Contributor"
      - "嵌入到数字时代的各个地方：服务器到手机"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        padding: 60px 80px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
      }
      
      /* Binary rain background */
      .slide-page::before {
        content: "01101001 10110010 01010110 11010101 01110100 10101011 01001101 11100010 01101001 10110010 01010110 11010101 01110100 10101011 01001101 11100010";
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: -100px;
        right: -100px;
        bottom: 0;
        font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
        font-size: 14px;
        line-height: 28px;
        color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.06);
        word-break: break-all;
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        user-select: none;
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .slide-header {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        display: flex;
        align-items: baseline;
        justify-content: space-between;
        margin-bottom: 40px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
        padding-bottom: 20px;
      }
      
      .slide-header h2 {
        font-size: 28px;
        font-weight: 600;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        margin: 0;
      }
      
      .slide-header .page-num {
        font-size: 14px;
        color: var(--amber);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
      }
      
      .main-content {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr 380px;
        gap: 60px;
        align-items: start;
      }
      
      /* Left column - content */
      .content-left h1 {
        font-size: 34px;
        font-weight: 400;
        line-height: 1.3;
        margin: 0 0 8px 0;
        color: var(--cream);
      }
      
      .content-left .subtitle {
        font-size: 16px;
        color: var(--amber);
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 32px;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .bullet-list {
        list-style: none;
        padding: 0;
        margin: 0;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li {
        display: flex;
        align-items: flex-start;
        gap: 16px;
        padding: 12px 0;
        border-bottom: 1px dashed rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.15);
        font-size: 18px;
        line-height: 1.4;
      }
      
      .bullet-list li:last-child {
        border-bottom: none;
      }
      
      .bullet-num {
        font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--amber);
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.1);
        border: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
        padding: 4px 8px;
        border-radius: 3px;
        min-width: 36px;
        text-align: center;
        flex-shrink: 0;
        margin-top: 2px;
      }
      
      .bullet-text {
        color: var(--cream);
      }
      
      .bullet-text .em {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      /* Right column - pixel globe */
      .globe-container {
        position: relative;
        width: 380px;
        height: 380px;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
      }
      
      .globe {
        width: 260px;
        height: 260px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: radial-gradient(circle at 35% 35%, #2a5088 0%, var(--prussian) 40%, #0f2547 100%);
        position: relative;
        box-shadow: 0 0 60px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.15), inset -20px -20px 40px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
        border: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.2);
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      
      .globe::before {
        content: "";
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        bottom: 0;
        background: 
          repeating-linear-gradient(0deg, transparent 0, transparent 22px, rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.08) 22px, rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.08) 24px),
          repeating-linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0, transparent 22px, rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.08) 22px, rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.08) 24px);
        border-radius: 50%;
      }
      
      /* Pixel continents */
      .pixel-dot {
        position: absolute;
        width: 8px;
        height: 8px;
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.5);
      }
      
      /* Floating binary digits around globe */
      .float-bin {
        position: absolute;
        font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.5;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
      }
      
      .float-bin:nth-child(1) { top: 10px; left: 20px; }
      .float-bin:nth-child(2) { top: 30px; right: 10px; }
      .float-bin:nth-child(3) { top: 180px; left: -5px; }
      .float-bin:nth-child(4) { top: 200px; right: 0px; }
      .float-bin:nth-child(5) { bottom: 20px; left: 30px; }
      .float-bin:nth-child(6) { bottom: 40px; right: 20px; }
      .float-bin:nth-child(7) { top: 120px; left: 0px; }
      .float-bin:nth-child(8) { top: 100px; right: -5px; }
      
      /* Network connection lines via box-shadow dots */
      .net-ring {
        position: absolute;
        width: 320px;
        height: 320px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px dashed rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.2);
        top: 50%;
        left: 50%;
        transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
        animation: none;
      }
      
      .net-ring-2 {
        position: absolute;
        width: 360px;
        height: 360px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px dotted rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.12);
        top: 50%;
        left: 50%;
        transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
      }
      
      .globe-caption {
        text-align: center;
        margin-top: 16px;
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      .globe-caption .accent {
        color: var(--amber);
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="slide-header">
        <h2>开源数字经济 · 全球图景</h2>
        <span class="page-num">36 / 52</span>
      </div>
      
      <div class="main-content">
        <div class="content-left">
          <h1>从点对点网络到全球基础设施</h1>
          <div class="subtitle">Supply Chain · Architecture · Governance</div>
          <ul class="bullet-list">
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">01</span>
              <span class="bullet-text"><span class="em">供应链</span>——开源作为数字时代的原材料流通体系</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">02</span>
              <span class="bullet-text">Internet 的<span class="em">架构设计</span>：点对点分布式</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">03</span>
              <span class="bullet-text">散落在各处的<span class="em">开源项目</span>及其共同体</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">04</span>
              <span class="bullet-text"><span class="em">关键节点</span>与分发权——谁掌握管道的入口</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">05</span>
              <span class="bullet-text"><span class="em">控制力</span>与 Contributor 的博弈关系</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">06</span>
              <span class="bullet-text">嵌入到数字时代的各个地方：<span class="em">服务器 → 手机</span></span>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      
        <div class="globe-container">

  - num: 37
    visual: "暗蓝背景上的二进制数字雨——开源软件价值的矩阵化呈现"
    bullets:
      - "超越产业"
      - "开放"
      - "放弃排他权，拥抱容他权"
      - "参与影响而不是试图控制"
      - "理解和尊重开发者本社"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
      
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        justify-content: center;
        padding: 60px 90px;
      }
      
      /* Binary rain background layers */
      .rain {
        position: absolute;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
        font-size: 13px;
        line-height: 1.6;
        color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.07);
        pointer-events: none;
        user-select: none;
        overflow: hidden;
        z-index: 0;
      }
      
      .rain.col1 { left: 2%; }
      .rain.col2 { left: 14%; }
      .rain.col3 { left: 28%; }
      .rain.col4 { left: 42%; }
      .rain.col5 { left: 56%; }
      .rain.col6 { left: 70%; }
      .rain.col7 { left: 84%; }
      
      .rain.col1 { opacity: 0.6; }
      .rain.col2 { opacity: 0.4; }
      .rain.col3 { opacity: 0.7; }
      .rain.col4 { opacity: 0.3; }
      .rain.col5 { opacity: 0.5; }
      .rain.col6 { opacity: 0.8; }
      .rain.col7 { opacity: 0.35; }
      
      .rain.col3 { font-size: 14px; color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.10); }
      .rain.col6 { font-size: 15px; color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.12); }
      
      /* Subtle vertical accent lines */
      .left-rule {
        position: absolute;
        left: 60px;
        top: 120px;
        bottom: 120px;
        width: 1px;
        background: linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent, rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3), transparent);
        z-index: 1;
      }
      
      /* Main content */
      .content {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 2;
        max-width: 880px;
      }
      
      .kicker {
        font-size: 14px;
        letter-spacing: 0.35em;
        color: var(--amber);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .slide-num {
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 0.2em;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-align: right;
        position: absolute;
        top: 40px;
        right: 90px;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .main-title {
        font-size: 46px;
        font-weight: 400;
        color: var(--cream);
        line-height: 1.2;
        margin-bottom: 4px;
        letter-spacing: 0.02em;
      }
      
      .main-title .em-amber {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      .title-underline {
        width: 80px;
        height: 2px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin: 22px 0 36px 0;
      }
      
      .values {
        list-style: none;
        margin: 0;
        padding: 0;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 18px;
      }
      
      .values li {
        display: flex;
        align-items: baseline;
        gap: 22px;
        padding-left: 4px;
      }
      
      .values .idx {
        font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--amber);
        min-width: 44px;
        letter-spacing: 0.1em;
        padding-top: 4px;
        opacity: 0.85;
      }
      
      .values .text {
        font-size: 22px;
        color: var(--cream);
        line-height: 1.5;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      .values .text .strong {
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      
      .values .text .dim {
        color: var(--copper);
      }
      
      /* Bottom decorative bar */
      .bottom-deco {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 40px;
        left: 90px;
        right: 90px;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
        z-index: 2;
      }
      
      .bottom-deco .dash {
        width: 40px;
        height: 1px;
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4);
      }
      
      .bottom-deco .label {
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 0.4em;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      </style>

  - num: 38
    visual: "旧皮革工作手套叠放——开源工作者的双手：劳动与守护"
    bullets:
      - "12次讲座的思路和目标"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page{
        --prussian:#1B3B6B;
        --cream:#F5F0E8;
        --copper:#8B7355;
        --amber:#D4A853;
        --muted:#555555;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        padding: 70px 90px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
      }
      
      /* Subtle leather-grain texture overlay */
      .slide-page::before{
        content:"";
        position:absolute;
        top:0;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;
        background:
          radial-gradient(ellipse at 20% 30%, rgba(212,168,83,0.04) 0%, transparent 50%),
          radial-gradient(ellipse at 80% 70%, rgba(139,115,85,0.05) 0%, transparent 50%);
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      
      /* Stacked glove metaphors — rounded rectangles in warm tones */
      .glove-stack{
        position:absolute;
        right: 80px;
        bottom: 90px;
        width: 320px;
        height: 280px;
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      
      .glove{
        position:absolute;
        border-radius: 24px 24px 18px 18px;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.2);
      }
      
      .glove-1{
        width: 200px;
        height: 220px;
        right: 0;
        bottom: 0;
        background: rgba(139,115,85,0.18);
        border-color: rgba(212,168,83,0.35);
        border-radius: 60px 30px 24px 24px;
        box-shadow: inset 0 2px 0 rgba(212,168,83,0.08);
      }
      
      .glove-2{
        width: 200px;
        height: 220px;
        right: 60px;
        bottom: 40px;
        background: rgba(139,115,85,0.12);
        border-color: rgba(212,168,83,0.22);
        border-radius: 60px 30px 24px 24px;
        transform: rotate(-6deg);
      }
      
      .glove-3{
        width: 200px;
        height: 220px;
        right: 30px;
        bottom: 80px;
        background: rgba(139,115,85,0.07);
        border-color: rgba(212,168,83,0.14);
        border-radius: 60px 30px 24px 24px;
        transform: rotate(4deg);
      }
      
      /* Finger suggestion on top glove */
      .glove-1::after{
        content:"";
        position:absolute;
        top: 10px;
        left: 50px;
        width: 18px;
        height: 60px;
        border-radius: 12px;
        background: rgba(212,168,83,0.06);
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.15);
        box-shadow:
          30px 0 0 -1px rgba(212,168,83,0.15),
          30px 0 0 0 rgba(212,168,83,0.04),
          60px 0 0 -1px rgba(212,168,83,0.15),
          60px 0 0 0 rgba(212,168,83,0.04),
          90px 0 0 -1px rgba(212,168,83,0.15),
          90px 0 0 0 rgba(212,168,83,0.04);
      }
      
      .glove-1::before{
        content:"";
        position:absolute;
        top: 30px;
        left: 70px;
        width: 100px;
        height: 4px;
        background: rgba(212,168,83,0.12);
      }
      
      /* Page number */
      .page-num{
        position:absolute;
        top: 40px;
        right: 90px;
        font-size: 14px;
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        color: var(--copper);
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      /* Section label */
      .section-label{
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 6px;
        color: var(--copper);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 30px;
        font-weight: 400;
      }
      
      /* Horizontal rule accent */
      .accent-line{
        width: 80px;
        height: 2px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin-bottom: 40px;
      }
      
      /* Main heading */
      .main-title{
        font-size: 52px;
        font-weight: 400;
        line-height: 1.2;
        color: var(--cream);
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        max-width: 640px;
      }
      
      .main-title .num{
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 600;
        font-style: italic;
      }
      
      /* Subtitle */
      .subtitle{
        font-size: 18px;
        color: var(--copper);
        margin-top: 30px;
        line-height: 1.8;
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
        max-width: 560px;
      }
      
      /* Left vertical bar */
      .left-bar{
        position:absolute;
        left: 0;
        top: 120px;
        width: 3px;
        height: 220px;
        background: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.6;
      }
      
      /* Decorative stitched line — like glove stitching */
      .stitch{
        position:absolute;
        left: 90px;
        bottom: 55px;
        display:flex;
        gap: 8px;
        align-items:center;
      }
      
      .stitch .dash{
        width: 12px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--copper);
        opacity: 0.4;
      }
      
      .stitch .dot{
        width: 3px;
        height: 3px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        background: var(--amber);
        opacity: 0.3;
      }
      
      /* Meta info bottom right */
      .meta{
        position:absolute;
        right: 90px;
        bottom: 40px;
        font-size: 11px;
        letter-spacing: 4px;
        color: var(--copper);
        opacity: 0.5;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="page-num">38 / 52</div>
      
      <div class="left-bar"></div>
      
      <div class="section-label">Coda · Summary</div>
      <div class="accent-line"></div>
      
      <h1 class="main-title">
        <span class="num">十二次</span>讲座的<br>思路与目标
      </h1>
      
      <p class="subtitle">
        开源工作者的双手——<br>
        劳动与守护，编织知识的网络
      </p>
      
      <div class="glove-stack">
        <div class="glove glove-3"></div>
        <div class="glove glove-2"></div>
        <div class="glove glove-1"></div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="stitch">
        <span class="dash"></span>
        <span class="dot"></span>
        <span class="dash"></span>
        <span class="dot"></span>
        <span class="dash"></span>
        <span class="dot"></span>
        <span class="dash"></span>
        <span class="dot"></span>
        <span class="dash"></span>
        <span class="dot"></span>
      </div>
      
      <div class="meta">CHAPTER Ⅻ · RECAPITULATION</div>

  - num: 39
    visual: "秋叶飘舞蓝天——开源劳动的收获与季节更替"
    bullets:

      - "开源历史概述"
      - "产业视角与文明"
      - "12次讲座的安排与估计"
    designed: |
      <style>
          .slide-page {
            font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
            background: var(--prussian);
            color: var(--cream);
            width: 1280px;
            height: 720px;
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
            padding: 60px 80px;
            box-sizing: border-box;
          }
      
          .slide-page .leaf {
            position: absolute;
            border-radius: 80% 0 80% 0;
            opacity: 0.12;
            pointer-events: none;
          }
      
          .slide-page .leaf-1 {
            width: 60px;
            height: 60px;
            background: var(--amber);
            top: 60px;
            left: 120px;
            transform: rotate(-30deg);
            opacity: 0.08;
          }
      
          .slide-page .leaf-2 {
            width: 40px;
            height: 40px;
            background: var(--copper);
            top: 180px;
            left: 80px;
            transform: rotate(45deg);
            opacity: 0.07;
          }
      
          .slide-page .leaf-3 {
            width: 70px;
            height: 70px;
            background: var(--amber);
            top: 320px;
            right: 60px;
            transform: rotate(15deg);
            opacity: 0.08;
          }
      
          .slide-page .leaf-4 {
            width: 45px;
            height: 45px;
            background: var(--copper);
            top: 500px;
            right: 140px;
            transform: rotate(-60deg);
            opacity: 0.07;
          }
      
          .slide-page .leaf-5 {
            width: 55px;
            height: 55px;
            background: var(--amber);
            top: 600px;
            left: 200px;
            transform: rotate(20deg);
            opacity: 0.06;
          }
      
          .slide-page .leaf-6 {
            width: 35px;
            height: 35px;
            background: var(--copper);
            top: 420px;
            left: 100px;
            transform: rotate(-10deg);
            opacity: 0.06;
          }
      
          .slide-page .header {
            display: flex;
            align-items: baseline;
            justify-content: space-between;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
          }
      
          .slide-page .slide-number {
            font-size: 14px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 3px;
          }
      
          .slide-page .section-tag {
            font-size: 12px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 4px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
          }
      
          .slide-page h1 {
            font-size: 44px;
            font-weight: 400;
            color: var(--cream);
            margin: 8px 0 0 0;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
          }
      
          .slide-page .subtitle {
            font-size: 16px;
            color: var(--copper);
            margin-top: 12px;
            font-style: italic;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
          }
      
          .slide-page .divider {
            width: 80px;
            height: 1px;
            background: var(--amber);
            margin: 28px 0 30px 0;
            opacity: 0.7;
          }
      
          .slide-page .agenda {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
            column-gap: 60px;
            row-gap: 18px;
            max-width: 900px;
          }
      
          .slide-page .agenda-item {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            padding: 10px 0;
            border-left: 2px solid var(--copper);
            padding-left: 20px;
            transition: none;
          }
      
          .slide-page .agenda-item .num {
            font-size: 22px;
            color: var(--amber);
            font-weight: 400;
            min-width: 36px;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
          }
      
          .slide-page .agenda-item .label {
            font-size: 17px;
            color: var(--cream);
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
            line-height: 1.4;
          }
      
          .slide-page .agenda-item .dash {
            flex: 1;
            height: 1px;
            background: var(--copper);
            opacity: 0.25;
            margin-left: 12px;
          }
      
          .slide-page .footer-bar {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 30px;
            left: 80px;
            right: 80px;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            font-size: 11px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 3px;
            opacity: 0.6;
          }
      
          .slide-page .footer-bar .line {
            flex: 1;

    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page{
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        background:var(--prussian);
        color:var(--cream);
        width:1280px;height:720px;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
        box-sizing:border-box;
      }
      
      /* 山间公路的弯曲光轨——用倾斜的渐变长条模拟尾灯拉出的光带 */
      .road-trail{
        position:absolute;
        height:2px;
        opacity:0.35;
        filter:blur(0.5px);
      }
      .trail-1{
        top:110px;left:-40px;width:1360px;
        transform:rotate(3deg);
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, var(--amber) 30%, var(--copper) 60%, transparent 100%);
        box-shadow:0 0 12px var(--amber), 0 0 24px var(--amber);
      }
      .trail-2{
        top:610px;left:-40px;width:1360px;
        transform:rotate(-2.5deg);
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, var(--cream) 35%, var(--amber) 70%, transparent 100%);
        box-shadow:0 0 8px var(--cream), 0 0 18px var(--amber);
        opacity:0.28;
      }
      .trail-3{
        top:360px;left:-40px;width:1360px;
        transform:rotate(1.5deg);
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0%, var(--copper) 50%, transparent 100%);
        opacity:0.2;
      }
      
      /* 光点——尾灯的光斑 */
      .glow-dot{
        position:absolute;
        width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;
        background:var(--amber);
        box-shadow:0 0 10px var(--amber), 0 0 20px var(--amber), 0 0 40px rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
      }
      .glow-dot.white{
        background:var(--cream);
        box-shadow:0 0 8px var(--cream), 0 0 18px var(--amber);
      }
      .glow-dot.d1{top:112px;left:180px;}
      .glow-dot.d2{top:113px;left:420px;}
      .glow-dot.d3{top:111px;left:760px;}
      .glow-dot.d4{top:112px;left:1020px;}
      .glow-dot.d5{top:113px;left:1180px;}
      .glow-dot.d6{top:611px;left:300px;}
      .glow-dot.d7{top:610px;left:680px;}
      .glow-dot.d8{top:611px;left:950px;}
      .glow-dot.w1{top:112px;left:280px;}
      .glow-dot.w2{top:112px;left:560px;}
      .glow-dot.w3{top:112px;left:900px;}
      
      /* 主内容区 */
      .content{
        position:relative;
        z-index:10;
        padding:80px 90px 70px 90px;
        height:100%;
        box-sizing:border-box;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
      }
      
      .top-label{
        font-size:13px;
        letter-spacing:6px;
        text-transform:uppercase;
        color:var(--copper);
        margin-bottom:14px;
      }
      
      .main-title{
        font-size:34px;
        font-weight:400;
        color:var(--cream);
        letter-spacing:1px;
        margin:0 0 8px 0;
        line-height:1.3;
      }
      .main-title em{
        font-style:italic;
        color:var(--amber);
      }
      
      .subtitle-line{
        font-size:15px;
        color:var(--copper);
        font-style:italic;
        margin-bottom:38px;
        letter-spacing:0.5px;
      }
      
      /* 章节列表——沿公路的里程碑 */
      .chapter-list{
        list-style:none;
        margin:0;
        padding:0;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        gap:18px;
      }
      .chapter-item{
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        gap:28px;
        padding:12px 24px;
        border-left:2px solid var(--copper);
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(139,115,85,0.08) 0%, transparent 70%);
        transition:none;
      }
      .chapter-item:last-child{
        border-left-color:var(--amber);
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(212,168,83,0.12) 0%, transparent 70%);
      }
      .chapter-num{
        font-size:28px;
        font-weight:400;
        color:var(--amber);
        width:52px;
        flex-shrink:0;
        font-feature-settings:"lnum";
        letter-spacing:1px;
      }
      .chapter-text{
        font-size:22px;
        color:var(--cream);
        font-weight:400;
        line-height:1.4;
      }
      .chapter-text.final{
        color:var(--amber);
        font-style:italic;
      }
      .chapter-arrow{
        margin-left:auto;
        color:var(--copper);
        font-size:18px;
        opacity:0.5;
      }
      
      /* 底部装饰——道路虚线 */
      .road-dash{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:32px;
        left:90px;
        right:90px;
        height:1px;
        background-image:linear-gradient(90deg, var(--copper) 0, var(--copper) 30px, transparent 30px, transparent 60px);
        background-size:60px 1px;
        opacity:0.3;
      }
      
      /* 右上角章节指示 */
      .page-indicator{
        position:absolute;
        top:36px;
        right:40px;
        font-size:12px;
        letter-spacing:3px;
        color:var(--copper);
        opacity:0.7;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="road-trail trail-1"></div>
      <div class="road-trail trail-2"></div>

    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-page {
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
        padding: 60px 80px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
      }
      
      /* Golden network arcs background */
      .arc {
        position: absolute;
        border-radius: 50%;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.12);
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      .arc-1 { width: 900px; height: 900px; right: -300px; top: -200px; border-width: 1px; }
      .arc-2 { width: 700px; height: 700px; right: -200px; top: -100px; border-color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.18); }
      .arc-3 { width: 500px; height: 500px; right: -100px; top: 0px; border-color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.22); }
      .arc-4 { width: 300px; height: 300px; right: 0px; top: 100px; border-color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.28); }
      .arc-5 { width: 1100px; height: 1100px; right: -400px; top: -300px; border-color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.08); }
      
      /* Inner arc */
      .arc-inner {
        position: absolute;
        width: 300px;
        height: 300px;
        border-radius: 50%;
        right: 0px;
        top: 100px;
        border: 1px dashed rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.2);
      }
      
      /* Network nodes */
      .node {
        position: absolute;
        width: 6px;
        height: 6px;
        background: var(--amber);
        border-radius: 50%;
        box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.6);
      }
      .node-1 { right: 200px; top: 120px; }
      .node-2 { right: 80px; top: 280px; }
      .node-3 { right: 280px; top: 340px; }
      .node-4 { right: 150px; top: 380px; }
      .node-5 { right: 320px; top: 180px; }
      .node-6 { right: 60px; top: 200px; }
      
      /* Thin connecting lines */
      .line {
        position: absolute;
        height: 1px;
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.25);
        transform-origin: left center;
      }
      .line-1 { right: 200px; top: 120px; width: 120px; transform: rotate(55deg); }
      .line-2 { right: 280px; top: 180px; width: 100px; transform: rotate(70deg); }
      .line-3 { right: 150px; top: 380px; width: 90px; transform: rotate(-30deg); }
      .line-4 { right: 80px; top: 280px; width: 140px; transform: rotate(-40deg); }
      
      /* Main layout */
      .main-content {
        position: relative;
        z-index: 10;
        height: 100%;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
      }
      
      .section-header {
        display: flex;
        align-items: baseline;
        gap: 24px;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
      }
      
      .section-num {
        font-size: 14px;
        letter-spacing: 6px;
        color: var(--amber);
        text-transform: uppercase;
        font-weight: 600;
      }
      
      .section-rule {
        width: 60px;
        height: 1px;
        background: var(--amber);
        display: inline-block;
        align-self: center;
      }
      
      .section-title {
        font-size: 42px;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--cream);
        margin: 18px 0 6px 0;
        letter-spacing: 2px;
      }
      
      .section-subtitle {
        font-size: 17px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 3px;
        margin-bottom: 36px;
      }
      
      .title-underline {
        width: 100px;
        height: 2px;
        background: var(--amber);
        margin-bottom: 34px;
      }
      
      /* Agenda list */
      .agenda {
        list-style: none;
        padding: 0;
        margin: 0;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 16px;
        max-width: 780px;
      }
      
      .agenda-item {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 22px;
        padding: 10px 0;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.1);
      }
      
      .agenda-num {
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 600;
        min-width: 36px;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        border-right: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
        padding-right: 16px;
      }
      
      .agenda-text {
        font-size: 17px;
        color: var(--cream);
        line-height: 1.5;
      }
      
      .agenda-text em {
        font-style: italic;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 500;
      }
      
      /* Footer */
      .footer {
        position: absolute;
        bottom: 30px;
        left: 80px;
        right: 80px;
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        z-index: 10;
      }
      
      .footer-line {
        flex: 1;
        height: 1px;
        background: rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.3);
        margin: 0 24px;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="arc arc-1"></div>
      <div class="arc arc-2"></div>
      <div class="arc arc-3"></div>
      <div class="arc arc-4"></div>
      <div class="arc arc-5"></div>
      <div class="arc-inner"></div>
      
      <div class="node node-1"></div>
      <div class="node node-2"></div>
      <div class="node node-3"></div>
      <div class="node node-4"></div>
      <div class="node node-5"></div>
      <div class="node node-6"></div>
      
      <div class="line line-1"></div>
      <div class="line line-2"></div>
      <div class="line line-3"></div>
      <div class="line line-4"></div>
      
      <div class="main-content">
        <div class="section-header">
          <span class="section-num">CHAPTER · 02</span>
          <span class="section-rule"></span>
          <span class="section-num">第二期</span>
        </div>
      
        <h1 class="section-title">软件开发劳动力市场</h1>
        <div class="section-subtitle">THE OPEN-SOURCE LABOR MARKET</div>
        <div class="title-underline"></div>
      
        <ul class="agenda">
          <li class="agenda-item">
            <span class="agenda-num">01</span>
            <span class="agenda-text">劳动力市场：开源世界的<em>供需关系</em></span>
          </li>
          <li class="agenda-item">
            <span class="agenda-num">02</span>
            <span class="agenda-text">是什么激励着开源开发者</span>
          </li>
          <li class="agenda-item">
            <span class="agenda-num">03</span>
            <span class="agenda-text">利他主义是<em>什么样的存在</em></span>
          </li>
          <li class="agenda-item">
            <span class="agenda-num">04</span>
            <span class="agenda-text">业余爱好者的<em>富余劳动力</em>？</span>
          </li>
          <li class="agenda-item">
            <span class="agenda-num">05</span>

          </li>
          <li class="agenda-item">
            <span class="agenda-num">06</span>

          </li>

      <div class="footer">
        <span>OPEN SOURCE</span>

        <span>41 / 52</span>

    visual: "多彩人头连接成网络——开源社区的社交属性与多元协作"
    bullets:
