      - "知识财产正当性解释"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        --prussian:#1B3B6B;
        --cream:#F5F0E8;
        --copper:#8B7355;
        --amber:#D4A853;
        --muted:#555555;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        width:100%;
        height:100%;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
      }
      
      .net-wrap{
        position:absolute;
        top:0;
        right:0;
        width:52%;
        height:100%;
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      
      /* Connected network lines */
      .net-wrap .line{
        position:absolute;
        background: var(--amber);
        opacity:0.22;
        height:1px;
        transform-origin:left center;
      }
      
      .net-wrap .line.l1{ top:22%; left:12%; width:340px; transform:rotate(28deg); }
      .net-wrap .line.l2{ top:35%; left:8%; width:280px; transform:rotate(-18deg); }
      .net-wrap .line.l3{ top:48%; left:15%; width:320px; transform:rotate(12deg); }
      .net-wrap .line.l4{ top:62%; left:10%; width:360px; transform:rotate(-22deg); }
      .net-wrap .line.l5{ top:74%; left:14%; width:290px; transform:rotate(35deg); }
      .net-wrap .line.l6{ top:30%; left:32%; width:200px; transform:rotate(-42deg); }
      .net-wrap .line.l7{ top:58%; left:30%; width:220px; transform:rotate(20deg); }
      .net-wrap .line.l8{ top:42%; left:42%; width:180px; transform:rotate(68deg); }
      
      /* Network nodes (head-like circles) */
      .net-wrap .node{
        position:absolute;
        border-radius:50%;
        border:2px solid var(--amber);
      }
      
      .net-wrap .node.s1{ width:36px; height:36px; top:14%; left:8%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.18); }
      .net-wrap .node.s2{ width:28px; height:28px; top:26%; left:22%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.12); border-color:var(--cream); border-opacity:0.6; }
      .net-wrap .node.s3{ width:44px; height:44px; top:34%; left:38%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.22); box-shadow:0 0 18px rgba(212,168,83,0.3); }
      .net-wrap .node.s4{ width:32px; height:32px; top:52%; left:14%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.14); }
      .net-wrap .node.s5{ width:52px; height:52px; top:58%; left:40%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.28); box-shadow:0 0 24px rgba(212,168,83,0.35); }
      .net-wrap .node.s6{ width:26px; height:26px; top:70%; left:26%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.10); border-color:var(--cream); }
      .net-wrap .node.s7{ width:38px; height:38px; top:48%; left:56%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.16); }
      .net-wrap .node.s8{ width:30px; height:30px; top:18%; left:46%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.12); border-color:var(--cream); border-opacity:0.5; }
      .net-wrap .node.s9{ width:22px; height:22px; top:82%; left:44%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.08); }
      .net-wrap .node.s10{ width:34px; height:34px; top:76%; left:10%; background:rgba(212,168,83,0.16); }
      
      /* Small accent circles (inner dot) to suggest eyes/features */
      .net-wrap .node::after{
        content:'';
        position:absolute;
        top:50%;
        left:50%;
        width:6px;
        height:6px;
        border-radius:50%;
        background:var(--amber);
        transform:translate(-50%,-50%);
        opacity:0.5;
      }
      
      /* Content area */
      .content{
        position:relative;
        z-index:2;
        padding: 90px 80px 70px 80px;
        max-width:58%;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
        justify-content:center;
        height:100%;
      }
      
      .slide-num{
        font-size:13px;
        letter-spacing:6px;
        color: var(--copper);
        margin-bottom:50px;
        font-weight:400;
      }
      
      .section-tag{
        font-size:14px;
        letter-spacing:4px;
        color: var(--amber);
        margin-bottom:22px;
        font-weight:400;
      }
      
      .main-title{
        font-size:38px;
        font-weight:600;
        line-height:1.35;
        color: var(--cream);
        margin-bottom:26px;
        letter-spacing:1px;
      }
      
      .main-title .accent{
        color: var(--amber);
      }
      
      .sub-title{
        font-size:20px;
        font-weight:400;
        color: var(--cream);
        opacity:0.82;
        line-height:1.5;
        padding-left:18px;
        border-left:2px solid var(--amber);
        margin-bottom:50px;
      }
      
      .meta-row{
        display:flex;

      .meta-row .dot{

      .meta-row .divider{

      /* Bottom footer line */
      .footer-bar{

      .footer-bar .progress{

      }
      
      .footer-bar .progress-fill{

      /* Left decorative vertical line */
      .left-accent{

      .left-accent::before{

    designed: |
      <style>
        .slide-inner {
          --prussian: #1B3B6B;
          --cream: #F5F0E8;
          --copper: #8B7355;
          --amber: #D4A853;
          --muted: #555555;
          font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
          background: var(--prussian);
          color: var(--cream);
          width: 100%;
          height: 100%;
          box-sizing: border-box;
          position: relative;
          overflow: hidden;
          display: flex;
          padding: 72px 96px;
        }
      
        /* Mountain silhouette background */
        .slide-inner::before {
          content: '';
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 0;
          left: 0;
          width: 0;
          height: 0;
          border-left: 320px solid transparent;
          border-right: 320px solid transparent;
          border-bottom: 200px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.06);
          left: -100px;
        }
      
        .slide-inner::after {
          content: '';
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 0;
          right: 0;
          width: 0;
          height: 0;
          border-left: 420px solid transparent;
          border-right: 280px solid transparent;
          border-bottom: 280px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.08);
          right: -80px;
        }
      
        /* Eagle symbol - geometric */
        .eagle-mark {
          position: absolute;
          top: 56px;
          right: 96px;
          width: 52px;
          height: 52px;
          border: 2px solid var(--amber);
          transform: rotate(45deg);
          opacity: 0.7;
        }
        .eagle-mark::before {
          content: '';
          position: absolute;
          top: 10px;
          left: 10px;
          width: 22px;
          height: 22px;
          border: 1px solid var(--amber);
          transform: rotate(0deg);
        }
      
        .left-col {
          flex: 0 0 380px;
          display: flex;
          flex-direction: column;
          justify-content: flex-start;
          padding-right: 48px;
          border-right: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.25);
          position: relative;
          z-index: 2;
        }
      
        .episode-tag {
          font-size: 14px;
          letter-spacing: 4px;
          text-transform: uppercase;
          color: var(--amber);
          margin-bottom: 12px;
        }
      
        .episode-num {
          font-size: 96px;
          font-weight: 400;
          color: var(--amber);
          line-height: 1;
          margin-bottom: 24px;
        }
      
        .episode-num sup {
          font-size: 28px;
          color: var(--copper);
          vertical-align: top;
          margin-left: 4px;
          letter-spacing: 2px;
        }
      
        .main-title {
          font-size: 24px;
          font-weight: 400;
          line-height: 1.5;
          color: var(--cream);
          margin-bottom: 36px;
        }
      
        .main-title em {
          font-style: italic;
          color: var(--amber);
        }
      
        .mountain-peak {
          width: 0;
          height: 0;
          border-left: 40px solid transparent;
          border-right: 40px solid transparent;
          border-bottom: 60px solid var(--amber);
          margin-bottom: 20px;
          opacity: 0.6;
        }
      
        .mountain-sub {
          font-size: 13px;
          color: var(--copper);
          letter-spacing: 2px;
          text-align: left;
          line-height: 1.6;
        }
      
        .right-col {
          flex: 1;
          display: flex;
          flex-direction: column;
          justify-content: center;
          padding-left: 64px;
          position: relative;
          z-index: 2;
        }
      
        .section-label {
          font-size: 12px;
          letter-spacing: 3px;
          text-transform: uppercase;
          color: var(--copper);
          margin-bottom: 28px;
          display: flex;
          align-items: center;
          gap: 16px;
        }
      
        .section-label::before {
          content: '';
          display: inline-block;
          width: 48px;
          height: 1px;
          background: var(--copper);
        }
      
        .topic-list {
          list-style: none;
          margin: 0;
          padding: 0;
        }
      
        .topic-list li {
          display: flex;
          align-items: baseline;
          gap: 24px;
          padding: 18px 0;
          border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(245,240,232,0.08);
          transition: none;
        }
      
        .topic-list li:last-child {
          border-bottom: none;
        }
      
        .topic-num {
          font-size: 14px;
          color: var(--amber);
          font-weight: 400;
          letter-spacing: 1px;
          min-width: 36px;
          flex-shrink: 0;
        }
      
        .topic-text {
          font-size: 19px;
          color: var(--cream);
          line-height: 1.5;
          flex: 1;
        }
      
        .topic-text strong {
          color: var(--amber);
          font-weight: 400;
        }
      
        .footer-bar {
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 40px;
          left: 96px;
          right: 96px;
          display: flex;
          justify-content: space-between;
          align-items: center;
          font-size: 12px;
          letter-spacing: 2px;
          color: var(--copper);
          z-index: 2;
        }
      
        .footer-bar .page-num {
          color: var(--amber);
        }
      
        .horizon-line {
          position: absolute;
          bottom: 180px;
          left: 0;
          right: 0;
          height: 1px;
          background: linear-gradient(to right, transparent, rgba(212,168,83,0.2), transparent);
          z-index: 1;
        }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="slide-inner">
        <div class="eagle-mark"></div>
      
        <div class="left-col">
          <div class="episode-tag">Episode</div>
          <div class="episode-num">04<sup>TH</sup></div>
          <div class="main-title">数字时代的<br><em>知识财产法</em>演变<br>与开源许可</div>
          <div class="mountain-peak"></div>
          <div class="mountain-sub">视野超越群山<br>Vision Beyond the Peaks</div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="right-col">
          <div class="section-label">本期议题 · Topics</div>
          <ul class="topic-list">
            <li>
              <span class="topic-num">I.</span>
              <span class="topic-text"><strong>所有权的终结</strong></span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="topic-num">II.</span>
              <span class="topic-text"><strong>许可的演化</strong>：软件访问的控制</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="topic-num">III.</span>
              <span class="topic-text"><strong>开源的让渡</strong></span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="topic-num">IV.</span>
              <span class="topic-text">如何对抗人的<strong>"搭便车"</strong>天性</span>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      
        <div class="horizon-line"></div>
      
        <div class="footer-bar">
          <span>The End of Ownership · The Rise of Licensing</span>
          <span class="page-num">43 / 52</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 44
    visual: "泛黄书页堆叠成几何——劳动与报酬：时间的沉淀与积累"
    bullets:
      - "第6期：信息规则与网络经济"
      - "信息想要免费，但是免费又是昂贵的"
      - "代码数字化：书籍、音乐、电影保持同步"
      - "代码塑造了新的空间，其本身却想要免费"
      - "可恨的先知们"
    designed: |
      <style>
          .slide-inner {
            --prussian: #1B3B6B;
            --cream: #F5F0E8;
            --copper: #8B7355;
            --amber: #D4A853;
            --muted: #555555;
      
            font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
            width: 1280px;
            height: 720px;
            position: relative;
            background: var(--prussian);
            overflow: hidden;
            color: var(--cream);
          }
      
          /* Layered page stack — right side geometric composition */
          .page-stack {
            position: absolute;
            right: -60px;
            top: 50%;
            transform: translateY(-50%) rotate(6deg);
            width: 520px;
            height: 560px;
            pointer-events: none;
          }
      
          .page-stack .sheet {
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #E8DCC0 0%, #D4C4A0 40%, #C9B48A 100%);
            border-radius: 2px;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 18px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);
          }
      
          .page-stack .sheet::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 40%;
            width: 1px;
            height: 100%;
            background: rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.25);
          }
      
          .page-stack .sheet::after {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 20px;
            left: 40px;
            right: 50px;
            height: 1px;
            background: rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2);
            box-shadow: 0 28px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2),
                        0 56px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2),
                        0 84px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2),
                        0 112px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2),
                        0 140px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2),
                        0 168px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2),
                        0 196px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2),
                        0 224px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2),
                        0 252px 0 rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.2);
          }
      
          .page-stack .sheet:nth-child(1) { transform: rotate(-4deg); opacity: 0.35; }
          .page-stack .sheet:nth-child(2) { transform: rotate(-1deg); opacity: 0.5; left: -20px; }
          .page-stack .sheet:nth-child(3) { transform: rotate(3deg); opacity: 0.65; left: -10px; }
          .page-stack .sheet:nth-child(4) { transform: rotate(0deg); opacity: 0.85; left: -5px; }
          .page-stack .sheet:nth-child(5) { transform: rotate(2deg); opacity: 1; }
      
          /* Faint corner detail on top sheet */
          .page-stack .sheet:nth-child(5) .corner-mark {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 30px;
            right: 35px;
            width: 24px;
            height: 24px;
            border-right: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.4);
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.4);
          }
      
          /* Left content area */
          .content {
            position: absolute;
            left: 80px;
            top: 0;
            width: 620px;
            height: 100%;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: center;
            z-index: 2;
          }
      
          .header-row {
            display: flex;
            align-items: baseline;
            gap: 16px;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
          }
      
          .page-label {
            font-size: 13px;
            letter-spacing: 3px;
            color: var(--amber);
            text-transform: uppercase;
            font-weight: 400;
          }
      
          .rule {
            flex: 1;
            height: 1px;
            background: linear-gradient(to right, var(--amber), transparent);
            opacity: 0.6;
          }
      
          .slide-title {
            font-size: 38px;
            font-weight: 400;
            color: var(--cream);
            margin: 0 0 8px 0;
            line-height: 1.25;
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
          }
      
          .slide-title .accent {
            color: var(--amber);
          }
      
          .subtitle {
            font-size: 14px;
            font-style: italic;
            color: var(--copper);
            margin-bottom: 36px;
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
          }
      
          .bullet-list {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
          }
      
          .bullet-list li {
            display: flex;
            align-items: flex-start;
            gap: 18px;
            padding: 12px 0;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.12);
            font-size: 16px;
            line-height: 1.55;
            color: var(--cream);
          }
      
          .bullet-list li:last-child {
            border-bottom: none;
          }
      
          .bullet-num {
            font-size: 13px;
            color: var(--amber);
            font-weight: 400;
            min-width: 28px;
            padding-top: 2px;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
          }
      
          .bullet-text {
            flex: 1;
          }
      
          .bullet-text .hl {
            color: var(--amber);
            font-style: italic;
          }
      
          /* Decorative elements */
          .corner-tl {
            position: absolute;
            top: 30px;
            left: 30px;
            width: 36px;
            height: 36px;
            border-top: 1px solid var(--amber);
            border-left: 1px solid var(--amber);
            opacity: 0.5;
          }
      
          .footer-info {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 30px;
            left: 80px;
            right: 80px;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            font-size: 11px;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            color: var(--copper);
            opacity: 0.7;
          }
      
          .footer-info .dot {
            display: inline-block;
            width: 4px;
            height: 4px;
            background: var(--amber);
            border-radius: 50%;
            margin: 0 10px;
            vertical-align: middle;
          }
      
          .chapter-tag {
            position: absolute;
            left: 80px;
            bottom: 70px;
            font-size: 11px;
            letter-spacing: 3px;
            color: var(--copper);
            opacity: 0.5;
            text-transform: uppercase;
          }
        </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
        
      
        <div class="corner-tl"></div>
      
        <div class="page-stack">
          <div class="sheet"></div>
          <div class="sheet"></div>
          <div class="sheet"></div>
          <div class="sheet"></div>
          <div class="sheet"><span class="corner-mark"></span></div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="content">
          <div class="header-row">
            <span class="page-label">Ch. 6</span>
            <span class="rule"></span>
          </div>
      
          <h1 class="slide-title">信息规则<span class="accent"> · </span>网络经济</h1>
          <p class="subtitle">Rules of Information in the Network Economy</p>
      
          <ul class="bullet-list">
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">01</span>
              <span class="bullet-text">第6期：信息规则与网络经济</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">02</span>
              <span class="bullet-text">信息想要<span class="hl">免费</span>，但是<span class="hl">免费</span>又是昂贵的</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">03</span>
              <span class="bullet-text">代码数字化：书籍、音乐、电影保持同步</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">04</span>
              <span class="bullet-text">代码塑造了<span class="hl">新的空间</span>，其本身却想要免费</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="bullet-num">05</span>
              <span class="bullet-text"><span class="hl">可恨的先知们</span></span>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      
        <div class="chapter-tag">— Accumulation of Time —</div>
      
        <div class="footer-info">
          <span>LECTURE SERIES<span class="dot"></span>INFORMATION ECONOMY</span>
          <span>44 / 52</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 45
    visual: "3D等距视角下的数据图表方块——量化劳动报酬的商业智能视角"
    bullets:
      - "第7期：基于互联网的协作"
      - "电子邮件与邮件列表/新闻组"
      - "文本/代码"
      - "CVS/SVN/Git的进化"
      - "SourceForge/Launchpad/GitHub 的演化"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        --prussian: #1B3B6B;
        --cream: #F5F0E8;
        --copper: #8B7355;
        --amber: #D4A853;
        --muted: #555555;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        padding: 60px 80px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: flex-end;
        padding-bottom: 20px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header .issue-tag {
        font-size: 13px;
        letter-spacing: 4px;
        color: var(--amber);
        text-transform: uppercase;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .header .page-num {
        font-size: 13px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner h1 {
        font-size: 38px;
        font-weight: 600;
        margin: 30px 0 4px 0;
        letter-spacing: 1px;
        color: var(--cream);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .subtitle {
        font-size: 16px;
        color: var(--copper);
        font-style: italic;
        margin-bottom: 36px;
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .iso-container {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: space-between;
        gap: 50px;
        height: 420px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .iso-grid {
        flex: 0 0 auto;
        position: relative;
        width: 480px;
        height: 420px;
        transform: perspective(900px) rotateX(55deg) rotateZ(-45deg);
        transform-origin: center center;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .iso-cell {
        position: absolute;
        width: 72px;
        height: 72px;
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.12);
        border: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.35);
        transition: all 0.3s;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .iso-cell.filled {
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.35);
        border-color: var(--amber);
        box-shadow: inset 0 0 12px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
      }
      
      .slide-inner .iso-cell.highlight {
        background: var(--amber);
        border-color: var(--amber);
        box-shadow: inset 0 0 15px rgba(255, 220, 140, 0.5), 0 0 20px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4);
      }
      
      /* Isometric blocks on the right */
      .slide-inner .evolution-list {
        flex: 1;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 14px;
        position: relative;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .evo-item {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        gap: 18px;
        padding: 14px 18px;
        background: rgba(245, 240, 232, 0.04);
        border-left: 2px solid var(--copper);
        position: relative;
        transition: all 0.3s;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .evo-item::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        left: -6px;

      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        width:100%;
        height:100vh;
        background:var(--prussian);
        color:var(--cream);
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
        box-sizing:border-box;
        padding:72px 88px;
      }
      
      /* Golden arc rising from bottom */
      .arc-outer{
        position:absolute;
        width:1400px;
        height:1400px;
        left:50%;
        bottom:52%;
        transform:translate(-50%,-50%);
        border-radius:50%;
        border:2px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.18);
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      .arc-mid{
        position:absolute;
        width:1000px;
        height:1000px;
        left:50%;
        bottom:52%;
        transform:translate(-50%,-50%);
        border-radius:50%;
        border:2px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.32);
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      .arc-inner{
        position:absolute;
        width:640px;
        height:640px;
        left:50%;
        bottom:52%;
        transform:translate(-50%,-50%);
        border-radius:50%;
        border:2px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.55);
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      
      /* Earth surface line */
      .surface{
        position:absolute;
        left:0;
        right:0;
        bottom:38%;
        height:1px;
        background:linear-gradient(to right,
          transparent 0%,
          rgba(212,168,83,0.08) 15%,
          rgba(212,168,83,0.35) 50%,
          rgba(212,168,83,0.08) 85%,
          transparent 100%);
      }
      
      /* Light rays above arc */
      .rays{
        position:absolute;
        left:50%;
        bottom:52%;
        width:640px;
        height:320px;
        transform:translate(-50%,0);
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      .rays::before,.rays::after{
        content:'';
        position:absolute;
        bottom:0;
        width:1px;
        height:100%;
        background:linear-gradient(to top,rgba(212,168,83,0.4),transparent);
      }
      .rays::before{left:30%;}
      .rays::after{left:70%;}
      
      .ray-mid{
        position:absolute;
        left:50%;
        bottom:52%;
        width:1px;
        height:360px;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        background:linear-gradient(to top,rgba(212,168,83,0.5),transparent);
      }
      
      /* Header section */
      .header{
        position:relative;
        z-index:2;
        text-align:center;
        margin-top:0;
      }
      .issue-label{
        font-size:14px;
        letter-spacing:6px;
        color:var(--copper);
        text-transform:uppercase;
        margin-bottom:14px;
      }
      .main-title{
        font-size:34px;
        font-weight:600;
        color:var(--amber);
        letter-spacing:2px;
        margin-bottom:8px;
      }
      .subtitle{
        font-size:17px;
        color:var(--cream);
        opacity:0.75;
        font-style:italic;
        letter-spacing:1px;
      }
      
      .divider{
        width:120px;
        height:1px;
        background:var(--amber);
        margin:28px auto 0;
        position:relative;
      }
      .divider::before{
        content:'';
        position:absolute;
        left:50%;
        top:-3px;
        width:7px;
        height:7px;
        background:var(--amber);
        border-radius:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
      }
      
      /* Content list */
      .topic-list{
        position:relative;
        z-index:2;
        list-style:none;
        margin:38px auto 0;
        padding:0;
        max-width:820px;
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:1fr;
        gap:14px;
      }
      .topic-list li{
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        gap:24px;
        padding:14px 28px;
        background:rgba(27,59,107,0.55);
        border-left:3px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.3);
        transition:border-color 0.3s;
      }
      .topic-list li:hover{
        border-left-color:var(--amber);
      }
      .num{
        font-size:15px;
        font-weight:600;
        color:var(--amber);
        font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;
        min-width:36px;
        letter-spacing:1px;
      }
      .topic-text{
        font-size:17px;
        color:var(--cream);
        letter-spacing:0.5px;
        line-height:1.5;
      }
      .topic-text .accent{
        color:var(--amber);
        font-weight:600;
      }
      
      /* Footer */
      .footer{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:32px;
        right:48px;
        font-size:12px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:3px;
        z-index:2;
      }
      .footer-left{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:32px;
        left:48px;
        font-size:12px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:2px;
        z-index:2;
      }
      </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="arc-outer"></div>
      <div class="arc-mid"></div>
      <div class="arc-inner"></div>
      <div class="surface"></div>
      <div class="ray-mid"></div>
      <div class="rays"></div>

      <ul class="topic-list">
        <li>

        <li>

      <div class="footer">46 / 52</div>

    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        --prussian:#1B3B6B;
        --prussian-deep:#142D54;
        --cream:#F5F0E8;
        --copper:#8B7355;
        --amber:#D4A853;
        --muted:#555555;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        width:100%;
        height:100%;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
        box-sizing:border-box;
      }
      
      /* Background grid lines mimicking chart axes */
      .slide-inner::before{
        content:'';
        position:absolute;
        top:0;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;
        background-image:
          linear-gradient(to right, rgba(245,240,232,0.04) 1px, transparent 1px),
          linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(245,240,232,0.04) 1px, transparent 1px);
        background-size: 80px 80px;
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      
      /* Candlestick chart decoration on the right */
      .candle-cluster{
        position:absolute;
        right:60px;
        bottom:60px;
        display:flex;
        align-items:flex-end;
        gap:14px;
        opacity:0.18;
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      .candle{
        position:relative;
        width:18px;
      }
      .candle .wick{
        position:absolute;
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        width:2px;
        background:var(--amber);
      }
      .candle .body{
        position:absolute;
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        width:18px;
        border:1.5px solid var(--amber);
        background:transparent;
      }
      .candle.down .body{
        background:var(--amber);
      }
      
      /* Line chart path using CSS */
      .line-chart{
        position:absolute;
        left:60px;
        right:60px;
        bottom:80px;
        height:120px;
        pointer-events:none;
        opacity:0.12;
      }
      .line-chart::before{
        content:'';
        position:absolute;
        left:0;right:0;
        bottom:0;
        height:2px;
        background:var(--amber);
      }
      .line-path{
        position:absolute;
        left:0;right:0;
        top:0;bottom:0;
        border-left:1px solid var(--amber);
        border-top:1px solid transparent;
      }
      
      /* Top area label */
      .slide-inner .slide-meta{
        position:absolute;
        top:30px;
        left:60px;
        right:60px;
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        font-size:13px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:0.12em;
        text-transform:uppercase;
      }
      .slide-inner .slide-meta .left-mark{
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        gap:12px;
      }
      .slide-inner .slide-meta .mark-line{
        width:40px;
        height:1px;
        background:var(--amber);
      }
      
      /* Main content area */
      .slide-inner .content{
        position:absolute;
        top:110px;
        left:60px;
        right:60px;
        bottom:230px;
        display:flex;
        flex-direction:column;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .eyebrow{
        font-size:14px;
        color:var(--amber);
        letter-spacing:0.3em;
        text-transform:uppercase;
        margin-bottom:16px;
      }
      
      .slide-inner h1{
        font-size:38px;
        font-weight:400;
        color:var(--cream);
        letter-spacing:0.02em;
        margin:0 0 6px 0;
        line-height:1.2;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .subtitle{
        font-size:15px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:0.08em;
        margin-bottom:32px;
        font-style:italic;
      }
      
      .slide-inner .accent-rule{
        width:60px;
        height:2px;
        background:var(--amber);
        margin-bottom:32px;
      }
      
      /* Discussion list */
      .slide-inner .questions{
        list-style:none;
        margin:0;
        padding:0;
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;
        gap:18px 48px;
        max-width:960px;
      }
      .slide-inner .questions li{
        display:flex;
        align-items:flex-start;
        gap:16px;
        padding:10px 0;
        border-left:2px solid transparent;
        padding-left:16px;
        transition:all 0.3s;
      }
      .slide-inner .questions li:hover{
        border-left-color:var(--amber);
      }
      .slide-inner .questions li .num{
        font-size:13px;
        color:var(--amber);
        font-family:Georgia,serif;
        min-width:28px;
        letter-spacing:0.05em;
        padding-top:3px;
      }
      .slide-inner .questions li .text{
        font-size:16px;
        color:var(--cream);
        line-height:1.5;
        letter-spacing:0.01em;
      }
      
      /* Bottom axis area */
      .axis-area{
        position:absolute;
        left:60px;
        right:60px;
        bottom:50px;
        height:60px;
        pointer-events:none;
      }
      .axis-label{
        position:absolute;
        bottom:18px;
        font-size:11px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:0.15em;
        text-transform:uppercase;
        opacity:0.6;
      }
      .axis-label.left{ left:0; }
      .axis-label.right{ right:0; text-align:right; }
      .axis-label.center{
        left:50%;
        transform:translateX(-50%);
        color:var(--amber);
        opacity:0.7;
      }
      
      .bottom-rule{
        position:absolute;
        left:60px;
        right:60px;
        bottom:40px;
        height:1px;
        background:var(--amber);
        opacity:0.4;
      }
      
      /* Page number */
      .page-indicator{
        position:absolute;
        top:30px;
        right:60px;
        font-size:13px;
        color:var(--copper);
        letter-spacing:0.1em;
      }
      .page-indicator .curr{
        color:var(--amber);
        font-size:16px;
      }
      </style>

      .slide-48 .pyramid .tier {

      }
      
      .slide-48 .pyramid .tier.active {

      .slide-48 .pyramid .tier.t1 { width: 100px; }
      .slide-48 .pyramid .tier.t2 { width: 160px; }

      .slide-48 .pyramid .tier .tier-num {

      .slide-48 .pyramid-caption {

      /* Decorative corner */
      .slide-48 .corner-tl,

      .slide-48 .corner-br {

      </style>

  - num: 49
    visual: "紫色亮片闪烁——第11期：文化作为不可替代的驱动力"
    bullets:
      - "第11期：文化的重要作用"
      - "行动与价值观"
      - "无法忽略的作用"
      - "态度不能代替能力，但可以坚持不懈"
    designed: |
      <style>
          .slide-page {
            --prussian: #1B3B6B;
            --cream: #F5F0E8;
            --copper: #8B7355;
            --amber: #D4A853;
            --muted: #555555;
            --violet: #6B4B8B;
      
            width: 100%;
            height: 100vh;
            background: var(--prussian);
            font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
            color: var(--cream);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: center;
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
            padding: 60px 90px;
            box-sizing: border-box;
          }
      
          /* Sparkle layer */
          .sparkle-layer {
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            pointer-events: none;
            z-index: 0;
          }
      
          .sparkle {
            position: absolute;
            width: 4px;
            height: 4px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: var(--amber);
            box-shadow:
              0 0 4px var(--amber),
              0 0 8px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.6);
            animation: twinkle 3s ease-in-out infinite;
          }
      
          .sparkle.violet {
            background: var(--violet);
            box-shadow:
              0 0 4px var(--violet),
              0 0 10px rgba(107, 75, 139, 0.6);
            width: 5px;
            height: 5px;
          }
      
          .sparkle.tiny {
            width: 2px;
            height: 2px;
            box-shadow:
              0 0 3px var(--amber),
              0 0 6px rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4);
          }
      
          .sparkle.s1 { top: 12%; left: 8%; animation-delay: 0s; }
          .sparkle.s2 { top: 18%; left: 72%; animation-delay: 0.5s; }
          .sparkle.s3 { top: 30%; left: 90%; animation-delay: 1s; }
          .sparkle.s4 { top: 45%; left: 5%; animation-delay: 0.3s; }
          .sparkle.s5 { top: 60%; left: 85%; animation-delay: 0.8s; }
          .sparkle.s6 { top: 75%; left: 15%; animation-delay: 1.2s; }
          .sparkle.s7 { top: 82%; left: 60%; animation-delay: 0.6s; }
          .sparkle.s8 { top: 22%; left: 45%; animation-delay: 1.5s; }
          .sparkle.s9 { top: 50%; left: 95%; animation-delay: 0.2s; }
          .sparkle.s10 { top: 8%; left: 35%; animation-delay: 0.9s; }
          .sparkle.s11 { top: 68%; left: 40%; animation-delay: 1.8s; }
          .sparkle.s12 { top: 88%; left: 78%; animation-delay: 1.1s; }
          .sparkle.s13 { top: 35%; left: 30%; animation-delay: 0.4s; }
          .sparkle.s14 { top: 55%; left: 70%; animation-delay: 1.6s; }
          .sparkle.s15 { top: 15%; left: 55%; animation-delay: 0.7s; }
      
          .sparkle.v1 { top: 25%; left: 20%; animation-delay: 1.3s; }
          .sparkle.v2 { top: 70%; left: 50%; animation-delay: 0.1s; }
          .sparkle.v3 { top: 40%; left: 78%; animation-delay: 1.7s; }
          .sparkle.v4 { top: 80%; left: 30%; animation-delay: 0.5s; }
          .sparkle.v5 { top: 10%; left: 82%; animation-delay: 1.4s; }
          .sparkle.v6 { top: 58%; left: 10%; animation-delay: 2s; }
      
          @keyframes twinkle {
            0%, 100% { opacity: 0.2; transform: scale(0.6); }
            25% { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1.4); }
            50% { opacity: 0.5; transform: scale(0.8); }
            75% { opacity: 0.9; transform: scale(1.2); }
          }
      
          /* Decorative cross-shaped sparkles */
          .cross-sparkle {
            position: absolute;
            width: 1px;
            height: 1px;
            pointer-events: none;
          }
      
          .cross-sparkle::before,
          .cross-sparkle::after {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            background: var(--amber);
            opacity: 0.5;
          }
      
          .cross-sparkle::before {
            width: 20px;
            height: 1px;
            top: 0;
            left: -10px;
          }
      
          .cross-sparkle::after {
            width: 1px;
            height: 20px;
            top: -10px;
            left: 0;
          }
      
          .cross-sparkle.c1 { top: 14%; left: 40%; }
          .cross-sparkle.c2 { top: 73%; left: 68%; }
          .cross-sparkle.c3 { top: 38%; left: 88%; }
          .cross-sparkle.c4 { top: 55%; left: 12%; }
      
          /* Content */
          .content {
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
            max-width: 820px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            width: 100%;
          }
      
          .issue-tag {
            display: inline-block;
            font-size: 13px;
            letter-spacing: 4px;
            color: var(--violet);
            font-weight: 600;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            margin-bottom: 28px;
            padding: 8px 18px;
            border: 1px solid var(--violet);
            border-radius: 2px;
          }
      
          .main-title {
            font-size: 44px;
            font-weight: 400;
            line-height: 1.35;
            color: var(--cream);
            margin-bottom: 12px;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
          }
      
          .main-title .accent {
            color: var(--amber);
            font-style: italic;
          }
      
          .divider {
            width: 80px;
            height: 2px;
            background: var(--violet);
            margin: 24px 0 32px 0;
            position: relative;
          }
      
          .divider::after {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: -2px;
            left: 80px;
            width: 140px;
            height: 2px;
            background: transparent;
            border-top: 1px dashed rgba(107, 75, 139, 0.4);
          }
      
          .points {
            list-style: none;
            padding: 0;
            margin: 0;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 18px;
          }
      
          .points li {
            display: flex;
            align-items: flex-start;
            gap: 20px;
            padding: 14px 20px;
            background: rgba(245, 240, 232, 0.03);
            border-left: 2px solid var(--amber);
            transition: background 0.3s ease;
          }
      
          .points li:nth-child(2) {
            border-left-color: var(--violet);
          }
      
          .points li:nth-child(3) {
            border-left-color: var(--amber);
          }
      
          .points li:nth-child(4) {
            border-left-color: var(--violet);
          }
      
          .point-num {
            font-size: 14px;
            color: var(--amber);
            font-weight: 600;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            min-width: 36px;
            padding-top: 4px;
          }
      
          .points li:nth-child(even) .point-num {
            color: var(--violet);
          }
      
          .point-text {
            font-size: 20px;
            line-height: 1.5;
            color: var(--cream);
          }
      
          .point-text .highlight {
            color: var(--amber);
            font-style: italic;
          }
      
          /* Corner ornaments */
          .corner {
            position: absolute;
            width: 40px;
            height: 40px;
            z-index: 1;
          }
      
          .corner.tl { top: 30px; left: 30px; border-top: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4); border-left: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4); }
          .corner.tr { top: 30px; right: 30px; border-top: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4); border-right: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4); }
          .corner.bl { bottom: 30px; left: 30px; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4); border-left: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4); }
          .corner.br { bottom: 30px; right: 30px; border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4); border-right: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.4); }
      
          .page-num {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 30px;
            right: 90px;
            font-size: 12px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            z-index: 2;
          }
      
          .glow-ring {
            position: absolute;
            top: 50%;
            left: 50%;
            transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
            width: 700px;
            height: 700px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            border: 1px solid rgba(107, 75, 139, 0.15);
            z-index: 0;
          }
      
          .glow-ring.r2 {
            width: 500px;
            height: 500px;
            border-color: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.1);
          }
        </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
        
      
        <div class="corner tl"></div>
        <div class="corner tr"></div>
        <div class="corner bl"></div>
        <div class="corner br"></div>
      
        <div class="glow-ring"></div>
        <div class="glow-ring r2"></div>
      
        <div class="sparkle-layer">
          <div class="sparkle s1"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s2"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s3"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s4"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s5"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s6"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s7"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s8"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s9"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s10"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s11"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s12"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s13"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s14"></div>
          <div class="sparkle s15"></div>
          <div class="sparkle violet v1"></div>
          <div class="sparkle violet v2"></div>
          <div class="sparkle violet v3"></div>
          <div class="sparkle violet v4"></div>
          <div class="sparkle violet v5"></div>
          <div class="sparkle violet v6"></div>
          <div class="cross-sparkle c1"></div>
          <div class="cross-sparkle c2"></div>
          <div class="cross-sparkle c3"></div>
          <div class="cross-sparkle c4"></div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="content">
          <span class="issue-tag">第 11 期 &nbsp;·&nbsp; ISSUE 11</span>
          <h1 class="main-title">文化作为<span class="accent">不可替代</span>的<br>驱动力</h1>
          <div class="divider"></div>
      
          <ul class="points">
            <li>
              <span class="point-num">01</span>
              <span class="point-text"><strong>文化的重要作用</strong>——塑造组织的底层逻辑与行为范式</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="point-num">02</span>
              <span class="point-text">行动与<span class="highlight">价值观</span>——文化是价值观在日常行动中的持续投射</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="point-num">03</span>
              <span class="point-text">无法忽略的<span class="highlight">作用</span>——技术可被替代，文化却定义了不可替代性</span>
            </li>
            <li>
              <span class="point-num">04</span>
              <span class="point-text">态度不能代替能力，<span class="highlight">但可以坚持不懈</span>——文化赋予行动以持久的韧性</span>
            </li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      
        <span class="page-num">49 / 52</span>
      </div>
      
  - num: 50
    visual: "金色图钉连线——12讲的统一框架：Williamson L1/L2/L3/L4 四层制度层级回顾"
    bullets:
      - "第5期：交易成本"
      - "罗纳德·科斯与《企业的性质》"
      - "Yochai Benkler：企鹅与利维坦"
      - "无需法律的秩序"
      - "博弈论"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner{
        width:1280px;
        height:720px;
        background:var(--prussian);
        color:var(--cream);
        font-family:'Source Serif 4','Noto Serif SC',Georgia,serif;
        position:relative;
        overflow:hidden;
        padding:60px 80px;
        box-sizing:border-box;
      }
      .slide-inner::before{
        content:"";
        position:absolute;
        top:0;left:0;right:0;
        height:2px;
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, var(--amber) 30%, var(--amber) 70%, transparent);
      }
      .slide-inner::after{
        content:"";
        position:absolute;
        bottom:0;left:0;right:0;
        height:2px;
        background:linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, var(--copper) 30%, var(--copper) 70%, transparent);
      }
      
      .sp-header{
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:flex-end;
        margin-bottom:28px;
        border-bottom:1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.25);
        padding-bottom:16px;
      }
      .sp-eyebrow{
        font-size:13px;
        letter-spacing:4px;
        color:var(--amber);
        text-transform:uppercase;
      }
      .sp-sub{
        font-size:12px;
        letter-spacing:2px;
        color:var(--copper);
      }
      .sp-title{
        font-size:32px;
        font-weight:600;
        color:var(--cream);
        margin:0 0 6px 0;
        letter-spacing:1px;
      }
      .sp-title em{
        color:var(--amber);
        font-style:normal;
      }
      .sp-title-rule{
        width:60px;
        height:2px;
        background:var(--amber);
        margin-top:10px;
      }
      
      .sp-body{
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:200px 1fr;
        gap:50px;
        margin-top:24px;
      }
      
      .sp-framework{
        position:relative;
        padding-left:20px;
      }
      .sp-framework-label{
        font-size:10px;
        letter-spacing:3px;
        color:var(--copper);
        margin-bottom:14px;
        text-transform:uppercase;
      }
      .sp-layer{
        display:flex;
        align-items:center;
        gap:12px;
        margin-bottom:16px;
        padding:8px 0;
        position:relative;
      }
      .sp-layer-num{
        font-size:16px;
        font-weight:700;
        color:var(--amber);
        width:36px;
        text-align:center;
        font-family:'Source Serif 4',serif;

      <div class="slide-inner">
      
      
      <div class="sp-header">
        <div>
          <div class="sp-eyebrow">Module V &nbsp;·&nbsp; Institutional Layers</div>

      <div class="sp-body">

          <div class="sp-layer">

          <div class="sp-layer">

            <span class="sp-layer-text">Behavioral Level<br>行为运作</span>
          </div>
      
          <div class="sp-framework-bottom">↑ Slower change &nbsp;|&nbsp; ↓ Faster change</div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="sp-content">
          <div class="sp-pinboard-label">Five Anchors &nbsp;·&nbsp; 12 Lectures Unified</div>
          <div class="sp-grid">
      
            <div class="sp-pin">
              <div class="sp-pin-main">
                <div class="sp-pin-num">Anchors 01</div>

              </div>
            </div>
      
            <div class="sp-pin">
              <div class="sp-pin-main">
                <div class="sp-pin-num">Anchors 02</div>

                <div class="sp-pin-en">Ronald Coase · The Nature of the Firm</div>
              </div>
            </div>
      
            <div class="sp-pin">
              <div class="sp-pin-main">
                <div class="sp-pin-num">Anchors 03</div>
                <div class="sp-pin-title">Yochai Benkler</div>

              </div>
            </div>
      
            <div class="sp-pin">
              <div class="sp-pin-main">
                <div class="sp-pin-num">Anchors 04</div>

  - num: 51
    visual: "制度层级结构图回顾——第0讲总结"
    bullets:
      - "请记住，有时我们得到的反馈就像被包装丑陋的包裹——但这并不意味着包裹里面装的不是一份礼物。"
      - "——大卫·布拉德福德，卡罗尔·罗宾 《深度关系：从建立信任到彼此成就》"
    designed: |
      <style>
          .slide-inner {
            --prussian: #1B3B6B;
            --cream: #F5F0E8;
            --copper: #8B7355;
            --amber: #D4A853;
            --muted: #555555;
            font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
            background: var(--prussian);
            color: var(--cream);
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: center;
            padding: 80px 120px;
            box-sizing: border-box;
            position: relative;
            overflow: hidden;
          }
      
          .slide-inner::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            top: 0;
            left: 0;
            width: 4px;
            height: 100%;
            background: var(--amber);
          }
      
          .hierarchy-bg {
            position: absolute;
            top: 50%;
            right: -40px;
            transform: translateY(-50%);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 14px;
            opacity: 0.08;
            pointer-events: none;
          }
      
          .hierarchy-bg .bar {
            background: var(--cream);
            height: 6px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            border-radius: 1px;
          }
      
          .hierarchy-bg .bar:nth-child(1) { width: 180px; }
          .hierarchy-bg .bar:nth-child(2) { width: 280px; }
          .hierarchy-bg .bar:nth-child(3) { width: 380px; }
          .hierarchy-bg .bar:nth-child(4) { width: 480px; }
          .hierarchy-bg .bar:nth-child(5) { width: 580px; }
      
          .slide-num {
            position: absolute;
            top: 36px;
            right: 50px;
            font-size: 14px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 2px;
          }
      
          .slide-tag {
            position: absolute;
            top: 36px;
            left: 60px;
            font-size: 13px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 3px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
          }
      
          .quote-container {
            max-width: 900px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            position: relative;
            z-index: 2;
          }
      
          .quote-mark {
            font-size: 160px;
            line-height: 1;
            color: var(--amber);
            opacity: 0.45;
            font-family: Georgia, serif;
            margin-bottom: -30px;
            margin-left: -10px;
            height: 80px;
          }
      
          .quote-text {
            font-size: 30px;
            line-height: 1.75;
            color: var(--cream);
            font-style: italic;
            font-weight: 300;
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
            margin-bottom: 50px;
            text-align: left;
          }
      
          .quote-text .highlight {
            color: var(--amber);
            font-style: italic;
          }
      
          .attribution {
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            align-items: flex-start;
            gap: 10px;
            padding-top: 28px;
            border-top: 1px solid rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.4);
            max-width: 500px;
          }
      
          .attribution::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
          }
      
          .author-name {
            font-size: 18px;
            color: var(--amber);
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            font-weight: 400;
          }
      
          .author-work {
            font-size: 14px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 0.5px;
            font-style: italic;
          }
      
          .bottom-rule {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 40px;
            left: 60px;
            right: 50px;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            font-size: 12px;
            color: var(--copper);
            letter-spacing: 2px;
          }
      
          .bottom-rule .line {
            flex: 1;
            height: 1px;
            background: rgba(139, 115, 85, 0.3);
            margin: 0 24px;
          }
        </style>
      <div class="slide-inner">
        
      
        <div class="hierarchy-bg">
          <div class="bar"></div>
          <div class="bar"></div>
          <div class="bar"></div>
          <div class="bar"></div>
          <div class="bar"></div>
        </div>
      
        <span class="slide-num">51 / 52</span>
        <span class="slide-tag">Epigraph</span>
      
        <div class="quote-container">
          <div class="quote-mark">&ldquo;</div>
          <p class="quote-text">
            请记住，有时我们得到的反馈就像被<span class="highlight">包装丑陋的包裹</span>——但这并不意味着包裹里面装的不是一份<span class="highlight">礼物</span>。
          </p>
          <div class="attribution">
            <span class="author-name">—— 大卫·布拉德福德 &nbsp;·&nbsp; 卡罗尔·罗宾</span>
            <span class="author-work">《深度关系：从建立信任到彼此成就》</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      
        <div class="bottom-rule">
          <span>CLOSING</span>
          <span class="line"></span>
          <span>第 0 讲 · 总结</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      
  - num: 52
    visual: "深普鲁士蓝背景，堆叠的书籍与思想脉络纹理，象征开源历史的层叠积累。"
    bullets:
      - "《创新与激励》，苏珊娜·斯科奇姆，格致出版社，2010.05"
      - "《制度与经济增长》，姚洋，文汇出版社，2022.08"
      - "《开源的成功之路》，Steven Weber，外语教学与研究出版社，2007.06"
      - "《资本主义的法律基础》，约翰·R·康芒斯，商务印书馆，2003.04"
      - "[开源之史]（本博客）/posts/history-of-open-source/summary/"
      - "《The Value of Open Source Software》，Hoffmann / Nagle / Zhou，HBS Working Paper 2024.01"
      - "《Open Source Software and Global Entrepreneurship》，Wright / Nagle / Greenstein，Research Policy 2023"
      - "《Economic Value of Open Source Software》，LF Research（YouTube）"
    designed: |
      <style>
      .slide-inner {
        width: 1280px;
        height: 720px;
        background: var(--prussian);
        color: var(--cream);
        font-family: 'Source Serif 4', 'Noto Serif SC', Georgia, serif;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        padding: 56px 72px;
        box-sizing: border-box;
        position: relative;
        overflow: hidden;
      }
      
      /* Layered book texture in background */
      .slide-inner::before {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        right: -40px;
        top: 80px;
        width: 280px;
        height: 560px;
        background: repeating-linear-gradient(
          to bottom,
          transparent 0px,
          transparent 18px,
          rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.06) 18px,
          rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.06) 19px
        );
        border-left: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.12);
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      .slide-inner::after {
        content: '';
        position: absolute;
        right: 30px;
        top: 90px;
        width: 260px;
        height: 540px;
        border: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.08);
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      
      /* Stacked book spines on the right */
      .book-stack {
        position: absolute;
        right: 50px;
        top: 120px;
        width: 100px;
        display: flex;
        flex-direction: column;
        gap: 3px;
        pointer-events: none;
        opacity: 0.5;
      }
      .book-stack .spine {
        height: 8px;
        background: var(--copper);
        border-radius: 1px;
      }
      .book-stack .spine.a { width: 100%; background: rgba(212,168,83,0.4); }
      .book-stack .spine.b { width: 92%; background: rgba(139,115,85,0.5); }
      .book-stack .spine.c { width: 96%; background: rgba(212,168,83,0.3); }
      .book-stack .spine.d { width: 88%; background: rgba(139,115,85,0.4); }
      .book-stack .spine.e { width: 100%; background: rgba(212,168,83,0.35); }
      
      /* Header */
      .slide-header {
        display: flex;
        align-items: baseline;
        justify-content: space-between;
        border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.3);
        padding-bottom: 16px;
        margin-bottom: 28px;
      }
      .slide-header h2 {
        font-size: 26px;
        font-weight: 600;
        color: var(--cream);
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
        margin: 0;
      }
      .slide-header h2 .accent {
        color: var(--amber);
      }
      .slide-header .page-num {
        font-size: 14px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 1px;
      }
      
      /* Section labels */
      .section-label {
        font-size: 12px;
        color: var(--amber);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
        text-transform: uppercase;
        margin-bottom: 8px;
        margin-top: 14px;
      }
      
      /* Two-column grid for references */
      .refs-grid {
        display: grid;
        grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
        gap: 10px 48px;
        max-width: 960px;
      }
      
      .ref-item {
        display: flex;
        align-items: flex-start;
        gap: 12px;
        padding: 4px 0;
      }
      .ref-num {
        font-size: 11px;
        color: var(--amber);
        font-weight: 600;
        min-width: 22px;
        padding-top: 2px;
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      }
      .ref-text {
        font-size: 13px;
        line-height: 1.55;
        color: var(--cream);
        flex: 1;
      }
      .ref-text .title {
        font-style: italic;
        color: var(--cream);
      }
      .ref-text .meta {
        color: var(--copper);
        font-size: 11.5px;
      }
      .ref-text .url {
        color: rgba(212,168,83,0.7);
        font-size: 11.5px;
        word-break: break-all;
      }
      .ref-text .tag {
        display: inline-block;
        font-size: 10px;
        color: var(--amber);
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,168,83,0.4);
        padding: 1px 6px;
        margin-left: 4px;
        border-radius: 2px;
        letter-spacing: 0.5px;
      }
      
      /* Bottom decorative line */
      .bottom-rule {
        position: absolute;
        left: 72px;
        right: 72px;
        bottom: 36px;
        height: 1px;
        background: rgba(212, 168, 83, 0.2);
      }
      .bottom-tag {
        position: absolute;
        left: 72px;
        bottom: 14px;
        font-size: 10px;
        color: var(--copper);
        letter-spacing: 2px;
      }
      .bottom-fini {
        position: absolute;
        right: 72px;
        bottom: 14px;
        font-size: 10px;
        color: var(--amber);
        letter-spacing: 3px;
      }
      </style>
